                    />
                </div>
            )}

            {/* URL mode — text input */}
            {mode === 'url' && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                        <input
                            type="url"
                            value={urlInput}
                            onChange={(e) => setUrlInput(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="https://example.com/image.jpg"
                            className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                        />
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={applyUrl}
                            disabled={!urlInput.trim()}
                            className="rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-slate-800 disabled:opacity-50 dark:bg-white dark:text-slate-900 dark:hover:bg-slate-100"
                        >
                            Use URL
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Preview */}
            {hasValue && (
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    className="relative mt-3 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                >
                    <div className={`flex ${previewHeight} items-center justify-center p-2`}>
                        {previewError ? (
                            <div className="text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                <AlertCircle className="mx-auto mb-1 h-5 w-5 text-rose-500" />
                                Couldn't load preview
                            </div>
                        ) : (
                            <img
                                src={value}
                                alt="Preview"
                                onError={() => setPreviewError(true)}
                                className="max-h-full max-w-full object-contain"
                            />
                        )}
                    </div>
                    <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={clear}
                        className="absolute right-2 top-2 rounded-md bg-slate-900/80 p-1.5 text-white backdrop-blur-sm transition-colors hover:bg-rose-600"
                        aria-label="Remove image"
                    >
                        <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    </button>
                </motion.div>
            )}

            {hint && (
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{hint}</p>
            )}
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.jsx =====
import { Moon, Sun } from 'lucide-react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import { useThemeStore } from '../../store/themeStore'

export default function ThemeToggle({ className = '' }) {
    const { theme, toggle } = useThemeStore()
    const isDark = theme === 'dark'

    return (
        <button
            onClick={toggle}
            className={`relative rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 ${className}`}
            aria-label={isDark ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode'}
            title={isDark ? 'Light mode' : 'Dark mode'}
        >
            <AnimatePresence mode="wait" initial={false}>
                {isDark ? (
                    <motion.div
                        key="moon"
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, rotate: -90, scale: 0.6 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, rotate: 0, scale: 1 }}
                        exit={{ opacity: 0, rotate: 90, scale: 0.6 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                    >
                        <Moon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </motion.div>
                ) : (
                    <motion.div
                        key="sun"
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, rotate: 90, scale: 0.6 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, rotate: 0, scale: 1 }}
                        exit={{ opacity: 0, rotate: -90, scale: 0.6 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                    >
                        <Sun className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </button>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/components/ui/Toast.jsx =====
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { CheckCircle2, XCircle, Info, AlertTriangle, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { toastSlide } from '../../lib/motion'

const icons = {
    success: CheckCircle2,
    error: XCircle,
    info: Info,
    warning: AlertTriangle,
}

const styles = {
    success: 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-800',
    error: 'border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 text-rose-800',
    info: 'border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 text-sky-800',
    warning: 'border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 text-amber-800',
}

const progressColors = {
    success: 'bg-emerald-500',
    error: 'bg-rose-500',
    info: 'bg-sky-500',
    warning: 'bg-amber-500',
}

export default function ToastContainer() {
    const { toasts, dismiss } = useToastStore()

    return (
        <div className="pointer-events-none fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2">
            <AnimatePresence>
                {toasts.map((t) => {
                    const Icon = icons[t.type] || Info
                    return (
                        <motion.div
                            key={t.id}
                            variants={toastSlide}
                            initial="initial"
                            animate="animate"
                            exit="exit"
                            layout
                            className={`pointer-events-auto relative w-80 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border shadow-lg ${styles[t.type]}`}
                        >
                            <div className="flex items-start gap-3 px-4 py-3">
                                <Icon className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0" />
                                <div className="flex-1 text-sm">{t.message}</div>
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => dismiss(t.id)}
                                    className="text-current opacity-60 transition-opacity hover:opacity-100"
                                    aria-label="Dismiss"
                                >
                                    <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </button>
                            </div>

                            {/* Progress bar */}
                            <motion.div
                                initial={{ scaleX: 1 }}
                                animate={{ scaleX: 0 }}
                                transition={{ duration: (t.duration || 4000) / 1000, ease: 'linear' }}
                                className={`h-0.5 origin-left ${progressColors[t.type]}`}
                            />
                        </motion.div>
                    )
                })}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/hooks/useAdaptivePoll.js =====
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'

/**
 * Smart polling hook:
 *   - Runs `callback` on an interval
 *   - Polls fast (5s) when tab is visible
 *   - Polls slow (30s) when tab is hidden
 *   - Refetches immediately when tab becomes visible again
 *   - Cleans up on unmount
 *
 * Usage:
 *   useAdaptivePoll(fetchTickets, { fastMs: 5000, slowMs: 30000 })
 */
export function useAdaptivePoll(
    callback,
    { fastMs = 5000, slowMs = 30000, enabled = true } = {}
) {
    const callbackRef = useRef(callback)
    const timeoutRef = useRef(null)

    // Keep the latest callback in a ref so we don't restart the timer
    // when the caller passes a new function reference.
    useEffect(() => {
        callbackRef.current = callback
    }, [callback])

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!enabled) return

        let cancelled = false

        const tick = () => {
            if (cancelled) return
            const isVisible = document.visibilityState === 'visible'
            const delay = isVisible ? fastMs : slowMs

            timeoutRef.current = setTimeout(async () => {
                if (cancelled) return
                try {
                    await callbackRef.current()
                } catch {
                    // Silent — the callback should handle its own errors
                }
                tick()
            }, delay)
        }

        const onVisibilityChange = () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
                if (timeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current)
                // Fire immediately when the tab becomes visible again
                Promise.resolve(callbackRef.current()).catch(() => {})
                tick()
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)

        // Start the loop
        tick()

        return () => {
            cancelled = true
            if (timeoutRef.current) clearTimeout(timeoutRef.current)
            document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisibilityChange)
        }
    }, [fastMs, slowMs, enabled])
}
===== ./frontend/src/lib/csv.js =====
/**
 * Convert an array of objects to CSV and trigger a download.
 *
 * @param {Array<Object>} rows - Array of objects
 * @param {Array<{key: string, label: string}>} columns - Column definitions
 * @param {string} filename - Download filename (without extension)
 */
export function exportToCsv(rows, columns, filename = 'export') {
    if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {
        throw new Error('No data to export')
    }

    // Escape a single cell
    const escape = (value) => {
        if (value === null || value === undefined) return ''
        const s = String(value)
        if (s.includes(',') || s.includes('"') || s.includes('\n') || s.includes('\r')) {
            return `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`
        }
        return s
    }

    // Header row
    const header = columns.map((c) => escape(c.label)).join(',')

    // Data rows
    const body = rows
        .map((row) => columns.map((c) => escape(resolvePath(row, c.key))).join(','))
        .join('\n')

    const csv = `\uFEFF${header}\n${body}` // BOM for Excel UTF-8 support

    // Trigger download
    const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' })
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
    const link = document.createElement('a')
    link.href = url
    link.download = `${filename}-${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`
    document.body.appendChild(link)
    link.click()
    link.remove()
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}

/**
 * Resolve a dot-path on an object: "user.name" → obj.user.name
 */
function resolvePath(obj, path) {
    if (!path) return ''
    return path.split('.').reduce((acc, key) => (acc == null ? '' : acc[key]), obj)
}

import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Ticket,
    CheckCircle2,
    Users,
    TrendingUp,
    Clock,
    Star,
    AlertCircle,
    ArrowUpRight,
    ArrowDownRight,
    Building2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import {
    ResponsiveContainer,
    AreaChart,
    Area,
    PieChart,
    Pie,
    Cell,
    XAxis,
    YAxis,
    CartesianGrid,
    Tooltip,
} from 'recharts'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import ExportButton from '../../components/ui/ExportButton'
import { SkeletonStats, SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { groupQueuesByDepartment } from '../../lib/groupQueues'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

function percentChange(current, previous) {
    if (previous === 0) return current > 0 ? 100 : 0
    return Math.round(((current - previous) / previous) * 1000) / 10
}

const ROLE_COLORS = {
    Students: '#4f46e5',
    Staff: '#f59e0b',
    Admins: '#8b5cf6',
}

const STATUS_COLORS = {
    WAITING: '#f59e0b',
    WAITLISTED: '#0ea5e9',
    CALLED: '#4f46e5',
    SERVING: '#6366f1',
    COMPLETED: '#10b981',
    NO_SHOW: '#ef4444',
    CANCELLED: '#94a3b8',
    TRANSFERRED: '#8b5cf6',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    WAITING: 'Waiting',
    WAITLISTED: 'Waitlisted',
    CALLED: 'Called',
    SERVING: 'Serving',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    NO_SHOW: 'No-show',
    CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
    TRANSFERRED: 'Transferred',
}

function KpiCard({ label, value, delta, icon: Icon, accent }) {
    const isPositive = delta >= 0
    return (
        <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5"
        >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">{label}</div>
                    <div className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                        {value}
                    </div>
                    {delta !== null && delta !== undefined && (
                        <div
                            className={`mt-1.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium ${
                                isPositive ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'
                            }`}
                        >
                            {isPositive ? (
                                <ArrowUpRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                            ) : (
                                <ArrowDownRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                            )}
                            {isPositive ? '+' : ''}
                            {delta}%
                            <span className="text-slate-400">vs last week</span>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
                {Icon && (
                    <div className={`rounded-lg p-2 ${accent}`}>
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </motion.div>
    )
}

function ChartTooltip({ active, payload, label }) {
    if (!active || !payload?.length) return null
    return (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3 text-xs shadow-lg">
            {label && (
                <div className="mb-1.5 font-medium text-slate-900">
                    {typeof label === 'string' && label.includes('-')
                        ? new Date(label).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                            weekday: 'short',
                            day: '2-digit',
                            month: 'short',
                        })
                        : label}
                </div>
            )}
            {payload.map((entry) => (
                <div key={entry.name} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
              className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full"
              style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color || entry.payload?.fill }}
          />
                    <span className="text-slate-500">{entry.name}:</span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{entry.value}</span>
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    )
}

export default function Analytics() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/admin/analytics')
            setAnalytics(res.data.data)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load analytics' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-6">
                <SkeletonStats count={4} />
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    if (!analytics) {
        return (
            <EmptyState
                icon={AlertCircle}
                title="Analytics unavailable"
                description="We couldn't load the analytics data. Try again later."
            />
        )
    }

    const ticketsDelta = percentChange(analytics.ticketsThisWeek, analytics.ticketsLastWeek)
    const completedDelta = percentChange(analytics.completedThisWeek, analytics.completedLastWeek)
    const newUsersDelta = percentChange(analytics.newUsersThisWeek, analytics.newUsersLastWeek)

    const trendData = (analytics.dailyTrend || []).map((d) => ({
        date: d.date,
        Joined: d.joined,
        Completed: d.completed,
        'New users': d.newUsers,
    }))

    const roleData = Object.entries(analytics.roleDistribution || {})
        .map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }))
        .filter((d) => d.value > 0)

    const statusData = Object.entries(analytics.statusBreakdown || {})
        .map(([key, value]) => ({
            name: STATUS_LABELS[key] || key,
            value,
            color: STATUS_COLORS[key] || '#94a3b8',
        }))
        .filter((d) => d.value > 0)

    const departmentGroups =
        analytics.queues && analytics.queues.length > 0
            ? groupQueuesByDepartment(
                analytics.queues.map((q) => ({
                    ...q,
                    waitingCount: q.waitingNow,
                }))
            )
            : []

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Analytics"
                description="Operational insights for your university."
                action={
                    <ExportButton
                        rows={analytics.queues || []}
                        columns={[
                            { key: 'queueName', label: 'Queue' },
                            { key: 'department', label: 'Department' },
                            { key: 'ticketsToday', label: 'Tickets Today' },
                            { key: 'servedToday', label: 'Served Today' },
                            { key: 'waitingNow', label: 'Waiting Now' },
                            { key: 'avgWaitMinutes', label: 'Avg Wait (min)' },
                            { key: 'avgServiceMinutes', label: 'Avg Service (min)' },
                            { key: 'noShowRate', label: 'No-show Rate (%)' },
                            { key: 'avgRating', label: 'Avg Rating' },
                        ]}
                        filename="uniqueue-analytics-queues"
                        label="Export queues"
                    />
                }
            />

            {/* ─── KPI ROW ─── */}
            <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
                <KpiCard
                    label="Tickets (7d)"
                    value={analytics.ticketsThisWeek?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={ticketsDelta}
                    icon={Ticket}
                    accent="bg-brand-50 text-brand-600"
                />
                <KpiCard
                    label="Completed (7d)"
                    value={analytics.completedThisWeek?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={completedDelta}
                    icon={CheckCircle2}
                    accent="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600"
                />
                <KpiCard
                    label="New users (7d)"
                    value={analytics.newUsersThisWeek?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={newUsersDelta}
                    icon={Users}
                    accent="bg-sky-50 text-sky-600"
                />
                <KpiCard
                    label="Currently waiting"
                    value={analytics.waitingNow?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={null}
                    icon={Clock}
                    accent="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600"
                />
            </div>

            {/* ─── ACTIVITY CHART ─── */}
            <Card className="mb-6">
                <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Activity trend</h3>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            Tickets joined, completed, and new users — last 14 days
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-brand-600" />
              Joined
            </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
              Completed
            </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-sky-500" />
              New users
            </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="h-72 w-full">
                    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                        <AreaChart
                            data={trendData}
                            margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
                        >
                            <defs>
                                <linearGradient id="aJoined" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                                    <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#4f46e5" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                                    <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#4f46e5" stopOpacity={0} />
                                </linearGradient>
                                <linearGradient id="aCompleted" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                                    <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#10b981" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                                    <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#10b981" stopOpacity={0} />
                                </linearGradient>
                                <linearGradient id="aNewUsers" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                                    <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#0ea5e9" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                                    <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#0ea5e9" stopOpacity={0} />
                                </linearGradient>
                            </defs>
                            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" vertical={false} />
                            <XAxis
                                dataKey="date"
                                tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }}
                                tickLine={false}
                                axisLine={{ stroke: '#e2e8f0' }}
                                tickFormatter={(d) =>
                                    new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                                        day: '2-digit',
                                        month: 'short',
                                    })
                                }
                            />
                            <YAxis
                                tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }}
                                tickLine={false}
                                axisLine={false}
                                allowDecimals={false}
                            />
                            <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />
                            <Area type="monotone" dataKey="Joined" stroke="#4f46e5" strokeWidth={2} fill="url(#aJoined)" />
                            <Area type="monotone" dataKey="Completed" stroke="#10b981" strokeWidth={2} fill="url(#aCompleted)" />
                            <Area type="monotone" dataKey="New users" stroke="#0ea5e9" strokeWidth={2} fill="url(#aNewUsers)" />
                        </AreaChart>
                    </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {/* ─── PIE CHARTS ROW ─── */}
            <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-4">
                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">User distribution</h3>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">By role</p>
                    </div>

                    {roleData.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">No users yet</div>
                    ) : (
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-center gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                            <div className="h-52">
                                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                                    <PieChart>
                                        <Pie
                                            data={roleData}
                                            dataKey="value"
                                            nameKey="name"
                                            cx="50%"
                                            cy="50%"
                                            innerRadius={45}
                                            outerRadius={75}
                                            paddingAngle={2}
                                            strokeWidth={0}
                                        >
                                            {roleData.map((entry) => (
                                                <Cell key={entry.name} fill={ROLE_COLORS[entry.name] || '#94a3b8'} />
                                            ))}
                                        </Pie>
                                        <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />
                                    </PieChart>
                                </ResponsiveContainer>
                            </div>

                            <ul className="space-y-2">
                                {roleData.map((item) => {
                                    const total = roleData.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0)
                                    const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((item.value / total) * 100) : 0
                                    return (
                                        <li key={item.name} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                      <span
                          className="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: ROLE_COLORS[item.name] || '#94a3b8' }}
                      />
                                            <span className="flex-1 truncate text-slate-600">{item.name}</span>
                                            <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{item.value}</span>
                                            <span className="w-10 text-right text-xs text-slate-500">{pct}%</span>
                                        </li>
                                    )
                                })}
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-4">
                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Ticket status</h3>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">Last 14 days</p>
                    </div>

                    {statusData.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">No tickets yet</div>
                    ) : (
                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-center gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                            <div className="h-52">
                                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                                    <PieChart>
                                        <Pie
                                            data={statusData}
                                            dataKey="value"
                                            nameKey="name"
                                            cx="50%"
                                            cy="50%"
                                            innerRadius={45}
                                            outerRadius={75}
                                            paddingAngle={2}
                                            strokeWidth={0}
                                        >
                                            {statusData.map((entry) => (
                                                <Cell key={entry.name} fill={entry.color} />
                                            ))}
                                        </Pie>
                                        <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />
                                    </PieChart>
                                </ResponsiveContainer>
                            </div>

                            <ul className="space-y-2">
                                {statusData.map((item) => {
                                    const total = statusData.reduce((s, d) => s + d.value, 0)
                                    const pct = total > 0 ? Math.round((item.value / total) * 100) : 0
                                    return (
                                        <li key={item.name} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                      <span
                          className="h-2.5 w-2.5 shrink-0 rounded-full"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: item.color }}
                      />
                                            <span className="flex-1 truncate text-slate-600">{item.name}</span>
                                            <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{item.value}</span>
                                            <span className="w-10 text-right text-xs text-slate-500">{pct}%</span>
                                        </li>
                                    )
                                })}
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </Card>
            </div>

            {/* ─── SECONDARY METRICS ─── */}
            <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                Avg wait time
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {analytics.avgWaitMinutes ?? 0}
                                <span className="ml-1 text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">min</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="rounded-lg bg-brand-50 p-2">
                            <Clock className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-600" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                Avg service time
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {analytics.avgServiceMinutes ?? 0}
                                <span className="ml-1 text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">min</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 p-2">
                            <TrendingUp className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                Avg rating
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 flex items-baseline gap-1">
                <span className="text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                  {analytics.avgRating ?? 0}
                </span>
                                <Star className="h-4 w-4 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="rounded-lg bg-amber-50 p-2">
                            <Star className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-600" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            </div>

            {/* ─── DEPARTMENT SUMMARY ─── */}
            {departmentGroups.length > 0 && (
                <Card padded={false} className="mb-6">
                    <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Department summary</h3>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            Rolled up by department — past 14 days.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full">
                            <thead className="bg-slate-50">
                            <tr>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Department
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Queues
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Tickets today
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Served today
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Waiting
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Avg wait
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Avg rating
                                </th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <motion.tbody
                                variants={stagger(0, 0.04)}
                                initial="initial"
                                animate="animate"
                                className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                            >
                                {departmentGroups.map((group) => {
                                    const totalToday = group.queues.reduce(
                                        (sum, q) => sum + (q.ticketsToday || 0),
                                        0
                                    )
                                    const totalServed = group.queues.reduce(
                                        (sum, q) => sum + (q.servedToday || 0),
                                        0
                                    )
                                    const totalWaiting = group.queues.reduce(
                                        (sum, q) => sum + (q.waitingNow || 0),
                                        0
                                    )
                                    const withWait = group.queues.filter((q) => q.avgWaitMinutes > 0)
                                    const avgWait = withWait.length
                                        ? Math.round(
                                        (withWait.reduce((sum, q) => sum + q.avgWaitMinutes, 0) /
                                            withWait.length) *
                                        10
                                    ) / 10
                                        : 0
                                    const withRating = group.queues.filter((q) => q.avgRating > 0)
                                    const avgRating = withRating.length
                                        ? Math.round(
                                        (withRating.reduce((sum, q) => sum + q.avgRating, 0) /
                                            withRating.length) *
                                        10
                                    ) / 10
                                        : 0

                                    return (
                                        <motion.tr
                                            key={group.key}
                                            variants={staggerItem}
                                            className="bg-slate-50/40"
                                        >
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                    <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                                    {group.label}
                                                </div>
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                                {group.queues.length}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                                {totalToday}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                                {totalServed}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                                {totalWaiting}
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                                {avgWait} min
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right">
                                                {avgRating > 0 ? (
                                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                              {avgRating}
                            </span>
                            <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                          </span>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">—</span>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                        </motion.tr>
                                    )
                                })}
                            </motion.tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            )}

            {/* ─── PER-QUEUE TABLE ─── */}
            {analytics.queues && analytics.queues.length > 0 && (
                <Card padded={false} className="mb-6">
                    <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Per-queue breakdown</h3>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            Performance for each queue over the past 14 days.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full">
                            <thead className="bg-slate-50">
                            <tr>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Queue
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Department
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Today
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Served
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Waiting
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Avg wait
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Rating
                                </th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <motion.tbody
                                variants={stagger(0, 0.03)}
                                initial="initial"
                                animate="animate"
                                className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                            >
                                {analytics.queues.map((q) => (
                                    <motion.tr key={q.queueId} variants={staggerItem}>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                            {q.queueName}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                            {q.department || '—'}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {q.ticketsToday}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {q.servedToday}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {q.waitingNow}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {q.avgWaitMinutes} min
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right">
                                            {q.avgRating > 0 ? (
                                                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                            {q.avgRating}
                          </span>
                          <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                        </span>
                                            ) : (
                                                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">—</span>
                                            )}
                                        </td>
                                    </motion.tr>
                                ))}
                            </motion.tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            )}

            {/* ─── STAFF PERFORMANCE ─── */}
            {analytics.staff && analytics.staff.length > 0 && (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                            <div>
                                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Staff performance</h3>
                                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                    Tickets served and feedback received in the last 14 days.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                            <ExportButton
                                rows={analytics.staff}
                                columns={[
                                    { key: 'fullName', label: 'Staff' },
                                    { key: 'ticketsServed', label: 'Tickets Served' },
                                    { key: 'avgServiceMinutes', label: 'Avg Service (min)' },
                                    { key: 'avgRating', label: 'Avg Rating' },
                                ]}
                                filename="uniqueue-analytics-staff"
                                label="Export staff"
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full">
                            <thead className="bg-slate-50">
                            <tr>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Staff
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Served
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Avg service
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Rating
                                </th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <motion.tbody
                                variants={stagger(0, 0.03)}
                                initial="initial"
                                animate="animate"
                                className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                            >
                                {analytics.staff.map((s) => (
                                    <motion.tr key={s.staffId} variants={staggerItem}>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                            {s.fullName}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {s.ticketsServed}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {s.avgServiceMinutes} min
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right">
                                            {s.avgRating > 0 ? (
                                                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                          <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                            {s.avgRating}
                          </span>
                          <Star className="h-3.5 w-3.5 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                        </span>
                                            ) : (
                                                <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">—</span>
                                            )}
                                        </td>
                                    </motion.tr>
                                ))}
                            </motion.tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Appointments.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Calendar, CalendarCheck, Clock, User, Mail, Search, Filter,
    CheckCircle2, XCircle, AlertCircle,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonRow } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    BOOKED: 'info',
    CONFIRMED: 'indigo',
    COMPLETED: 'success',
    CANCELLED: 'danger',
    NO_SHOW: 'warning',
}

const FILTERS = ['ALL', 'BOOKED', 'CONFIRMED', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED', 'NO_SHOW']

function StatTile({ label, value, hint, icon: Icon, accent, valueColor }) {
    return (
        <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        {label}
                    </div>
                    <div className={`mt-2 truncate text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight ${valueColor}`}>
                        {value}
                    </div>
                    {hint && (
                        <div className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{hint}</div>
                    )}
                </div>
                {Icon && (
                    <div className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${accent}`}>
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </Card>
    )
}

export default function Appointments() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([])
    const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [filter, setFilter] = useState('ALL')
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [a, s] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/admin/appointments'),
                api.get('/api/admin/appointments/stats').catch(() => ({ data: { data: null } })),
            ])
            setAppointments(a.data?.data || [])
            setStats(s.data?.data || null)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load appointments' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 5000, slowMs: 15000 })

    const filtered = useMemo(() => {
        let list = appointments
        if (filter !== 'ALL') list = list.filter((a) => a.status === filter)
        if (search.trim()) {
            const s = search.toLowerCase()
            list = list.filter(
                (a) =>
                    a.studentName?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                    a.studentEmail?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                    a.staffName?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                    a.slotTitle?.toLowerCase().includes(s)
            )
        }
        return list
    }, [appointments, filter, search])

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <>
                <PageHeader title="Appointments" description="Every appointment across your university." />
                <Card padded={false}>
                    {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <SkeletonRow key={i} />)}
                </Card>
            </>
        )
    }

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Appointments"
                description="Every appointment students have booked across your university."
            />

            {/* Stat tiles */}
            {stats && (
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-5"
                >
                    <StatTile
                        label="Total"
                        value={stats.total ?? 0}
                        hint="All time"
                        icon={Calendar}
                        accent="bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300"
                        valueColor="text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                    />
                    <StatTile
                        label="Today"
                        value={stats.today ?? 0}
                        hint="Booked for today"
                        icon={CalendarCheck}
                        accent="bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 dark:bg-brand-950/40 dark:text-brand-400"
                        valueColor="text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400"
                    />
                    <StatTile
                        label="Upcoming"
                        value={stats.upcoming ?? 0}
                        hint="Scheduled ahead"
                        icon={Clock}
                        accent="bg-sky-50 text-sky-600 dark:bg-sky-950/40 dark:text-sky-400"
                        valueColor="text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400"
                    />
                    <StatTile
                        label="Completed"
                        value={stats.completed ?? 0}
                        hint="All time"
                        icon={CheckCircle2}
                        accent="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400"
                        valueColor="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"
                    />
                    <StatTile
                        label="Cancelled"
                        value={stats.cancelled ?? 0}
                        hint="All time"
                        icon={XCircle}
                        accent="bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400"
                        valueColor="text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400"
                    />
                </motion.div>
            )}

            {/* Filters */}
            {appointments.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-5 space-y-3">
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                        {FILTERS.map((t) => {
                            const count = t === 'ALL'
                                ? appointments.length
                                : appointments.filter((a) => a.status === t).length
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={t}
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setFilter(t)}
                                    className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                                        filter === t
                                            ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-slate-900'
                                            : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                                    }`}
                                >
                                    {t.charAt(0) + t.slice(1).toLowerCase().replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                                    <span className="ml-1.5 opacity-60">{count}</span>
                                </button>
                            )
                        })}
                    </div>

                    <div className="relative">
                        <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={search}
                            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Search by student, staff, or slot"
                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-2.5 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {appointments.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Calendar}
                    title="No appointments yet"
                    description="When students book slots with your staff, they'll appear here."
                />
            ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Filter}
                    title="No appointments match"
                    description="Try a different filter or clear the search."
                    action={
                        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => { setFilter('ALL'); setSearch('') }}>
                            Clear filters
                        </Button>
                    }
                />
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    variants={stagger(0, 0.03)}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    className="space-y-3"
                >
                    {filtered.map((a) => (
                        <motion.div key={a.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                            <Card>
                                <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                                    <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
                                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-sm font-bold text-white">
                                            {a.studentName?.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase() || '?'}
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="min-w-0">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                <h3 className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                    {a.studentName || 'Unknown student'}
                                                </h3>
                                                <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[a.status] || 'default'}>
                                                    {a.status}
                                                </Badge>
                                            </div>
                                            {a.studentEmail && (
                                                <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    <Mail className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                                                    {a.studentEmail}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                            <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                {a.slotTitle && (
                                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Calendar className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                        {a.slotTitle}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                                {a.startTime && (
                                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                        {new Date(a.startTime).toLocaleString()}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                                {a.staffName && (
                                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <User className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                        {a.staffName}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </Card>
                        </motion.div>
                    ))}
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/AdminDashboard.jsx =====
import { Routes, Route, Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
    LayoutGrid,
    BarChart3,
    Star,
    ListOrdered,
    Users,
    Route as RouteIcon,
    Settings as SettingsIcon,
    Building2,
    CalendarCheck,
} from 'lucide-react'
import DashboardLayout from '../components/layout/DashboardLayout'
import AdminHeader from '../components/admin/AdminHeader'
import AdminHome from './admin/AdminHome'
import Overview from './admin/Overview'
import Analytics from './admin/Analytics'
import Feedback from './admin/Feedback'
import Queues from './admin/Queues'
import Offices from './admin/Offices'
import Staff from './admin/Staff'
import Appointments from './admin/Appointments'
import Workflows from './admin/Workflows'
import Settings from './admin/Settings'

const tabs = [
    { to: '/admin/overview', label: 'Overview', icon: LayoutGrid },
    { to: '/admin/analytics', label: 'Analytics', icon: BarChart3 },
    { to: '/admin/appointments', label: 'Appointments', icon: CalendarCheck },
    { to: '/admin/feedback', label: 'Feedback', icon: Star },
    { to: '/admin/queues', label: 'Services', icon: ListOrdered },
    { to: '/admin/offices', label: 'Offices', icon: Building2 },
    { to: '/admin/staff', label: 'Staff', icon: Users },
    { to: '/admin/workflows', label: 'Workflows', icon: RouteIcon },
    { to: '/admin/settings', label: 'Settings', icon: SettingsIcon },
]

const ROUTE_SUBTITLES = {
    '/admin': 'Everything you need to run your university today.',
    '/admin/overview': "A snapshot of what's happening across your university.",
    '/admin/analytics': 'Track wait times, service quality, and staff performance.',
    '/admin/appointments': 'Review and manage every appointment across your university.',
    '/admin/feedback': 'See what students are saying about your service.',
    '/admin/queues': 'Create and manage the services students can join.',
    '/admin/offices': 'Manage the offices that host your services.',
    '/admin/staff': 'Add staff, assign them to services, and manage accounts.',
    '/admin/workflows': 'Build multi-step clearance processes.',
    '/admin/settings': 'Configure your university branding, features, and limits.',
}

export default function AdminDashboard() {
    const location = useLocation()
    const subtitle = ROUTE_SUBTITLES[location.pathname] || 'Manage your university.'

    return (
        <DashboardLayout tabs={tabs}>
            <AdminHeader subtitle={subtitle} />
            <Routes>
                <Route index element={<AdminHome />} />
                <Route path="overview" element={<Overview />} />
                <Route path="analytics" element={<Analytics />} />
                <Route path="appointments" element={<Appointments />} />
                <Route path="feedback" element={<Feedback />} />
                <Route path="queues" element={<Queues />} />
                <Route path="offices" element={<Offices />} />
                <Route path="staff" element={<Staff />} />
                <Route path="workflows" element={<Workflows />} />
                <Route path="settings" element={<Settings />} />
                <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/admin" replace />} />
            </Routes>
        </DashboardLayout>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Feedback.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { Star, MessageSquare, Search, Filter } from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import ExportButton from '../../components/ui/ExportButton'
import { SkeletonRow } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { formatDateTime } from '../../lib/format'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

const RATING_FILTERS = [
    { value: '', label: 'All ratings' },
    { value: '5', label: '5 stars' },
    { value: '4', label: '4 stars' },
    { value: '3', label: '3 stars' },
    { value: '2', label: '2 stars' },
    { value: '1', label: '1 star' },
]

export default function Feedback() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
    const [ratingFilter, setRatingFilter] = useState('')

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/admin/feedback')
            setFeedback(res.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load feedback' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const filtered = feedback.filter((f) => {
        if (ratingFilter && String(f.rating) !== ratingFilter) return false
        if (!search.trim()) return true
        const q = search.toLowerCase()
        return (
            f.ticketNumber?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            f.userFullName?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            f.comment?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        )
    })

    // Compute average
    const avgRating =
        feedback.length > 0
            ? (feedback.reduce((sum, f) => sum + (f.rating || 0), 0) / feedback.length).toFixed(1)
            : '—'

    // Rating distribution
    const distribution = [5, 4, 3, 2, 1].map((r) => ({
        rating: r,
        count: feedback.filter((f) => f.rating === r).length,
    }))
    const maxCount = Math.max(1, ...distribution.map((d) => d.count))

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Student Feedback"
                description="What students say about your service."
                action={
                    <ExportButton
                        rows={feedback}
                        columns={[
                            { key: 'ticketNumber', label: 'Ticket' },
                            { key: 'userFullName', label: 'Student' },
                            { key: 'rating', label: 'Rating' },
                            { key: 'comment', label: 'Comment' },
                            { key: 'createdAt', label: 'Submitted' },
                        ]}
                        filename="uniqueue-feedback"
                        label="Export feedback"
                    />
                }
            />

            {/* Summary cards */}
            {feedback.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    {/* Average rating */}
                    <Card>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                            <div className="flex h-16 w-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-amber-50">
                                <span className="text-2xl font-semibold text-amber-700">{avgRating}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Average rating
                                </div>
                                <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                                    {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
                                        <Star
                                            key={n}
                                            className={`h-4 w-4 ${
                                                n <= Math.round(parseFloat(avgRating))
                                                    ? 'fill-amber-400 text-amber-400'
                                                    : 'text-slate-300'
                                            }`}
                                        />
                                    ))}
                                    <span className="ml-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    from {feedback.length} {feedback.length === 1 ? 'review' : 'reviews'}
                  </span>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>

                    {/* Distribution */}
                    <Card>
                        <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 mb-3">
                            Rating distribution
                        </div>
                        <div className="space-y-1.5">
                            {distribution.map((d) => (
                                <div key={d.rating} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                                    <span className="w-3 text-slate-600">{d.rating}</span>
                                    <Star className="h-3 w-3 fill-amber-400 text-amber-400" />
                                    <div className="h-1.5 flex-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                                        <motion.div
                                            initial={{ width: 0 }}
                                            animate={{ width: `${(d.count / maxCount) * 100}%` }}
                                            transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
                                            className="h-full rounded-full bg-amber-400"
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                    <span className="w-6 text-right text-slate-600">{d.count}</span>
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Filters */}
            {feedback.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4 space-y-3">
                    <Input
                        placeholder="Search by ticket, student, or comment…"
                        value={search}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                        icon={Search}
                    />

                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                        {RATING_FILTERS.map((f) => (
                            <button
                                key={f.value}
                                onClick={() => setRatingFilter(f.value)}
                                className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                                    ratingFilter === f.value
                                        ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white'
                                        : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
                                }`}
                            >
                                {f.label}
                            </button>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* List */}
            {loading ? (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => (
                        <SkeletonRow key={i} />
                    ))}
                </Card>
            ) : feedback.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={MessageSquare}
                    title="No feedback yet"
                    description="Students haven't rated any services yet. Feedback will appear here once they do."
                />
            ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Filter}
                    title="No feedback matches your filters"
                    description="Try adjusting your search or rating filter."
                />
            ) : (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <motion.ul
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.03)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                    >
                        {filtered.map((f) => (
                            <motion.li
                                key={f.id}
                                variants={staggerItem}
                                className="flex flex-col gap-3 px-4 py-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:gap-4 sm:px-6"
                            >
                                <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                                    {f.userFullName?.split(' ').map((p) => p[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                                </div>

                                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      {f.userFullName}
                    </span>
                                        <Badge variant="default">
                                            Ticket {f.ticketNumber}
                                        </Badge>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                                        {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
                                            <Star
                                                key={n}
                                                className={`h-4 w-4 ${
                                                    n <= f.rating
                                                        ? 'fill-amber-400 text-amber-400'
                                                        : 'text-slate-300'
                                                }`}
                                            />
                                        ))}
                                        <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      {formatDateTime(f.createdAt)}
                    </span>
                                    </div>

                                    {f.comment && (
                                        <p className="mt-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
                                            {f.comment}
                                        </p>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </motion.li>
                        ))}
                    </motion.ul>
                </Card>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Offices.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Building2, MapPin, Users, ListOrdered, Search, X, Ticket, Pencil,
    Plus, ArrowRight, Mail, FileText,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
