import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonQueueGrid } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import OfficeFormModal from '../../components/admin/OfficeFormModal'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

function OfficeCard({ office, onEdit, onOpen }) {
    return (
        <motion.div variants={staggerItem}>
            <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                    <button
                        onClick={onOpen}
                        className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3 text-left"
                    >
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-950/40">
                            <Building2 className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                            <h3 className="truncate text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                {office.name}
                            </h3>
                            {office.location && (
                                <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    <MapPin className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                                    {office.location}
                                </p>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </button>
                    <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
                        {!office.isActive && (
                            <Badge variant="default">Inactive</Badge>
                        )}
                        <button
                            onClick={onEdit}
                            className="rounded-md p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                            aria-label="Edit office"
                        >
                            <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {office.description && (
                    <p className="mt-1 line-clamp-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        {office.description}
                    </p>
                )}

                <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                    <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                            <ListOrdered className="h-3 w-3" /> Services
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-1 text-base font-bold text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400">
                            {office.serviceCount ?? 0}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                            <Users className="h-3 w-3" /> Staff
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-1 text-base font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400">
                            {office.staffCount ?? 0}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                            <Ticket className="h-3 w-3" /> Waiting
                        </div>
                        <div className="mt-1 text-base font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                            {office.waitingCount ?? 0}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <button
                    onClick={onOpen}
                    className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1 self-start text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                >
                    View services
                    <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </button>
            </Card>
        </motion.div>
    )
}

function OfficeDetail({ office, queues, onClose }) {
    const services = queues.filter((q) => q.officeId === office.id)

    return (
        <>
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                onClick={onClose}
                className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm"
            />
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center overflow-y-auto p-4">
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 12 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 12 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    className="relative my-8 w-full max-w-3xl rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                >
                    <button
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                        aria-label="Close"
                    >
                        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>

                    <div className="flex items-start gap-3 pr-8">
                        <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-950/40">
                            <Building2 className="h-6 w-6 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <h2 className="truncate text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                {office.name}
                            </h2>
                            {office.location && (
                                <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 truncate text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                                    {office.location}
                                </p>
                            )}
                            {office.description && (
                                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    {office.description}
                                </p>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        <Link to="/admin/queues">
                            <Button variant="secondary" icon={Pencil} className="shrink-0">
                                Manage services
                            </Button>
                        </Link>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-6">
                        <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            Services in this office
                        </h3>

                        {services.length === 0 ? (
                            <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-6 text-center dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    No services yet.
                                </p>
                                <Link
                                    to="/admin/queues"
                                    className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 hover:underline"
                                >
                                    <Plus className="h-3 w-3" />
                                    Create a service
                                </Link>
                            </div>
                        ) : (
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                                {services.map((q) => (
                                    <div
                                        key={q.id}
                                        className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-4 dark:border-slate-700"
                                    >
                                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                            <div className="min-w-0">
                                                <h4 className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                    {q.name}
                                                </h4>
                                                {q.description && (
                                                    <p className="mt-0.5 line-clamp-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                        {q.description}
                                                    </p>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                            <Badge variant={q.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                                                {q.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                                            </Badge>
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs">
                      <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400">
                        <Users className="h-3 w-3" />
                          {q.waitingCount ?? 0} waiting
                      </span>
                                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                        <Ticket className="h-3 w-3" />
                        ~{q.estimatedMinutes ?? 0} min
                      </span>
                                            {q.staffCount > 0 && (
                                                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          <Users className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                    {q.staffCount} staff
                        </span>
                                            )}
                                        </div>

                                        {q.staff && q.staff.length > 0 && (
                                            <div className="mt-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 dark:border-slate-800">
                                                <div className="mb-2 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-400">
                                                    Assigned staff
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                                                    {q.staff.map((s) => (
                                                        <span
                                                            key={s.id}
                                                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300"
                                                        >
                              <span className="flex h-4 w-4 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-[8px] font-bold text-white">
                                {s.fullName?.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase()}
                              </span>
                                                            {s.fullName}
                            </span>
                                                    ))}
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                ))}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
                        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
                            Close
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </motion.div>
            </div>
        </>
    )
}

export default function Offices() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [offices, setOffices] = useState([])
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
    const [showModal, setShowModal] = useState(false)
    const [editTarget, setEditTarget] = useState(null)
    const [openOffice, setOpenOffice] = useState(null)

    const load = async () => {
        try {
            const [o, q] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/admin/offices'),
                api.get('/api/admin/queues'),
            ])
            setOffices(o.data?.data || [])
            setQueues(q.data?.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load offices' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [])

    const filtered = useMemo(() => {
        if (!search.trim()) return offices
        const s = search.toLowerCase()
        return offices.filter(
            (o) =>
                o.name.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                o.location?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                o.description?.toLowerCase().includes(s)
        )
    }, [offices, search])

    const openCreate = () => {
        setEditTarget(null)
        setShowModal(true)
    }

    const openEdit = (office) => {
        setEditTarget(office)
        setShowModal(true)
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <>
                <PageHeader title="Offices" description="Manage your university's offices." />
                <SkeletonQueueGrid count={6} />
            </>
        )
    }

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Offices"
                description="Manage the offices that host your services."
                action={
                    <Button icon={Plus} onClick={openCreate}>
                        New office
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            {offices.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-5">
                    <div className="relative">
                        <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={search}
                            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Search offices"
                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-2.5 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {offices.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Building2}
                    title="No offices yet"
                    description="Create your first office — Bursary, Registry, Library — then add services under it."
                    action={<Button icon={Plus} onClick={openCreate}>Create office</Button>}
                />
            ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Search}
                    title="No matches"
                    description="Try a different search term."
                    action={<Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSearch('')}>Clear search</Button>}
                />
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3"
                >
                    {filtered.map((o) => (
                        <OfficeCard
                            key={o.id}
                            office={o}
                            onEdit={() => openEdit(o)}
                            onOpen={() => setOpenOffice(o)}
                        />
                    ))}
                </motion.div>
            )}

            <AnimatePresence>
                {showModal && (
                    <OfficeFormModal
                        office={editTarget}
                        onClose={() => setShowModal(false)}
                        onSaved={load}
                        onDeleted={load}
                    />
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            <AnimatePresence>
                {openOffice && (
                    <OfficeDetail
                        office={openOffice}
                        queues={queues}
                        onClose={() => setOpenOffice(null)}
                    />
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Overview.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    LayoutGrid,
    Users,
    Ticket,
    Clock,
    Plus,
    MapPin,
    UserCheck,
    Building2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import StatCard from '../../components/ui/StatCard'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonStats, SkeletonQueueGrid } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useAuthStore } from '../../store/authStore'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { groupQueuesByOffice } from '../../lib/groupQueues'
import { stagger, staggerItem, expand } from '../../lib/motion'

export default function Overview() {
    const { user } = useAuthStore()
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [staff, setStaff] = useState([])
    const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false)
    const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        name: '',
        department: '',
        location: '',
        maxCapacity: 50,
        averageServiceMinutes: 5,
        allowWaitlist: true,
    })

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [q, a, s] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/admin/queues'),
                api.get('/api/admin/analytics').catch(() => ({ data: { data: null } })),
                api.get('/api/admin/staff').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
            ])
            setQueues(q.data.data || [])
            setAnalytics(a.data.data || null)
            setStaff(s.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const createQueue = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setCreating(true)
        try {
            await api.post('/api/admin/queues', {
                ...form,
                department: form.department?.trim() || null,
                maxCapacity: Number(form.maxCapacity),
                averageServiceMinutes: Number(form.averageServiceMinutes),
                opensAt: '00:00:00',
                closesAt: '23:59:59',
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Queue created' })
            setForm({
                name: '',
                department: '',
                location: '',
                maxCapacity: 50,
                averageServiceMinutes: 5,
                allowWaitlist: true,
            })
            setShowCreate(false)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to create queue' })
        } finally {
            setCreating(false)
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <>
                <div className="mb-6 h-10 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-slate-200" />
                <SkeletonStats count={4} />
                <div className="mb-8 mt-8 h-6 w-1/4 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-200" />
                <SkeletonQueueGrid count={3} />
            </>
        )
    }

    const groups = groupQueuesByOffice(queues)

    const QueueCard = ({ q }) => (
        <motion.div variants={staggerItem}>
            <Card className="h-full">
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{q.name}</h3>
                    <Badge variant={q.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                        {q.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                    </Badge>
                </div>
                {q.location && (
                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-500">
                        <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        {q.location}
                    </p>
                )}
                <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-xs">
                    <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Capacity</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                            {q.maxCapacity}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Avg. min</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                            {q.averageServiceMinutes}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Staff</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                            {q.staffCount ?? 0}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </Card>
        </motion.div>
    )

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Administration"
                description={`Managing operations for ${user?.fullName}'s university`}
                action={
                    <Button
                        icon={Plus}
                        onClick={() => setShowCreate((v) => !v)}
                        variant={showCreate ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                    >
                        {showCreate ? 'Cancel' : 'New queue'}
                    </Button>
                }
            />


            <AnimatePresence>
                {showCreate && (
                    <motion.div
                        variants={expand}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        exit="exit"
                        className="mb-8 overflow-hidden"
                    >
                        <Card>
                            <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                Create new queue
                            </h3>
                            <form onSubmit={createQueue} className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                <Input
                                    label="Queue name"
                                    value={form.name}
                                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
                                    placeholder="e.g. Fee Payments"
                                    required
                                />
                                <Input
                                    label="Location"
                                    value={form.location}
                                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, location: e.target.value })}
                                    placeholder="e.g. Block A, Room 12"
                                />
                                <Input
                                    label="Department"
                                    value={form.department}
                                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, department: e.target.value })}
                                    placeholder="e.g. Bursary"
                                    icon={Building2}
                                />
                                <Input
                                    label="Capacity per day"
                                    type="number"
                                    value={form.maxCapacity}
                                    onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, maxCapacity: e.target.value })}
                                />
                                <Input
                                    label="Average service time (min)"
                                    type="number"
                                    value={form.averageServiceMinutes}
                                    onChange={(e) =>
                                        setForm({ ...form, averageServiceMinutes: e.target.value })
                                    }
                                />
                                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                                    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
                                        <input
                                            type="checkbox"
                                            checked={form.allowWaitlist}
                                            onChange={(e) =>
                                                setForm({ ...form, allowWaitlist: e.target.checked })
                                            }
                                            className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500"
                                        />
                                        Allow students to join a waitlist when the queue is full
                                    </label>
                                </div>
                                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                                    <Button type="submit" loading={creating} disabled={creating}>
                                        Create queue
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </Card>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            <div className="mb-4">
                <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Queues</h2>
            </div>

            {queues.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={LayoutGrid}
                    title="No queues yet"
                    description="Create your first queue to start serving students."
                    action={
                        <Button icon={Plus} onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
                            Create queue
                        </Button>
                    }
                />
            ) : (
                <div className="space-y-8">
                    {groups.map((group, groupIdx) => (
                        <motion.section
                            key={group.key}
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.4, delay: groupIdx * 0.05 }}
                        >
                            {/* Department header */}
                            <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-2">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                    <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700">
                                        {group.label}
                                    </h4>
                                    <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-600">
                    {group.queues.length}{' '}
                                        {group.queues.length === 1 ? 'queue' : 'queues'}
                  </span>
                                </div>
                                {group.waitingTotal > 0 && (
                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    <Users className="h-3 w-3" />
                                        {group.waitingTotal} waiting
                  </span>
                                )}
                            </div>

                            <motion.div
                                variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                                initial="initial"
                                animate="animate"
                                className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3"
                            >
                                {group.queues.map((q) => (
                                    <QueueCard key={q.id} q={q} />
                                ))}
                            </motion.div>
                        </motion.section>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Staff list */}
            <div className="mt-10">
                <h2 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Staff members</h2>
                {staff.length === 0 ? (
                    <EmptyState
                        icon={UserCheck}
                        title="No staff members"
                        description="Add staff members to serve tickets in your queues."
                    />
                ) : (
                    <Card padded={false}>
                        <motion.ul
                            variants={stagger(0, 0.04)}
                            initial="initial"
                            animate="animate"
                            className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                        >
                            {staff.map((s) => (
                                <motion.li
                                    key={s.id}
                                    variants={staggerItem}
                                    className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-4"
                                >
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                                            {s.fullName?.split(' ').map((p) => p[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                                        </div>
                                        <div>
                                            <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                                {s.fullName}
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{s.email}</div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </motion.li>
                            ))}
                        </motion.ul>
                    </Card>
                )}
            </div>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Queues.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Plus, Pencil, Trash2, MapPin, Clock, LayoutGrid, Search,
    Building2, GraduationCap,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonQueueGrid } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem, expand } from '../../lib/motion'

const emptyForm = {
    name: '', description: '', officeId: '', location: '',
    opensAt: '08:00', closesAt: '17:00',
    maxCapacity: 50, averageServiceMinutes: 5,
    allowWaitlist: true, autoPromoteWaitlist: true, maxWaitlistSize: 20,
    matricRequired: false,
}

export default function Queues() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [offices, setOffices] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
    const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null)
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
    const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
    const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState(null)
    const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false)
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [q, o] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/admin/queues'),
                api.get('/api/admin/offices'),
            ])
            setQueues(q.data.data || [])
            setOffices(o.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load data' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])

    const setField = (f) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [f]: e.target.value })

    const openCreate = (prefillOfficeId = '') => {
        setForm({ ...emptyForm, officeId: prefillOfficeId })
        setEditingId(null)
        setShowForm(true)
        setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('queue-form')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }), 50)
    }

    const openEdit = (queue) => {
        setForm({
            name: queue.name || '',
            description: queue.description || '',
            officeId: queue.officeId || '',
            location: queue.location || '',
            opensAt: (queue.opensAt || '08:00').slice(0, 5),
            closesAt: (queue.closesAt || '17:00').slice(0, 5),
            maxCapacity: queue.maxCapacity ?? 50,
            averageServiceMinutes: queue.averageServiceMinutes ?? 5,
            allowWaitlist: queue.allowWaitlist ?? true,
            autoPromoteWaitlist: queue.autoPromoteWaitlist ?? true,
            maxWaitlistSize: queue.maxWaitlistSize ?? 20,
            matricRequired: queue.matricRequired ?? false,
        })
        setEditingId(queue.id)
        setShowForm(true)
        setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('queue-form')?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' }), 50)
    }

    const closeForm = () => {
        setShowForm(false)
        setEditingId(null)
        setForm(emptyForm)
    }

    const save = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!form.officeId) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Please select an office' })
            return
        }
        setSaving(true)
        try {
            const payload = {
                name: form.name,
                description: form.description || null,
                officeId: Number(form.officeId),
                location: form.location || null,
                opensAt: `${form.opensAt}:00`,
                closesAt: `${form.closesAt}:00`,
                maxCapacity: Number(form.maxCapacity),
                averageServiceMinutes: Number(form.averageServiceMinutes),
                allowWaitlist: form.allowWaitlist,
                autoPromoteWaitlist: form.autoPromoteWaitlist,
                maxWaitlistSize: Number(form.maxWaitlistSize),
                matricRequired: !!form.matricRequired,
            }
            if (editingId) {
                await api.put(`/api/admin/queues/${editingId}`, payload)
                push({ type: 'success', message: 'Service updated' })
            } else {
                await api.post('/api/admin/queues', payload)
                push({ type: 'success', message: 'Service created' })
            }
            closeForm()
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to save service' })
        } finally {
            setSaving(false)
        }
    }

    const deleteQueue = async () => {
        if (!deleteTarget) return
        setDeleting(true)
        try {
            await api.delete(`/api/admin/queues/${deleteTarget.id}`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Service deleted' })
            setDeleteTarget(null)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to delete' })
        } finally {
            setDeleting(false)
        }
    }

    // Group queues by office — never produces an "unassigned" bucket
    const groups = (() => {
        const map = new Map()
        for (const q of queues) {
            const label = (q.officeName || 'General').trim() || 'General'
            const key = label.toLowerCase()
            if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, { key, label, queues: [] })
            map.get(key).queues.push(q)
        }
        return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => a.label.localeCompare(b.label))
    })()

    const filteredQueues = search.trim()
        ? queues.filter((q) => {
            const s = search.toLowerCase()
            return (
                q.name?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                q.officeName?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                q.location?.toLowerCase().includes(s)
            )
        })
        : queues

    const filteredGroups = search.trim()
        ? groups
            .map((g) => ({
                ...g,
                queues: g.queues.filter((q) => filteredQueues.includes(q)),
            }))
            .filter((g) => g.queues.length > 0)
        : groups

    if (loading) {
        return <div className="space-y-4"><SkeletonQueueGrid count={6} /></div>
    }

    const QueueCard = ({ q }) => (
        <motion.div variants={staggerItem}>
            <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{q.name}</h3>
                        {q.matricRequired && (
                            <span className="mt-1 inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-amber-700 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400">
                <GraduationCap className="h-3 w-3" />
                Matric required
              </span>
                        )}
                    </div>
                    <Badge variant={q.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                        {q.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                    </Badge>
                </div>

                {q.location && (
                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm text-slate-500">
                        <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        {q.location}
                    </p>
                )}

                {q.description && (
                    <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-slate-600">{q.description}</p>
                )}

                <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-xs">
                    <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Capacity</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-bold text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400">
                            {q.maxCapacity}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Avg. min</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                            {q.averageServiceMinutes}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                        <div className="text-slate-500">Staff</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400">
                            {q.staffCount ?? 0}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                {q.opensAt && q.closesAt && (
                    <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                        <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        Open {String(q.opensAt).slice(0, 5)} – {String(q.closesAt).slice(0, 5)}
                    </div>
                )}

                <div className="mt-auto flex items-center gap-2 pt-5">
                    <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" icon={Pencil} onClick={() => openEdit(q)} fullWidth>
                        Edit
                    </Button>
                    <button
                        onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(q)}
                        className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600"
                        aria-label="Delete service"
                    >
                        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                </div>
            </Card>
        </motion.div>
    )

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Services"
                description="Create and manage the services students can join."
                action={
                    <Button
                        icon={Plus}
                        onClick={() => (showForm ? closeForm() : openCreate())}
                        variant={showForm ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                    >
                        {showForm ? 'Cancel' : 'New service'}
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <AnimatePresence>
                {showForm && (
                    <motion.div id="queue-form" variants={expand} initial="initial" animate="animate" exit="exit" className="mb-8 overflow-hidden">
                        <Card>
                            <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {editingId ? 'Edit service' : 'Create service'}
                            </h3>
                            <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-4">
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                    <Input
                                        label="Service name"
                                        value={form.name}
                                        onChange={setField('name')}
                                        placeholder="Enter service name"
                                        required
                                    />
                                    <div>
                                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                            Office <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                                        </label>
                                        <select
                                            value={form.officeId}
                                            onChange={setField('officeId')}
                                            required
                                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                        >
                                            <option value="">Select office</option>
                                            {offices.map((o) => (
                                                <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                                                    {o.name}
                                                </option>
                                            ))}
                                        </select>
                                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            {offices.length === 0
                                                ? 'No offices yet — create one first.'
                                                : 'Students see services grouped by office.'}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                    <Input
                                        label="Location"
                                        value={form.location}
                                        onChange={setField('location')}
                                        placeholder="Enter location"
                                        icon={MapPin}
                                    />
                                    <Input
                                        label="Description"
                                        value={form.description}
                                        onChange={setField('description')}
                                        placeholder="What is this service for?"
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                                    <Input label="Opens at" type="time" value={form.opensAt} onChange={setField('opensAt')} />
                                    <Input label="Closes at" type="time" value={form.closesAt} onChange={setField('closesAt')} />
                                </div>

                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                                    <Input label="Max capacity" type="number" min="1" value={form.maxCapacity} onChange={setField('maxCapacity')} hint="Maximum waiting tickets" />
                                    <Input label="Avg. service minutes" type="number" min="1" value={form.averageServiceMinutes} onChange={setField('averageServiceMinutes')} hint="Used for wait estimates" />
                                </div>

                                <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        checked={form.matricRequired}
                                        onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, matricRequired: e.target.checked })}
                                        className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500"
                                    />
                                    <div>
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                            <GraduationCap className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                            Matric number required
                                        </div>
                                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            Freshers must upgrade to full student access before joining.
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                </label>

                                <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                                    <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={closeForm} disabled={saving}>Cancel</Button>
                                    <Button type="submit" loading={saving} disabled={saving || offices.length === 0}>
                                        {editingId ? 'Save changes' : 'Create service'}
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </Card>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {queues.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4">
                    <Input
                        placeholder="Search by service name, office, or location"
                        value={search}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                        icon={Search}
                    />
                </div>
            )}

            {queues.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={LayoutGrid}
                    title="No services yet"
                    description="Create your first service — students will see it under its office."
                    action={<Button icon={Plus} onClick={() => openCreate()}>Create service</Button>}
                />
            ) : filteredQueues.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Search}
                    title="No services match"
                    description="Try a different search term."
                    action={<Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSearch('')}>Clear search</Button>}
                />
            ) : (
                <div className="space-y-8">
                    {filteredGroups.map((group, groupIdx) => (
                        <motion.section
                            key={group.key}
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.4, delay: groupIdx * 0.05 }}
                        >
                            <div className="mb-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-2 dark:border-slate-700">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                    <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                    <h4 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                        {group.label}
                                    </h4>
                                    <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {group.queues.length} {group.queues.length === 1 ? 'service' : 'services'}
                  </span>
                                </div>
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => openCreate(group.queues[0]?.officeId || '')}
                                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:bg-brand-50"
                                >
                                    <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                    Add to {group.label}
                                </button>

                            </div>

                            <motion.div
                                variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                                initial="initial"
                                animate="animate"
                                className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3"
                            >
                                {group.queues.map((q) => <QueueCard key={q.id} q={q} />)}
                            </motion.div>
                        </motion.section>
                    ))}
                </div>
            )}

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!deleteTarget}
                onClose={() => !deleting && setDeleteTarget(null)}
                onConfirm={deleteQueue}
                title={`Delete "${deleteTarget?.name}"?`}
                description="This removes the service and all its ticket history."
                confirmLabel="Delete service"
                variant="danger"
                loading={deleting}
            />
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Settings.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import {
    Building2, Palette, Save, GraduationCap, Users, RefreshCw,
    Sparkles, FileText, Hash,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import ImageUrlInput from '../../components/ui/ImageUrlInput'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useUniversityStore } from '../../store/universityStore'

const PRESET_COLORS = [
    { label: 'Indigo', value: '#4f46e5' },
    { label: 'Blue', value: '#2563eb' },
    { label: 'Emerald', value: '#059669' },
    { label: 'Rose', value: '#e11d48' },
    { label: 'Amber', value: '#d97706' },
    { label: 'Violet', value: '#7c3aed' },
    { label: 'Slate', value: '#0f172a' },
    { label: 'Crimson', value: '#b91c1c' },
    { label: 'Teal', value: '#0d9488' },
]

const VERIFICATION_MODES = [
    { value: 'PATTERN', label: 'Pattern only',   hint: 'Fast — validates format only' },
    { value: 'LIST',    label: 'Admission list', hint: 'Matches against uploaded list' },
    { value: 'BOTH',    label: 'Both',           hint: 'Strongest — pattern + list match' },
]

export default function Settings() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const { refresh: refreshUniversity } = useUniversityStore()
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        name: '', logoUrl: null, campusImageUrl: null, tagline: '',
        primaryColor: '#4f46e5', emailDomain: '',
        matricPattern: '', matricExample: '', matricDescription: '',
        maxStaff: 10, maxQueues: 5,
        fresherRegistrationEnabled: true,
        fresherVerificationMode: 'PATTERN',
        requireJambReg: true,
        jambRegPattern: '', jambRegExample: '', jambRegDescription: '',
        requireAdmissionRef: false,
        admissionRefPattern: '', admissionRefExample: '', admissionRefDescription: '',
        requireApplicationNumber: false,
        applicationNumberPattern: '', applicationNumberExample: '', applicationNumberDescription: '',
    })

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/admin/settings')
            const u = res.data.data
            setForm({
                name: u.name || '',
                logoUrl: u.logoUrl || null,
                campusImageUrl: u.campusImageUrl || null,
                tagline: u.tagline || '',
                primaryColor: u.primaryColor || '#4f46e5',
                emailDomain: u.emailDomain || '',
                matricPattern: u.matricPattern || '',
                matricExample: u.matricExample || '',
                matricDescription: u.matricDescription || '',
                maxStaff: u.maxStaff ?? 10,
                maxQueues: u.maxQueues ?? 5,
                fresherRegistrationEnabled: u.fresherRegistrationEnabled !== false,
                fresherVerificationMode: u.fresherVerificationMode || 'PATTERN',
                requireJambReg: u.requireJambReg !== false,
                jambRegPattern: u.jambRegPattern || '',
                jambRegExample: u.jambRegExample || '',
                jambRegDescription: u.jambRegDescription || '',
                requireAdmissionRef: !!u.requireAdmissionRef,
                admissionRefPattern: u.admissionRefPattern || '',
                admissionRefExample: u.admissionRefExample || '',
                admissionRefDescription: u.admissionRefDescription || '',
                requireApplicationNumber: !!u.requireApplicationNumber,
                applicationNumberPattern: u.applicationNumberPattern || '',
                applicationNumberExample: u.applicationNumberExample || '',
                applicationNumberDescription: u.applicationNumberDescription || '',
            })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load settings' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])

    useEffect(() => {
        const root = document.documentElement
        const base = form.primaryColor || '#4f46e5'
        const hexToRgb = (hex) => {
            const h = hex.replace('#', '')
            const full = h.length === 3 ? h.split('').map((c) => c + c).join('') : h
            const n = parseInt(full, 16)
            return { r: (n >> 16) & 255, g: (n >> 8) & 255, b: n & 255 }
        }
        const rgbToHex = (r, g, b) =>
            `#${[r, g, b].map((v) => Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(255, v))).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')}`
        const mix = (color, target, amount) => {
            const c = hexToRgb(color)
            const t = target === 'white' ? { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255 } : { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 }
            const a = Math.abs(amount)
            return rgbToHex(c.r + (t.r - c.r) * a, c.g + (t.g - c.g) * a, c.b + (t.b - c.b) * a)
        }
        const shades = {
            50: mix(base, 'white', 0.95), 100: mix(base, 'white', 0.88),
            200: mix(base, 'white', 0.72), 300: mix(base, 'white', 0.5),
            400: mix(base, 'white', 0.24), 500: mix(base, 'white', 0.1),
            600: base, 700: mix(base, 'black', 0.18), 800: mix(base, 'black', 0.32),
            900: mix(base, 'black', 0.48), 950: mix(base, 'black', 0.65),
        }
        Object.entries(shades).forEach(([k, v]) => root.style.setProperty(`--color-brand-${k}`, v))
    }, [form.primaryColor])

    const save = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setSaving(true)
        try {
            await api.put('/api/admin/settings', {
                ...form,
                maxStaff: Number(form.maxStaff),
                maxQueues: Number(form.maxQueues),
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Settings saved' })
            refreshUniversity()
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to save' })
        } finally {
            setSaving(false)
        }
    }

    const setField = (field) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [field]: e.target.value })
    const setCheck = (field) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [field]: e.target.checked })

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
                <div className="mb-8 h-10 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-slate-200" />
                <div className="space-y-6">
                    {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="h-48 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-slate-100" />
                    ))}
                </div>
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl">
            <PageHeader
                title="University Settings"
                description="Manage branding, registration rules, and fresher verification."
            />

            <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-6">
                {/* BRANDING */}
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50">
                            <Palette className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Branding</h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Logo, campus photo and colors.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <Input label="University name" value={form.name} onChange={setField('name')} placeholder="University of Lagos" required icon={Building2} />
                    <Input label="Tagline" value={form.tagline} onChange={setField('tagline')} placeholder="Skip the line. Not the service." hint="Shown on the login page hero." />

                    <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                        <ImageUrlInput label="University logo URL" value={form.logoUrl} onChange={(url) => setForm({ ...form, logoUrl: url })} placeholder="https://unilag.edu.ng/logo.png" hint="Right-click the logo on your school's site → Copy image address." previewHeight="h-32" />
                        <ImageUrlInput label="Campus photo URL" value={form.campusImageUrl} onChange={(url) => setForm({ ...form, campusImageUrl: url })} placeholder="https://unilag.edu.ng/campus.jpg" hint="Paste any public image URL of your campus." previewHeight="h-32" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-4">
                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Primary color</label>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                            {PRESET_COLORS.map((c) => (
                                <button key={c.value} type="button" onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, primaryColor: c.value })}
                                        className={`h-9 w-9 rounded-lg border-2 transition-transform hover:scale-110 ${form.primaryColor === c.value ? 'border-slate-900 ring-2 ring-slate-900 ring-offset-1' : 'border-white shadow-sm'}`}
                                        style={{ backgroundColor: c.value }} title={c.label} aria-label={c.label}
                                />
                            ))}
                            <label className="ml-2 flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-slate-50">
                                <input type="color" value={form.primaryColor} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, primaryColor: e.target.value })} className="h-5 w-5 cursor-pointer rounded border-0 bg-transparent p-0" />
                                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-slate-600">{form.primaryColor}</span>
                            </label>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>

                {/* REGISTRATION RULES */}
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50">
                            <GraduationCap className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Registration rules</h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">How continuing students validate their identity.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <Input label="Email domain" value={form.emailDomain} onChange={setField('emailDomain')} placeholder="@unilag.edu.ng" hint="Only emails ending with this are accepted for continuing students." />
                    <Input label="Matric number pattern (regex)" value={form.matricPattern} onChange={setField('matricPattern')} placeholder="^\d{2}/\d{5}/\d{3}$" hint="Format enforced when students submit a matric." />

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                        <Input label="Matric example" value={form.matricExample} onChange={setField('matricExample')} placeholder="21/12345/001" />
                        <Input label="Matric format description" value={form.matricDescription} onChange={setField('matricDescription')} placeholder="YY/XXXXX/XXX" />
                    </div>
                </Card>

                {/* FRESHER REGISTRATION */}
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50">
                            <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Fresher registration</h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">How new students register before they get a matric number.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
                        <input
                            type="checkbox"
                            checked={form.fresherRegistrationEnabled}
                            onChange={setCheck('fresherRegistrationEnabled')}
                            className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500"
                        />
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">Allow fresher registration</div>
                            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                Freshers register with their JAMB / admission info and can
                                access fresher-friendly queues. They upgrade to full
                                student access once they receive a matric.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </label>

                    {form.fresherRegistrationEnabled && (
                        <>
                            <div className="mt-5">
                                <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">Verification mode</label>
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-3">
                                    {VERIFICATION_MODES.map((m) => (
                                        <button
                                            key={m.value}
                                            type="button"
                                            onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, fresherVerificationMode: m.value })}
                                            className={`rounded-lg border p-3 text-left transition-colors ${
                                                form.fresherVerificationMode === m.value
                                                    ? 'border-brand-500 bg-brand-50'
                                                    : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300'
                                            }`}
                                        >
                                            <div className={`text-sm font-semibold ${form.fresherVerificationMode === m.value ? 'text-brand-700' : 'text-slate-900'}`}>
                                                {m.label}
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">{m.hint}</div>
                                        </button>
                                    ))}
                                </div>
                                {(form.fresherVerificationMode === 'LIST' || form.fresherVerificationMode === 'BOTH') && (
                                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-amber-700">
                                        ⚠️ Requires an uploaded admission list. Upload it from the super admin panel.
                                    </p>
                                )}
                            </div>

                            {/* JAMB */}
                            <div className="mt-5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-4">
                                <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3">
                                    <input type="checkbox" checked={form.requireJambReg} onChange={setCheck('requireJambReg')} className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500" />
                                    <div className="flex-1">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                            <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                            Require JAMB registration number
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </label>
                                {form.requireJambReg && (
                                    <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 pl-7 sm:grid-cols-3">
                                        <Input label="Pattern (regex)" value={form.jambRegPattern} onChange={setField('jambRegPattern')} placeholder="^\d{10}$" />
                                        <Input label="Example" value={form.jambRegExample} onChange={setField('jambRegExample')} placeholder="2025123456" />
                                        <Input label="Description" value={form.jambRegDescription} onChange={setField('jambRegDescription')} placeholder="10-digit number" />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>

                            {/* Admission ref */}
                            <div className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-4">
                                <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3">
                                    <input type="checkbox" checked={form.requireAdmissionRef} onChange={setCheck('requireAdmissionRef')} className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500" />
                                    <div className="flex-1">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                            <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                            Require admission reference number
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </label>
                                {form.requireAdmissionRef && (
                                    <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 pl-7 sm:grid-cols-3">
                                        <Input label="Pattern (regex)" value={form.admissionRefPattern} onChange={setField('admissionRefPattern')} placeholder="^ADM/\d{4}/\d{5}$" />
                                        <Input label="Example" value={form.admissionRefExample} onChange={setField('admissionRefExample')} placeholder="ADM/2025/00123" />
                                        <Input label="Description" value={form.admissionRefDescription} onChange={setField('admissionRefDescription')} placeholder="ADM/YYYY/NNNNN" />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>

                            {/* Application number */}
                            <div className="mt-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-4">
                                <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3">
                                    <input type="checkbox" checked={form.requireApplicationNumber} onChange={setCheck('requireApplicationNumber')} className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500" />
                                    <div className="flex-1">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                            <Hash className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                            Require application number
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </label>
                                {form.requireApplicationNumber && (
                                    <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 pl-7 sm:grid-cols-3">
                                        <Input label="Pattern (regex)" value={form.applicationNumberPattern} onChange={setField('applicationNumberPattern')} placeholder="^APP-\d{4}-\d{5}$" />
                                        <Input label="Example" value={form.applicationNumberExample} onChange={setField('applicationNumberExample')} placeholder="APP-2025-00123" />
                                        <Input label="Description" value={form.applicationNumberDescription} onChange={setField('applicationNumberDescription')} placeholder="APP-YYYY-NNNNN" />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}
                </Card>

                {/* LIMITS */}
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50">
                            <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Account limits</h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Maximum staff members and queues.</p>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                        <Input label="Max staff members" type="number" min="1" value={form.maxStaff} onChange={setField('maxStaff')} />
                        <Input label="Max active queues" type="number" min="1" value={form.maxQueues} onChange={setField('maxQueues')} />
                    </div>
                </Card>

                <div className="sticky bottom-0 -mx-4 flex items-center justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white/90 px-4 py-4 backdrop-blur sm:-mx-6 sm:px-6">
                    <Button type="button" variant="secondary" icon={RefreshCw} onClick={load} disabled={saving}>Reset</Button>
                    <Button type="submit" icon={Save} loading={saving} disabled={saving}>
                        {saving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save changes'}
                    </Button>
                </div>
            </form>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Staff.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Plus,
    Trash2,
    Mail,
    Phone,
    Building2,
    X,
    Users,
    Link as LinkIcon,
    Search,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem, expand } from '../../lib/motion'

const initialForm = {
    fullName: '',
    email: '',
    password: '',
    department: '',
    phone: '',
}

export default function Staff() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [staff, setStaff] = useState([])
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false)
    const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
    const [form, setForm] = useState(initialForm)
    const [assignTarget, setAssignTarget] = useState(null)
    const [assigning, setAssigning] = useState(false)
    const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState(null)
    const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false)
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [s, q] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/admin/staff'),
                api.get('/api/admin/queues'),
            ])
            setStaff(s.data.data || [])
            setQueues(q.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const setField = (f) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [f]: e.target.value })

    const createStaff = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setCreating(true)
        try {
            await api.post('/api/admin/staff', form)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Staff member added' })
            setForm(initialForm)
            setShowCreate(false)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
