            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to create staff' })
        } finally {
            setCreating(false)
        }
    }

    const assignToQueue = async (queueId) => {
        if (!assignTarget) return
        setAssigning(true)
        try {
            await api.post(`/api/admin/queues/${queueId}/staff/${assignTarget.id}`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Staff assigned to queue' })
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to assign' })
        } finally {
            setAssigning(false)
        }
    }

    const unassignFromQueue = async (queueId, staffId) => {
        try {
            await api.delete(`/api/admin/queues/${queueId}/staff/${staffId}`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Staff removed from queue' })
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to unassign' })
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    const assignmentsByStaff = new Map()
    queues.forEach((q) => {
        ;(q.staff || []).forEach((s) => {
            if (!assignmentsByStaff.has(s.id)) assignmentsByStaff.set(s.id, [])
            assignmentsByStaff.get(s.id).push({ queueId: q.id, queueName: q.name })
        })
    })

    const filteredStaff = staff.filter((s) => {
        if (!search.trim()) return true
        const q = search.toLowerCase()
        return (
            s.fullName?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            s.email?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
            s.department?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        )
    })

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Staff Management"
                description="Add staff members and assign them to queues so they can serve students."
                action={
                    <Button
                        icon={Plus}
                        onClick={() => setShowCreate((v) => !v)}
                        variant={showCreate ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                    >
                        {showCreate ? 'Cancel' : 'Add staff'}
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <AnimatePresence>
                {showCreate && (
                    <motion.div
                        variants={expand}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        exit="exit"
                        className="mb-8 overflow-hidden"
                    >
                        <Card>
                            <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                Add a new staff member
                            </h3>
                            <form onSubmit={createStaff} className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                <Input label="Full name" value={form.fullName} onChange={setField('fullName')} placeholder="Jane Doe" required />
                                <Input label="Email" type="email" value={form.email} onChange={setField('email')} placeholder="jane.staff@university.edu.ng" required />
                                <Input label="Password=REDACTED
                                <Input label="Department" value={form.department} onChange={setField('department')} placeholder="e.g. Bursary" icon={Building2} />
                                <Input label="Phone" value={form.phone} onChange={setField('phone')} placeholder="e.g. 08012345678" icon={Phone} />
                                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                                    <Button type="submit" loading={creating} disabled={creating}>
                                        Create staff account
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </Card>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {staff.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-4">
                    <Input
                        placeholder="Search by name, email, or department…"
                        value={search}
                        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                        icon={Search}
                    />
                </div>
            )}

            {staff.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Users}
                    title="No staff members yet"
                    description="Add your first staff member so they can begin serving students."
                    action={
                        <Button icon={Plus} onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
                            Add staff
                        </Button>
                    }
                />
            ) : filteredStaff.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Search}
                    title="No staff match your search"
                    description="Try a different name, email, or department."
                    action={
                        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSearch('')}>
                            Clear search
                        </Button>
                    }
                />
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    className="space-y-3"
                >
                    {filteredStaff.map((s) => {
                        const assigned = assignmentsByStaff.get(s.id) || []
                        return (
                            <motion.div key={s.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                                <Card>
                                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
                                        <div className="flex min-w-0 items-start gap-3">
                                            <div className="flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-sm font-semibold text-white">
                                                {s.fullName?.split(' ').map((p) => p[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="min-w-0">
                                                <div className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                    {s.fullName}
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="mt-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Mail className="h-3 w-3" />
                              {s.email}
                          </span>
                                                </div>

                                                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                                                    {assigned.length === 0 ? (
                                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                              Not assigned to any queue
                            </span>
                                                    ) : (
                                                        assigned.map((a) => (
                                                            <span
                                                                key={a.queueId}
                                                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-brand-700"
                                                            >
                                {a.queueName}
                                                                <button
                                                                    onClick={() => unassignFromQueue(a.queueId, s.id)}
                                                                    className="rounded-full p-0.5 hover:bg-brand-100"
                                                                    aria-label={`Remove from ${a.queueName}`}
                                                                >
                                  <X className="h-3 w-3" />
                                </button>
                              </span>
                                                        ))
                                                    )}
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-2">
                                            <Button
                                                size="sm"
                                                variant="secondary"
                                                icon={LinkIcon}
                                                onClick={() => setAssignTarget(s)}
                                            >
                                                Assign
                                            </Button>
                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(s)}
                                                className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600"
                                                aria-label="Remove staff"
                                            >
                                                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </Card>
                            </motion.div>
                        )
                    })}
                </motion.div>
            )}

            <AnimatePresence>
                {assignTarget && (
                    <>
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            onClick={() => !assigning && setAssignTarget(null)}
                            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm"
                        />
                        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                            <motion.div
                                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 8 }}
                                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                                exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 8 }}
                                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                                className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
                            >
                                <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                    Assign {assignTarget.fullName}
                                </h3>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                    Choose which queues this staff member should serve.
                                </p>

                                <div className="mt-5 max-h-72 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto">
                                    {queues.map((q) => {
                                        const alreadyAssigned = (q.staff || []).some(
                                            (s) => s.id === assignTarget.id
                                        )
                                        return (
                                            <button
                                                key={q.id}
                                                disabled={alreadyAssigned || assigning}
                                                onClick={() => assignToQueue(q.id)}
                                                className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg border px-4 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors ${
                                                    alreadyAssigned
                                                        ? 'cursor-not-allowed border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50'
                                                        : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-brand-300 hover:bg-brand-50/50'
                                                }`}
                                            >
                        <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                          {q.name}
                        </span>
                                                {alreadyAssigned ? (
                                                    <Badge variant="success">Assigned</Badge>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <Plus className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                                                )}
                                            </button>
                                        )
                                    })}
                                </div>

                                <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end">
                                    <Button
                                        variant="secondary"
                                        size="sm"
                                        onClick={() => setAssignTarget(null)}
                                        disabled={assigning}
                                    >
                                        Done
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </motion.div>
                        </div>
                    </>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!deleteTarget}
                onClose={() => !deleting && setDeleteTarget(null)}
                onConfirm={async () => {
                    setDeleting(true)
                    try {
                        const assignments = assignmentsByStaff.get(deleteTarget.id) || []
                        for (const a of assignments) {
                            await api.delete(`/api/admin/queues/${a.queueId}/staff/${deleteTarget.id}`)
                        }
                        push({ type: 'info', message: 'Removed from all queues' })
                        setDeleteTarget(null)
                        await load()
                    } catch (err) {
                        push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed' })
                    } finally {
                        setDeleting(false)
                    }
                }}
                title={`Remove ${deleteTarget?.fullName}?`}
                description="This will remove the staff member from all queues. They'll no longer be able to serve students."
                confirmLabel="Remove staff"
                variant="danger"
                loading={deleting}
            />
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/admin/Workflows.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Plus,
    Route as RouteIcon,
    Trash2,
    ChevronUp,
    ChevronDown,
    X,
    Users,
    CheckCircle2,
    Info,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Select from '../../components/ui/Select'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem, expand } from '../../lib/motion'

export default function Workflows() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [workflows, setWorkflows] = useState([])
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false)
    const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
    const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState(null)
    const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false)

    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        name: '',
        description: '',
        session: '',
        steps: [{ queueId: '', label: '', instructions: '', isRequired: true }],
    })

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [w, q] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/admin/workflows'),
                api.get('/api/admin/queues'),
            ])
            setWorkflows(w.data.data || [])
            setQueues(q.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const addStep = () => {
        setForm({
            ...form,
            steps: [...form.steps, { queueId: '', label: '', instructions: '', isRequired: true }],
        })
    }

    const removeStep = (idx) => {
        if (form.steps.length <= 1) return
        setForm({ ...form, steps: form.steps.filter((_, i) => i !== idx) })
    }

    const moveStep = (idx, dir) => {
        const newSteps = [...form.steps]
        const target = idx + dir
        if (target < 0 || target >= newSteps.length) return
            ;[newSteps[idx], newSteps[target]] = [newSteps[target], newSteps[idx]]
        setForm({ ...form, steps: newSteps })
    }

    const updateStep = (idx, field, value) => {
        const newSteps = [...form.steps]
        newSteps[idx] = { ...newSteps[idx], [field]: value }
        setForm({ ...form, steps: newSteps })
    }

    const submit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (form.steps.some((s) => !s.queueId)) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Please select a queue for every step' })
            return
        }
        setCreating(true)
        try {
            await api.post('/api/admin/workflows', {
                name: form.name,
                description: form.description || null,
                session: form.session || null,
                steps: form.steps.map((s) => ({
                    queueId: Number(s.queueId),
                    label: s.label || null,
                    instructions: s.instructions || null,
                    isRequired: s.isRequired,
                })),
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Workflow created' })
            setForm({
                name: '',
                description: '',
                session: '',
                steps: [{ queueId: '', label: '', instructions: '', isRequired: true }],
            })
            setShowCreate(false)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to create workflow' })
        } finally {
            setCreating(false)
        }
    }

    const deleteWorkflow = async () => {
        if (!deleteTarget) return
        setDeleting(true)
        try {
            await api.delete(`/api/admin/workflows/${deleteTarget.id}`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Workflow deleted' })
            setDeleteTarget(null)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to delete' })
        } finally {
            setDeleting(false)
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    const queueOptions = queues.map((q) => ({ value: q.id, label: q.name }))

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Workflows"
                description="Build multi-step clearance chains. Students complete them in order."
                action={
                    <Button
                        icon={Plus}
                        onClick={() => setShowCreate((v) => !v)}
                        variant={showCreate ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                    >
                        {showCreate ? 'Cancel' : 'New workflow'}
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            <AnimatePresence>
                {showCreate && (
                    <motion.div
                        variants={expand}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        exit="exit"
                        className="mb-8 overflow-hidden"
                    >
                        <Card>
                            <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Create workflow</h3>
                            <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                                    <Input
                                        label="Workflow name"
                                        value={form.name}
                                        onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })}
                                        placeholder="Enter workflow name"
                                        required
                                    />
                                    <Input
                                        label="Session"
                                        value={form.session}
                                        onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, session: e.target.value })}
                                        placeholder="Enter session"
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div className="mb-4 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 p-4 text-sm text-sky-900">
                                    <Info className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-sky-600" />
                                    <div>
                                        <p className="font-medium">What is this?</p>
                                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-sky-800">
                                            This is a required multi-step process. Complete each service in order —
                                            locked steps unlock as you finish the previous one.
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                        Description
                                    </label>
                                    <textarea
                                        rows={2}
                                        value={form.description}
                                        onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })}
                                        placeholder="What does this workflow accomplish?"
                                        className="w-full resize-none rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm focus-visible:border-brand-500 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-500/20"
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                                        <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                            Steps (in order)
                                        </label>
                                        <Button type="button" size="sm" variant="secondary" icon={Plus} onClick={addStep}>
                                            Add step
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="space-y-3">
                                        {form.steps.map((step, idx) => (
                                            <div
                                                key={idx}
                                                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 p-4"
                                            >
                                                <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                        <span className="flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-brand-600 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                                                            {idx + 1}
                                                        </span>
                                                        <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                                            Step {idx + 1}
                                                        </span>
                                                    </div>
                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => moveStep(idx, -1)}
                                                            disabled={idx === 0}
                                                            className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-white disabled:opacity-30"
                                                        >
                                                            <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                        </button>
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => moveStep(idx, 1)}
                                                            disabled={idx === form.steps.length - 1}
                                                            className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-white disabled:opacity-30"
                                                        >
                                                            <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                        </button>
                                                        <button
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => removeStep(idx)}
                                                            disabled={form.steps.length === 1}
                                                            className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600 disabled:opacity-30"
                                                        >
                                                            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                        </button>
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>

                                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                                                    <Select
                                                        label="Service"
                                                        value={step.queueId}
                                                        onChange={(e) => updateStep(idx, 'queueId', e.target.value)}
                                                        options={queueOptions}
                                                        placeholder="Select service"
                                                        required
                                                    />
                                                    <Input
                                                        label="Label (optional)"
                                                        value={step.label}
                                                        onChange={(e) => updateStep(idx, 'label', e.target.value)}
                                                        placeholder="Enter a short description"
                                                    />
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        ))}
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                                    <Button
                                        type="button"
                                        variant="secondary"
                                        onClick={() => setShowCreate(false)}
                                        disabled={creating}
                                    >
                                        Cancel
                                    </Button>
                                    <Button type="submit" loading={creating} disabled={creating}>
                                        Create workflow
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </Card>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {workflows.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={RouteIcon}
                    title="No active workflows"
                    description="Workflows are multi-step processes assigned by your university — like clearance or registration — where students visit several services in order. When you create one, it will appear here."
                    action={
                        <Button icon={Plus} onClick={() => setShowCreate(true)}>
                            Create workflow
                        </Button>
                    }
                />
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2"
                >
                    {workflows.map((w) => (
                        <motion.div key={w.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                            <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
                                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                    <div className="min-w-0">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                            <RouteIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{w.name}</h3>
                                        </div>
                                        {w.session && (
                                            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">Session {w.session}</p>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                        <Badge variant={w.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                                            {w.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                                        </Badge>
                                        <button
                                            onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(w)}
                                            className="rounded-lg p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600"
                                            aria-label="Delete workflow"
                                        >
                                            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                {w.description && (
                                    <p className="mt-3 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-slate-600">{w.description}</p>
                                )}

                                <ol className="mt-4 space-y-1.5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                                    {w.steps.map((s, idx) => (
                                        <li key={s.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-slate-700">
                                            <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600">
                                                {idx + 1}
                                            </span>
                                            <span className="flex-1 truncate">
                                                {s.queueName}
                                                {s.label && (
                                                    <span className="ml-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500">· {s.label}</span>
                                                )}
                                            </span>
                                        </li>
                                    ))}
                                </ol>

                                <div className="mt-auto flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                        <Users className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                        {w.inProgressCount ?? 0} in progress
                                    </span>
                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                        {w.completedCount ?? 0} completed
                                    </span>
                                </div>
                            </Card>
                        </motion.div>
                    ))}
                </motion.div>
            )}

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!deleteTarget}
                onClose={() => !deleting && setDeleteTarget(null)}
                onConfirm={deleteWorkflow}
                title={`Delete "${deleteTarget?.name}"?`}
                description="Workflows with students still in progress cannot be deleted. Make sure all students have completed or abandoned it first."
                confirmLabel="Delete workflow"
                variant="danger"
                loading={deleting}
            />
        </>
    )
}

import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { Mail, ArrowLeft, Send, CheckCircle2, Copy } from 'lucide-react'
import Input from '../components/ui/Input'
import Button from '../components/ui/Button'
import api from '../api/axios'
import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'
import { ease, fadeUp } from '../lib/motion'

export default function ForgotPassword() {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
    const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)
    const [resetToken=REDACTED

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setLoading(true)
        try {
            const res = await api.post('/api/auth/forgot-password', { email })
            setSent(true)
            const token=REDACTED
            if (token) setResetToken(token)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to send reset link' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }

    const copyToken=REDACTED
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(resetToken)
        push({ type: 'success', message: 'Token copied' })
    }

    return (
        <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 px-4 py-12">
            <motion.div
                variants={fadeUp}
                initial="initial"
                animate="animate"
                transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease }}
                className="w-full max-w-md"
            >
                <Link
                    to="/login"
                    className="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 transition-colors hover:text-slate-700"
                >
                    <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Back to sign in
                </Link>

                <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 shadow-sm">
                    {!sent ? (
                        <>
                            <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                                Forgot password?
                            </h1>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                Enter the email you registered with and we'll send a reset link.
                            </p>

                            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-6">
                                <Input
                                    label="Email"
                                    type="email"
                                    value={email}
                                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="you@university.edu.ng"
                                    icon={Mail}
                                    required
                                    autoFocus
                                />

                                <Button type="submit" fullWidth loading={loading} disabled={loading}>
                                    {loading ? 'Sending…' : 'Send reset link'}
                                    {!loading && <Send className="h-4 w-4" />}
                                </Button>
                            </form>
                        </>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <div className="mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-100">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 text-emerald-600" />
                            </div>
                            <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                                Check your email
                            </h1>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                If an account exists with <strong>{email}</strong>, a reset link has been
                                sent. It expires in 30 minutes.
                            </p>

                            {resetToken && (
                                <div className="mt-5 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-3 text-xs">
                                    <p className="mb-2 font-medium text-amber-900">
                                        ⚠️ Dev mode — reset link (email not configured):
                                    </p>
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                        <code className="flex-1 truncate rounded bg-white px-2 py-1 font-mono text-[10px] text-amber-900">
                                            {resetToken}
                                        </code>
                                        <button
                                            onClick={copyToken}
                                            className="rounded-md border border-amber-300 bg-white p-1.5 transition-colors hover:bg-amber-100"
                                            aria-label="Copy token"
                                        >
                                            <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-amber-700" />
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                    <button
                                        onClick={() => navigate(`/reset-password=REDACTED
                                        className="mt-2 text-[11px] font-medium text-amber-800 underline"
                                    >
                                        Open reset page →
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            )}

                            <Button
                                variant="secondary"
                                fullWidth
                                className="mt-5"
                                onClick={() => {
                                    setSent(false)
                                    setEmail('')
                                    setResetToken(null)
                                }}
                            >
                                Try a different email
                            </Button>
                        </>
                    )}
                </div>
            </motion.div>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/Home.jsx =====
    import { useEffect, useRef, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion, useReducedMotion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    ArrowRight,
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    Sparkles,
    Users,
    Building2,
    ShieldCheck,
    Clock,
    Bell,
    LineChart,
    Route as RouteIcon,
    Ticket,
    GraduationCap,
    CheckCircle2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuthStore } from '../store/authStore'

const SLIDES = [
    {
        id: 'students',
        src: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1523240795612-9a054b0db644',
        alt: 'Students walking on a university campus',
        eyebrow: { icon: GraduationCap, label: 'For students' },
        headline: 'Skip the line.',
        headlineAccent: 'Not the service.',
        sub: 'Join a queue from your phone, watch your position in real time, and show up exactly when it’s your turn.',
        cta: { label: 'Get started free', to: '/register' },
        ctaSecondary: { label: 'Sign in', to: '/login' },
    },
    {
        id: 'freshers',
        src: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1541339907198-e08756dedf3f',

        alt: 'Administrator viewing analytics on a laptop',
        eyebrow: { icon: LineChart, label: 'For administrators' },
        headline: 'See every queue.',

const AUTO_ADVANCE_MS = 5000
const SLIDE_MS = 800
const SLIDE_COUNT = SLIDES.length

// Source-set with a wide range of sizes so browsers pick the fastest
// one that still looks sharp on their viewport.
function buildSrcSet(baseUrl) {
    return [
        `${baseUrl}?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=640&dpr=2 640w`,
        `${baseUrl}?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=1024&dpr=2 1024w`,
        `${baseUrl}?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=1440&dpr=2 1440w`,
        `${baseUrl}?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=1920&dpr=2 1920w`,
        `${baseUrl}?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=2560&dpr=2 2560w`,
    ].join(', ')
}
const FULL_URL = (base) => `${base}?auto=format&fit=crop&q=80&w=1920&dpr=2`

function HeroCarousel() {
    const sectionRef = useRef(null)
    const [index, setIndex] = useState(0)
    const [paused, setPaused] = useState(false)
    const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(() => new Set())
    const [imageReady, setImageReady] = useState(() => new Set())
    const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion()
    const { user } = useAuthStore()

    const dashboards = {
        SUPER_ADMIN: '/super-admin',
        ADMIN: '/admin',
        STAFF: '/staff',
        USER: '/student',
        FRESHER: '/student',
    }
    const dashboardHref = user ? dashboards[user.role] || '/' : null

    const goTo = useCallback((next) => {
        setIndex((current) => ((next % SLIDE_COUNT) + SLIDE_COUNT) % SLIDE_COUNT)
    }, [])

    const next = useCallback(() => goTo(index + 1), [goTo, index])
    const prev = useCallback(() => goTo(index - 1), [goTo, index])

    // ─── Auto-advance ───
    // FIX: On remount (navigating back to home), the paused state resets to
    // false, so the timer always starts fresh.
    useEffect(() => {
        if (paused || reduceMotion) return
        const t = setTimeout(() => goTo(index + 1), AUTO_ADVANCE_MS)
        return () => clearTimeout(t)
    }, [index, paused, reduceMotion, goTo])

    // ─── Global mouse tracking (replaces onMouseEnter/onMouseLeave) ───
    // Only considers a hover "active" when the cursor actually MOVES inside
    // the section. Prevents the carousel from getting stuck paused when the
    // mouse happens to be sitting over the hero on remount.
    useEffect(() => {
        const onMove = (e) => {
            const el = sectionRef.current
            if (!el) return
            const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect()
            const inside =
                e.clientX >= rect.left &&
                e.clientX <= rect.right &&
                e.clientY >= rect.top &&
                e.clientY <= rect.bottom
            setPaused((p) => (p === inside ? p : inside))
        }
        window.addEventListener('mousemove', onMove, { passive: true })
        return () => window.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMove)
    }, [])

    // ─── Preload ALL slides on mount ───
    // Using `img.srcset` so the browser picks the right size for the current
    // viewport. By the time we're on slide 2+, every image is already cached.
    useEffect(() => {
        SLIDES.forEach((s, i) => {
            if (loaded.has(i)) return
            const img = new Image()
            img.srcset = buildSrcSet(s.src)
            img.sizes = '100vw'
            img.src = FULL_URL(s.src)
            img.onload = () => {
                setLoaded((prev) => {
                    if (prev.has(i)) return prev
                    const nxt = new Set(prev)
                    nxt.add(i)
                    return nxt
                })
            }
        })
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [])

    // Keyboard nav
    useEffect(() => {
        const onKey = (e) => {
            if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') next()
            else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') prev()
        }
        window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
        return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    }, [next, prev])

    return (
        <section
            ref={sectionRef}
            className="relative isolate h-[92vh] min-h-[600px] w-full overflow-hidden bg-slate-900"
            aria-roledescription="carousel"
            aria-label="Product highlights"
        >
            <motion.div
                className="absolute inset-0 flex"
                animate={{ x: `-${index * 100}%` }}
                transition={{
                    duration: reduceMotion ? 0 : SLIDE_MS / 1000,
                    ease: [0.83, 0, 0.17, 1],
                }}
            >
                {SLIDES.map((s, i) => {
                    const isLoaded = imageReady.has(i)
                    const isActive = i === index
                    return (
                        <div
                            key={s.id}
                            className="relative h-full w-full shrink-0 overflow-hidden"
                            aria-hidden={!isActive}
                        >
                            {/* ─── Skeleton shimmer while the image loads ─── */}
                            <div
                                className={`absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-800 via-slate-700 to-slate-800 transition-opacity duration-500 ${
                                    isLoaded ? 'opacity-0' : 'opacity-100'
                                }`}
                            >
                                <motion.div
                                    initial={{ x: '-100%' }}
                                    animate={{ x: '100%' }}
                                    transition={{ duration: 1.8, repeat: Infinity, ease: 'linear' }}
                                    className="absolute inset-y-0 w-1/2 bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-white/[0.06] to-transparent"
                                />
                            </div>

                            {/* ─── Image ─── */}
                            <img
                                src={FULL_URL(s.src)}
                                srcSet={buildSrcSet(s.src)}
                                sizes="100vw"
                                alt={s.alt}
                                loading={i === 0 ? 'eager' : 'lazy'}
                                fetchPriority={i === 0 ? 'high' : undefined}
                                decoding="async"
                                onLoad={() =>
                                    setImageReady((prev) => {
                                        if (prev.has(i)) return prev
                                        const nxt = new Set(prev)
                                        nxt.add(i)
                                        return nxt
                                    })
                                }
                                className={`absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover transition-opacity duration-700 ${
                                    isLoaded ? 'opacity-100' : 'opacity-0'
                                }`}
                                draggable={false}
                            />

                            {/* ─── Gradient overlays ─── */}
                            <div
                                className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0"
                                style={{
                                    background:
                                        'linear-gradient(to right, rgba(2,6,23,0.90) 0%, rgba(2,6,23,0.76) 28%, rgba(2,6,23,0.40) 50%, rgba(2,6,23,0.08) 72%, rgba(2,6,23,0) 100%)',
                                }}
                            />
                            <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-32 bg-gradient-to-t from-slate-950/75 via-slate-950/25 to-transparent" />

                            {/* ─── Text content ─── */}
                            <div className="absolute inset-0 z-10 mx-auto flex max-w-7xl flex-col justify-center px-6 sm:px-8 lg:px-12">
                                <div className="max-w-2xl">
                                    {/* ─── Professional role badge ─── */}
                                    <div className="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-3 rounded-full border border-white/15 bg-white/[0.06] py-1.5 pl-1.5 pr-4 shadow-xl shadow-black/25 backdrop-blur-xl">
                    <span className="relative flex h-7 w-7 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-500 to-brand-700 shadow-md shadow-brand-600/40">
                      <span className="absolute inset-0 animate-ping rounded-full bg-brand-400/40" />
                      <s.eyebrow.icon className="relative h-3.5 w-3.5 text-white" />
                    </span>
                                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-white">
                      {s.eyebrow.label}
                    </span>
                                    </div>

                                    <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold leading-[1.05] tracking-tight text-white drop-shadow-2xl sm:text-6xl lg:text-7xl">
                                        {s.headline}
                                        <span className="block bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-300 via-brand-400 to-brand-200 bg-clip-text text-transparent">
                      {s.headlineAccent}
                    </span>
                                    </h1>

                                    <p className="mt-6 max-w-xl text-base leading-relaxed text-white/90 drop-shadow-lg sm:text-lg">
                                        {s.sub}
                                    </p>

                                    <div className="mt-9 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                                        <Link
                                            to={dashboardHref || s.cta.to}
                                            className="group inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-white px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 shadow-lg shadow-black/25 transition-all hover:bg-slate-100 hover:shadow-xl"
                                        >
                                            {dashboardHref ? 'Go to dashboard' : s.cta.label}
                                            <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5" />
                                        </Link>
                                        <Link
                                            to={s.ctaSecondary.to}
                                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-white/30 bg-white/10 px-6 py-3.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur-md transition-colors hover:border-white/50 hover:bg-white/20"
                                        >
                                            {s.ctaSecondary.label}
                                        </Link>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )
                })}
            </motion.div>

            {/* ─── Arrows ─── */}
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={prev}
                aria-label="Previous slide"
                className="group absolute left-3 top-1/2 z-20 hidden -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/25 bg-white/10 p-3 text-white backdrop-blur-md transition-all hover:border-white/50 hover:bg-white/20 lg:flex"
            >
                <ChevronLeft className="h-5 w-5 transition-transform group-hover:-translate-x-0.5" />
            </button>
            <button
                type="button"
                onClick={next}
                aria-label="Next slide"
                className="group absolute right-3 top-1/2 z-20 hidden -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full border border-white/25 bg-white/10 p-3 text-white backdrop-blur-md transition-all hover:border-white/50 hover:bg-white/20 lg:flex"
            >
                <ChevronRight className="h-5 w-5 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5" />
            </button>

            {/* ─── Dots + progress ─── */}
            <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-8 z-20 flex flex-col items-center gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    {SLIDES.map((s, i) => {
                        const isActive = i === index
                        return (
                            <button
                                key={s.id}
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => goTo(i)}
                                aria-label={`Go to slide ${i + 1}`}
                                className="group relative h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/30 transition-all"
                                style={{ width: isActive ? 40 : 12 }}
                            >
                <span
                    className={`absolute inset-0 rounded-full transition-colors ${
                        isActive ? 'bg-white' : 'bg-white/60 group-hover:bg-white'
                    }`}
                />
                                {isActive && !paused && !reduceMotion && (
                                    <motion.span
                                        key={`progress-${index}`}
                                        initial={{ scaleX: 0 }}
                                        animate={{ scaleX: 1 }}
                                        transition={{ duration: AUTO_ADVANCE_MS / 1000, ease: 'linear' }}
                                        className="absolute inset-0 origin-left rounded-full bg-brand-300"
                                    />
                                )}
                            </button>
                        )
                    })}
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-white/50">
                    {String(index + 1).padStart(2, '0')} / {String(SLIDE_COUNT).padStart(2, '0')}
                </div>
            </div>
        </section>
    )
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const FEATURES = [

    {

===== ./frontend/src/pages/Login.jsx =====
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { ArrowRight, LogOut, CheckCircle2, Loader2, Eye, EyeOff } from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api/axios'
import { useAuthStore } from '../store/authStore'
import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'
import { ease, fadeUp } from '../lib/motion'

const dashboards = {
    SUPER_ADMIN: '/super-admin',
    ADMIN: '/admin',
    STAFF: '/staff',
    USER: '/student',
    FRESHER: '/student',
}

const DEFAULT_BRAND = { name: 'UniQueue', logoUrl: null, campusImageUrl: null }

function resolveImageUrl(url) {
    if (!url) return null
    if (url.startsWith('http://') || url.startsWith('https://') || url.startsWith('data:')) return url
    const base = import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || ''
    return base ? `${base}${url}` : url
}

export default function Login() {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { login, loading, user, logout } = useAuthStore()
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
    const [password=REDACTED
    const [showPassword=REDACTED
    const [redirecting, setRedirecting] = useState(false)
    const [brand, setBrand] = useState(DEFAULT_BRAND)
    const [imageError, setImageError] = useState(false)
    const [unverified, setUnverified] = useState(false)

    // Detect university from email domain
    useEffect(() => {
        const trimmed = email.trim().toLowerCase()
        const at = trimmed.indexOf('@')
        if (at === -1 || at === trimmed.length - 1) {
            setBrand(DEFAULT_BRAND)
            return
        }
        const domain = trimmed.slice(at + 1)
        const timer = setTimeout(async () => {
            try {
                const res = await api.get(`/api/auth/universities/lookup?domain=${encodeURIComponent(domain)}`)
                const d = res.data?.data
                if (d) {
                    setBrand({
                        name: d.name || DEFAULT_BRAND.name,
                        logoUrl: resolveImageUrl(d.logoUrl),
                        campusImageUrl: resolveImageUrl(d.campusImageUrl),
                    })
                    setImageError(false)
                } else {
                    setBrand(DEFAULT_BRAND)
                }
            } catch {
                setBrand(DEFAULT_BRAND)
            }
        }, 500)
        return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }, [email])

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setRedirecting(true)
        setUnverified(false)

        const res = await login(email, password)

        if (res.success) {
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Signed in successfully' })
            navigate(dashboards[res.role] || '/')
            return
        }

        setRedirecting(false)

        // Detect "not verified" error and offer to go verify
        const msg = (res.error || '').toLowerCase()
        if (msg.includes('not verified') || msg.includes('verification')) {
            setUnverified(true)
            push({
                type: 'info',
                message: 'Your email is not verified. Redirecting to verification…',
            })
            // Auto-redirect after a short beat so the toast is visible
            setTimeout(() => {
                navigate('/verify-email', { state: { email: email.trim() } })
            }, 1200)
            return
        }

        push({ type: 'error', message: res.error })
    }

    const handleSwitchAccount = () => {
        logout()
        push({ type: 'info', message: 'Signed out — sign in with another account' })
        setEmail('')
        setPassword('')
    }

    const showAlreadySignedInBanner = user && !redirecting

    const inputClass =
        'w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 transition-colors focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500'

    return (
        <div className="flex min-h-screen">
            {/* LEFT: image panel */}
            <div className="relative hidden w-1/2 shrink-0 overflow-hidden bg-slate-900 lg:block xl:w-[55%]">
                {brand.campusImageUrl && !imageError ? (
                    <motion.img
                        key={brand.campusImageUrl}
                        src={brand.campusImageUrl}
                        alt=""
                        aria-hidden="true"
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 1.04 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 1.1, ease }}
                        className="absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover object-center"
                        onError={() => setImageError(true)}
                    />
                ) : (
                    <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-600 via-brand-700 to-slate-900" />
                )}
                <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 right-0 w-12 bg-gradient-to-l from-slate-950/25 to-transparent" />
            </div>

            {/* RIGHT: form */}
            <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center bg-white px-6 py-12 dark:bg-slate-950">
                <motion.div
                    variants={fadeUp}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease }}
                    className="w-full max-w-sm"
                >
                    {/* School header */}
                    <motion.div
                        key={`form-brand-${brand.name}`}
                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -4 }}
                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}
                        className="mb-10 flex flex-col items-center text-center"
                    >
                        <div className="flex h-20 w-20 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
                            {brand.logoUrl ? (
                                <img
                                    src={brand.logoUrl}
                                    alt={brand.name}
                                    className="h-full w-full object-contain"
                                    onError={(e) => { e.target.style.display = 'none' }}
                                />
                            ) : (
                                <div className="flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-600 text-lg font-bold text-white">
                                    UQ
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        <h1 className="mt-6 text-xl font-bold uppercase leading-tight tracking-wide text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100 sm:text-2xl">
                            {brand.name}
                        </h1>
                        <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            Queue Management System
                        </p>
                    </motion.div>

                    {showAlreadySignedInBanner && (
                        <div className="mb-6 rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-4">
                            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
                                <div className="flex-1">
                                    <p className="text-sm font-medium text-emerald-900">You're already signed in</p>
                                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-emerald-700">
                                        Signed in as <strong>{user.email}</strong>
                                    </p>
                                    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                                        <Link
                                            to={dashboards[user.role] || '/'}
                                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md bg-emerald-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-emerald-700"
                                        >
                                            Go to dashboard
                                            <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                                        </Link>
                                        <button
                                            onClick={handleSwitchAccount}
                                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-emerald-300 bg-white px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-700 transition-colors hover:bg-emerald-50"
                                        >
                                            <LogOut className="h-3 w-3" />
                                            Switch account
                                        </button>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Unverified hint banner */}
                    {unverified && (
                        <div className="mb-6 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm text-amber-900">
                            <p className="font-medium">Your email isn't verified</p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-800">
                                We're redirecting you to verify it now. Or{' '}
                                <Link to="/verify-email" state={{ email: email.trim() }} className="font-medium underline">
                                    click here to verify
                                </Link>
                                .
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Sign in
                    </h2>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Enter your credentials to access your dashboard.
                    </p>

                    <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-8 space-y-4">
                        <div>
                            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                Email
                            </label>
                            <input
                                type="email"
                                value={email}
                                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="Enter your email"
                                autoComplete="email"
                                required
                                disabled={redirecting}
                                className={inputClass}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                Password
                            </label>
                            <div className="relative">
                                <input
                                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                                    value={password}
                                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="Enter your password"
                                    autoComplete="current-password"
                                    required
                                    disabled={redirecting}
                                    className={`${inputClass} pr-10`}
                                />
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setShowPassword=REDACTED
                                    className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 flex items-center pr-3 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                                    aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                                >
                                    {showPassword=REDACTED
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end">
                            <Link
                                to="/forgot-password"
                                className="text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700"
                            >
                                Forgot password?
                            </Link>
                        </div>

                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={loading || redirecting}
                            className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-60"
                        >
                            {loading || redirecting ? (
                                <>
                                    <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                                    Signing in…
                                </>
                            ) : (
                                <>
                                    Sign in
                                    <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </>
                            )}
                        </button>
                    </form>

                    {/* Footer links */}
                    <div className="mt-6 space-y-2 text-center text-sm">
                        <p className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            Don't have an account?{' '}
                            <Link to="/register" className="font-medium text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700">
                                Register as a student
                            </Link>
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                            Haven't verified your email yet?{' '}
                            <Link
                                to="/verify-email"
                                state={{ email: email.trim() }}
                                className="font-medium text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                            >
                                Verify now
                            </Link>
                        </p>
                    </div>
                </motion.div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'

    Lock,
    Check,
    AlertCircle,
    Eye,
    EyeOff,
    GraduationCap,
    ArrowRight,
    ShieldCheck,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api/axios'
import Card from '../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../components/ui/Badge'
import { SkeletonRow } from '../components/ui/Skeleton'
import ChangeEmailModal from '../components/profile/ChangeEmailModal'
