import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'
import { getInitials } from '../lib/format'

const ROLE_LABELS = {
    SUPER_ADMIN: 'Super Admin',
    ADMIN: 'Administrator',
    STAFF: 'Staff',
    USER: 'Student',
    FRESHER: 'Fresher',
}

const ROLE_VARIANTS = {
    SUPER_ADMIN: 'indigo',
    ADMIN: 'warning',
    STAFF: 'info',
    USER: 'default',
    FRESHER: 'info',
}

// ── Reusable password input with show/hide toggle ──
function PasswordField({ label, value, onChange, placeholder, autoComplete }) {
    const [show, setShow] = useState(false)
    return (
        <div>
            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                {label}
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
                <input
                    type={show ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    value={value}
                    onChange={onChange}
                    placeholder={placeholder}
                    autoComplete={autoComplete}
                    required
                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 transition-colors focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                />
                <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShow((v) => !v)}
                    className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 flex items-center pr-3 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                    aria-label={show ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                >
                    {show ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />}
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}

export default function Profile() {
    const { user, refreshProfile, logout } = useAuthStore()
    const { refresh: refreshUniversity } = useUniversityStore()
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const navigate = useNavigate()

    const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState(null)

    const [profileForm, setProfileForm] = useState({ fullName: '', department: '', phone: '' })
    const [savingProfile, setSavingProfile] = useState(false)

    const [matricInput, setMatricInput] = useState('')
    const [upgrading, setUpgrading] = useState(false)

    const [password=REDACTED
        currentPassword: '',
        newPassword: '',
        confirmPassword: '',
    })
    const [savingPassword=REDACTED

    const [showEmailModal, setShowEmailModal] = useState(false)

    const role = profile?.role || user?.role
    const isSuperAdmin = role === 'SUPER_ADMIN'
    const isAdmin = role === 'ADMIN'
    const isStudent = role === 'USER'
    const isFresher = role === 'FRESHER'

    const dashboards = {
        SUPER_ADMIN: '/super-admin',
        ADMIN: '/admin',
        STAFF: '/staff',
        USER: '/student',
        FRESHER: '/student',
    }
    const backHref = dashboards[role] || '/'

    // ── Load profile ──
    const load = async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/profile')
            const p = res.data?.data
            setProfile(p)
            setProfileForm({
                fullName: p?.fullName || '',
                department: p?.department || '',
                phone: p?.phone || '',
            })
        } catch (err) {
            setLoadError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load profile')
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
        // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
    }, [])

    const updateProfile = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!profileForm.fullName.trim()) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Full name is required' })
            return
        }
        setSavingProfile(true)
        try {
            const payload = {
                fullName: profileForm.fullName.trim(),
                phone: profileForm.phone.trim() || null,
            }
            if (!isSuperAdmin) payload.department = profileForm.department.trim() || null

            await api.put('/api/profile', payload)
            await load()
            await refreshProfile()
            if (refreshUniversity) refreshUniversity()
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Profile updated' })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to update profile' })
        } finally {
            setSavingProfile(false)
        }
    }

    // ── Fresher → Student upgrade ──
    const upgradeToStudent = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!matricInput.trim()) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Enter your matric number' })
            return
        }
        setUpgrading(true)
        try {
            await api.put('/api/profile', { matricNumber: matricInput.trim() })
            await load()
            await refreshProfile()
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Upgraded to student! You now have full access.' })
            setMatricInput('')
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Upgrade failed' })
        } finally {
            setUpgrading(false)
        }
    }

    const changePassword=REDACTED
        e.preventDefault()
        if (passwordForm.newPassword.length < 8) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'New password must be at least 8 characters' })
            return
        }
        if (password=REDACTED
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Passwords do not match' })
            return
        }
        setSavingPassword(true)
        try {
            await api.put('/api/profile/password', {
                currentPassword: passwordForm.currentPassword,
                newPassword: passwordForm.newPassword,
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Password changed successfully' })
            setPasswordForm({ currentPassword: '', newPassword: '', confirmPassword: '' })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to change password' })
        } finally {
            setSavingPassword(false)
        }
    }

    const handleEmailChanged = async () => {
        setShowEmailModal(false)
        push({
            type: 'info',
            message: 'Email updated. Please sign in again with your new email.',
            duration: 5000,
        })
        setTimeout(() => {
            logout()
            navigate('/login')
        }, 1500)
    }

    const display = profile || user

    const inputClass =
        'w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 transition-colors focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500'

    const labelClass =
        'mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'

    // ── Loading state ──
    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
                <div className="mb-6 h-8 w-32 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
                <Card className="mb-6">
                    <SkeletonRow />
                </Card>
                <Card className="mb-6">
                    <SkeletonRow />
                </Card>
            </div>
        )
    }

    // ── Error state ──
    if (loadError) {
        return (
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
                <button
                    onClick={() => navigate(backHref)}
                    className="mb-6 -ml-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                >
                    <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Back to dashboard
                </button>
                <Card>
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-3 py-8 text-center">
                        <AlertCircle className="h-6 w-6 text-rose-600" />
                        <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">{loadError}</p>
                        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
                            Try again
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 lg:px-8">
            {/* ───── Back button ───── */}
            <button
                onClick={() => navigate(backHref)}
                className="mb-6 -ml-3 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-100"
            >
                <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                Back to dashboard
            </button>

            {/* ───── HEADER ───── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.35 }}
            >
                <Card className="mb-6 overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-start gap-5 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
                        <div className="flex h-20 w-20 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-slate-50 ring-1 ring-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:ring-slate-700">
                            {profile?.universityLogoUrl ? (
                                <img
                                    src={profile.universityLogoUrl}
                                    alt={profile.universityName || 'University'}
                                    className="h-full w-full object-contain p-2"
                                />
                            ) : (
                                <div className="flex h-full w-full items-center justify-center bg-slate-900 text-2xl font-semibold text-white dark:bg-slate-700">
                                    {getInitials(display?.fullName)}
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <h1 className="truncate text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                {display?.fullName || 'Your profile'}
                            </h1>
                            <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                                {display?.email}
                            </p>

                            <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                <Badge variant={ROLE_VARIANTS[role] || 'default'}>
                                    <Shield className="mr-1 h-3 w-3" />
                                    {ROLE_LABELS[role] || role}
                                </Badge>

                                {!isSuperAdmin && profile?.universityName && (
                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">
                    <Building2 className="h-3 w-3 text-slate-400" />
                                        {profile.universityName}
                  </span>
                                )}

                                {isStudent && profile?.matricNumber && (
                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 font-mono text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300">
                    {profile.matricNumber}
                  </span>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            </motion.div>

            {/* ───── FRESHER UPGRADE CARD ───── */}
            {isFresher && profile?.canUpgrade && (
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.35, delay: 0.04 }}
                >
                    <Card className="mb-6 overflow-hidden border-brand-200 bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-50 to-white dark:border-brand-900 dark:from-brand-950/40 dark:to-slate-900">
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                            <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-brand-600">
                                <GraduationCap className="h-6 w-6 text-white" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Received your matric number?
                                </h2>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    Enter it below to upgrade to full student access. You'll
                                    immediately unlock all queues at your university.
                                </p>

                                <form onSubmit={upgradeToStudent} className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-2 sm:flex-row">
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={matricInput}
                                        onChange={(e) => setMatricInput(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="Enter your matric number"
                                        className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                                    />
                                    <Button type="submit" loading={upgrading} disabled={upgrading}>
                                        {upgrading ? 'Upgrading…' : 'Upgrade account'}
                                        {!upgrading && <ArrowRight className="ml-1 h-4 w-4" />}
                                    </Button>
                                </form>

                                {profile?.matricPatternHint && (
                                    <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                        Format: <span className="font-mono">{profile.matricPatternHint}</span>
                                    </p>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>
            )}

            {isFresher && !profile?.canUpgrade && (
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    transition={{ delay: 0.04 }}
                >
                    <Card className="mb-6 border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 dark:border-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/40">
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                            <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-amber-600" />
                            <div>
                                <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-100">
                                    You're registered as a fresher
                                </h2>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:text-amber-200">
                                    Your university hasn't set up a matric number format yet. Contact
                                    your admin once you receive your matric.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>
            )}

            {/* ───── LOGIN & SECURITY ───── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.05 }}
            >
                <Card className="mb-6">
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-950/40">
                            <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                Login credentials
                            </h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Your email and how to access your account
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                            <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Email address
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2 truncate text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />
                                {display?.email}
                            </div>
                            {profile?.recoveryEmail && (
                                <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    <span className="font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide">Recovery:</span>{' '}
                                    <span className="font-mono">{profile.recoveryEmail}</span>
                                </div>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        <Button
                            type="button"
                            variant="secondary"
                            onClick={() => setShowEmailModal(true)}
                            className="shrink-0"
                        >
                            Change email
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            </motion.div>

            {/* ───── PERSONAL INFO ───── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.08 }}
            >
                <Card className="mb-6">
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-950/40">
                            <UserIcon className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                Personal information
                            </h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Update your details
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <form onSubmit={updateProfile} className="space-y-4">
                        <div>
                            <label className={labelClass}>Full name</label>
                            <input
                                type="text"
                                value={profileForm.fullName}
                                onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, fullName: e.target.value })}
                                placeholder="Enter your full name"
                                required
                                className={inputClass}
                            />
                        </div>

                        {isFresher && profile?.jambRegNumber && (
                            <div>
                                <label className={labelClass}>JAMB registration number</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={profile.jambRegNumber}
                                    readOnly
                                    className="w-full cursor-not-allowed rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3.5 py-2.5 font-mono text-sm text-slate-500 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-500"
                                />
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div className={`grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 ${!isSuperAdmin ? 'sm:grid-cols-2' : ''}`}>
                            {!isSuperAdmin && (
                                <div>
                                    <label className={labelClass}>
                                        {isAdmin ? 'Office / Unit' : 'Department'}
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={profileForm.department}
                                        onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, department: e.target.value })}
                                        placeholder={isAdmin ? 'Enter your office or unit' : 'Enter your department'}
                                        className={inputClass}
                                    />
                                </div>
                            )}

                            <div>
                                <label className={labelClass}>Phone number</label>
                                <input
                                    type="tel"
                                    value={profileForm.phone}
                                    onChange={(e) => setProfileForm({ ...profileForm, phone: e.target.value })}
                                    placeholder="Enter your phone number"
                                    className={inputClass}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
                            <Button type="submit" loading={savingProfile} disabled={savingProfile}>
                                Save changes
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </Card>
            </motion.div>

            {/* ───── PASSWORD ───── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.12 }}
            >
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40">
                            <Lock className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                Change password
                            </h2>
                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                At least 8 characters
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <form onSubmit={changePassword=REDACTED
                        <PasswordField
                            label="Current password"
                            value={passwordForm.currentPassword}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                                setPasswordForm({ ...passwordForm, currentPassword: e.target.value })
                            }
                            placeholder="Enter your current password"
                            autoComplete="current-password"
                        />
                        <PasswordField
                            label="New password"
                            value={passwordForm.newPassword}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                                setPasswordForm({ ...passwordForm, newPassword: e.target.value })
                            }
                            placeholder="At least 8 characters"
                            autoComplete="new-password"
                        />
                        <PasswordField
                            label="Confirm new password"
                            value={passwordForm.confirmPassword}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                                setPasswordForm({ ...passwordForm, confirmPassword: e.target.value })
                            }
                            placeholder="Re-enter new password"
                            autoComplete="new-password"
                        />

                        {passwordForm.confirmPassword && (
                            <div>
                                {password=REDACTED
                                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-emerald-600">
                                        <Check className="h-3 w-3" /> Passwords match
                                    </p>
                                ) : (
                                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-rose-600">
                                        <AlertCircle className="h-3 w-3" /> Passwords don't match
                                    </p>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
                            <Button type="submit" loading={savingPassword=REDACTED
                                Update password
                            </Button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </Card>
            </motion.div>

            {/* ───── EMAIL CHANGE MODAL ───── */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {showEmailModal && (
                    <ChangeEmailModal
                        currentEmail={display?.email}
                        onClose={() => setShowEmailModal(false)}
                        onSuccess={handleEmailChanged}
                    />
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/Register.jsx =====
import { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    ArrowRight, Loader2, AlertCircle, BookOpen, Sparkles, Eye, EyeOff,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api/axios'
import { useAuthStore } from '../store/authStore'
import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'
import { ease } from '../lib/motion'

const TABS = [
    { id: 'STUDENT', label: 'Continuing student', icon: BookOpen },
    { id: 'FRESHER', label: 'Fresher', icon: Sparkles },
]

export default function Register() {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { register, loading } = useAuthStore()
    const { push } = useToastStore()

    const [accountType, setAccountType] = useState('STUDENT')
    const [universities, setUniversities] = useState([])
    const [loadingUnis, setLoadingUnis] = useState(true)
    const [showPassword=REDACTED
    const [showConfirm, setShowConfirm] = useState(false)

    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        email: '',
        password: '',
        confirmPassword: '',
        fullName: '',
        universityId: '',
        department: '',
        phone: '',
        matricNumber: '',
        jambRegNumber: '',
        admissionRef: '',
        applicationNumber: '',
    })

    const setField = (f) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [f]: e.target.value })

    useEffect(() => {
        let cancelled = false
        api.get('/api/auth/universities')
            .then((res) => { if (!cancelled) setUniversities(res.data?.data || []) })
            .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) push({ type: 'error', message: 'Could not load universities' }) })
            .finally(() => { if (!cancelled) setLoadingUnis(false) })
        return () => { cancelled = true }
    }, [push])

    const selectedUni = useMemo(
        () => universities.find((u) => String(u.id) === String(form.universityId)) || null,
        [universities, form.universityId]
    )

    const fresherEnabled = selectedUni?.fresherRegistrationEnabled !== false

    useEffect(() => {
        if (accountType === 'FRESHER' && selectedUni && !fresherEnabled) setAccountType('STUDENT')
    }, [accountType, selectedUni, fresherEnabled])

    const submit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (form.password.length < 8) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Password must be at least 8 characters' })
            return
        }
        if (form.password=REDACTED
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Passwords do not match' })
            return
        }
        if (!form.universityId) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Please select your university' })
            return
        }

        const payload = {
            email: form.email.trim(),
            password: form.password,
            fullName: form.fullName.trim(),
            universityId: Number(form.universityId),
            department: form.department.trim() || null,
            phone: form.phone.trim() || null,
            accountType,
        }

        if (accountType === 'STUDENT') {
            payload.matricNumber = form.matricNumber.trim()
        } else {
            if (selectedUni?.requireJambReg !== false) payload.jambRegNumber = form.jambRegNumber.trim() || null
            if (selectedUni?.requireAdmissionRef) payload.admissionRef = form.admissionRef.trim() || null
            if (selectedUni?.requireApplicationNumber) payload.applicationNumber = form.applicationNumber.trim() || null
        }

        const res = await register(payload)
        if (res.success) {
            push({ type: 'success', message: accountType === 'FRESHER' ? 'Registered as fresher. Check your email for the code.' : 'Account created. Check your email for the code.' })
            navigate('/verify-email', { state: { email: form.email.trim(), devCode: res.devVerificationCode } })
        } else {
            push({ type: 'error', message: res.error })
        }
    }

    const inputClass =
        'w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3.5 py-2.5 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 transition-colors focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500 disabled:opacity-50'

    const labelClass = 'mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300'

    return (
        <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-950">
            {/* Left brand panel */}
            <div className="relative hidden w-1/2 overflow-hidden bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-600 via-brand-700 to-slate-900 lg:flex lg:flex-col lg:justify-between lg:p-12">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white/95 text-sm font-bold text-brand-700">UQ</div>
                    <span className="text-lg font-semibold text-white">UniQueue</span>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <h1 className="text-3xl font-semibold leading-tight text-white">
                        Join your university's digital queue system.
                    </h1>
                    <p className="mt-4 max-w-md text-sm text-slate-200">
                        Continuing students sign in with their matric. Freshers register with
                        their JAMB or admission reference.
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-10 space-y-3">
                        {['Verified students get instant queue access', 'Freshers get a personal dashboard from day one', 'No more standing in line for hours'].map((item) => (
                            <div key={item} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                <div className="mt-0.5 flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/15">
                                    <svg className="h-3 w-3 text-white" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth={3}>
                                        <path strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" d="M5 13l4 4L19 7" />
                                    </svg>
                                </div>
                                <span className="text-sm text-slate-200">{item}</span>
                            </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">© {new Date().getFullYear()} UniQueue. All rights reserved.</div>
            </div>

            {/* Right form */}
            <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center px-4 py-10 sm:px-6 lg:px-10">
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                    transition={{ duration: 0.5, ease }}
                    className="w-full max-w-md"
                >
                    <div className="mb-6 lg:hidden">

                            <span className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">UniQueue</span>
                        </Link>
                    </div>

                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Create your account
                    </h2>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Already registered?{' '}
                        <Link to="/login" className="font-medium text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700">Sign in</Link>
                    </p>

                    {/* Tabs */}
                    <div className="mt-6 flex rounded-lg bg-slate-100 p-0.5 dark:bg-slate-800">
                        {TABS.map((t) => {
                            const Icon = t.icon
                            const disabled = t.id === 'FRESHER' && selectedUni && !fresherEnabled
                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={t.id}
                                    type="button"
                                    disabled={disabled}
                                    onClick={() => setAccountType(t.id)}
                                    className={`flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium transition-colors ${
                                        accountType === t.id
                                            ? 'bg-white text-slate-900 shadow-sm dark:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-100'
                                            : disabled
                                                ? 'cursor-not-allowed text-slate-300'
                                                : 'text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-100'
                                    }`}
                                >
                                    <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                                    {t.label}
                                </button>
                            )
                        })}
                    </div>

                    {accountType === 'FRESHER' && (
                        <div className="mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 p-3 text-xs text-sky-900 dark:border-sky-900 dark:bg-sky-950/40 dark:text-sky-300">

                                onChange={setField('email')}

                            />

                                    required
                                    className={inputClass}
                                />
                                {selectedUni?.matricDescription && (
                                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                        Format: <span className="font-mono">{selectedUni.matricDescription}</span>
                                    </p>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        )}

                        {accountType === 'FRESHER' && selectedUni && (
                            <>
                                {selectedUni.requireJambReg !== false && (
                                    <div>
                                        <label className={labelClass}>JAMB registration number</label>
                                        <input
                                            type="text"
                                            value={form.jambRegNumber}
                                            onChange={setField('jambRegNumber')}
                                            placeholder="Enter your JAMB registration number"
                                            required
                                            className={inputClass}
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                                {selectedUni.requireAdmissionRef && (
                                    <div>
                                        <label className={labelClass}>Admission reference</label>
                                        <input
                                            type="text"
                                            value={form.admissionRef}
                                            onChange={setField('admissionRef')}
                                            placeholder="Enter admission reference"
                                            className={inputClass}
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                                {selectedUni.requireApplicationNumber && (
                                    <div>
                                        <label className={labelClass}>Application number</label>
                                        <input
                                            type="text"
                                            value={form.applicationNumber}
                                            onChange={setField('applicationNumber')}
                                            placeholder="Enter application number"
                                            className={inputClass}
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                )}
                            </>
                        )}

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                            <div>
                                <label className={labelClass}>Department (optional)</label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={form.department}
                                    onChange={setField('department')}
                                    placeholder="Enter department"
                                    className={inputClass}
                                />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className={labelClass}>Phone (optional)</label>
                                <input
                                    type="tel"
                                    value={form.phone}
                                    onChange={setField('phone')}
                                    placeholder="Enter phone number"
                                    className={inputClass}
                                />
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                            <div>
                                <label className={labelClass}>Password</label>
                                <div className="relative">
                                    <input
                                        type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                                        value={form.password}
                                        onChange={setField('password')}
                                        placeholder="At least 8 characters"
                                        required
                                        className={`${inputClass} pr-10`}
                                    />
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => setShowPassword=REDACTED
                                        className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 flex items-center pr-3 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                                        aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                                    >
                                        {showPassword=REDACTED
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <label className={labelClass}>Confirm password</label>
                                <div className="relative">
                                    <input
                                        type={showConfirm ? 'text' : 'password'}
                                        value={form.confirmPassword}
                                        onChange={setField('confirmPassword')}
                                        placeholder="Re-enter password"
                                        required
                                        className={`${inputClass} pr-10`}
                                    />
                                    <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => setShowConfirm((v) => !v)}
                                        className="absolute inset-y-0 right-0 flex items-center pr-3 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:text-slate-600 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                                        aria-label={showConfirm ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                                    >
                                        {showConfirm ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />}
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <button
                            type="submit"
                            disabled={loading}
                            className="!mt-5 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:opacity-60"
                        >
                            {loading ? (
                                <>
                                    <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                                    Creating account…
                                </>
                            ) : (
                                <>
                                    {accountType === 'FRESHER' ? 'Create fresher account' : 'Create account'}
                                    <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </>
                            )}
                        </button>
                    </form>

                    {accountType === 'FRESHER' && (
                        <p className="mt-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            After you receive your matric, you'll be able to upgrade to full
                            student access from your profile.
                        </p>
                    )}
                </motion.div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/ResetPassword=REDACTED
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { Lock, ArrowLeft, CheckCircle2, KeyRound } from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api/axios'
import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'

export default function ResetPassword() {
    const [params] = useSearchParams()
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { push } = useToastStore()

    const [token=REDACTED
    const [newPassword=REDACTED
    const [confirmPassword=REDACTED
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false)
    const [error, setError] = useState('')

    useEffect(() => {
        const t = params.get('token')
        if (t) setToken(t)
    }, [params])

    const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setError('')

        if (!token) {
            setError('Reset token is required')
            return
        }
        if (newPassword=REDACTED
            setError('Passwords do not match')
            return
        }
        if (newPassword.length < 8) {
            setError('Password must be at least 8 characters')
            return
        }

        setLoading(true)
        try {
            await api.post('/api/auth/reset-password', { token, newPassword })
            setSuccess(true)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Password reset. You can now sign in.' })
            setTimeout(() => navigate('/login'), 1500)
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to reset password')
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }

    return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 py-12">
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-md px-4">
                <Link
                    to="/login"
                    className="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700"
                >
                    <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Back to sign in
                </Link>

                <div className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 shadow-sm">
                    {success ? (
                        <div className="text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-100">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="h-6 w-6 text-emerald-600" />
                            </div>
                            <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                Password updated
                            </h1>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                Redirecting you to sign in…
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <div className="mb-4 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-indigo-50">
                                <KeyRound className="h-6 w-6 text-indigo-600" />
                            </div>
                            <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                Set a new password
                            </h1>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                At least 8 characters.
                            </p>

                            {error && (
                                <div className="mt-4 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-rose-700">
                                    {error}
                                </div>
                            )}

                            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
                                {/* Token field — always visible for debugging */}
                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                        Reset token=REDACTED
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={token}
                                        onChange={(e) => setToken(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="Paste your reset token"
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
                                        required
                                    />
                                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                        Get this from the Forgot Password page.
                                    </p>
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                        New password
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="password"
                                        value={newPassword}
                                        onChange={(e) => setNewPassword(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="At least 8 characters"
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                        Confirm new password
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="password"
                                        value={confirmPassword}
                                        onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder="Re-enter password"
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-indigo-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500/20"
                                        required
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <button
                                    type="submit"
                                    disabled={loading}
                                    className="w-full rounded-lg bg-indigo-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-indigo-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                                >
                                    {loading ? 'Resetting…' : 'Reset password'}
                                </button>
                            </form>
                        </>
                    )}
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/staff/ActivityPage.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Ticket, CheckCircle2, TrendingUp, Award, Clock, Activity as ActivityIcon,
    CalendarCheck, XCircle, Building2, AlertCircle, Zap,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import { SkeletonStats, SkeletonRow } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    WAITING: 'warning',
    WAITLISTED: 'info',
    CALLED: 'indigo',
    SERVING: 'indigo',
    COMPLETED: 'success',
    NO_SHOW: 'danger',
    CANCELLED: 'default',
    TRANSFERRED: 'default',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    WAITING: 'Waiting',
    WAITLISTED: 'Waitlisted',
    CALLED: 'Called',
    SERVING: 'Serving',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    NO_SHOW: 'No-show',
    CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
    TRANSFERRED: 'Transferred',
}

const TILE_DELAYS = [0, 0.05, 0.1, 0.15, 0.2]

function KpiTile({ label, value, hint, icon: Icon, accent, valueColor, delay }) {
    return (
        <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.4, delay }}
        >
            <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            {label}
                        </div>
                        <div className={`mt-2 truncate text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight ${valueColor}`}>
                            {value}
                        </div>
                        {hint && (
                            <div className="mt-1 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                {hint}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </div>
                    {Icon && (
                        <div className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${accent}`}>
                            <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
            </Card>
        </motion.div>
    )
}

function relativeTime(dt) {
    if (!dt) return ''
    const diff = Date.now() - new Date(dt).getTime()
    const mins = Math.floor(diff / 60000)
    if (mins < 1) return 'just now'
    if (mins < 60) return `${mins} min ago`
    const hours = Math.floor(mins / 60)
    if (hours < 24) return `${hours}h ago`
    const days = Math.floor(hours / 24)
    return `${days}d ago`
}

export default function ActivityPage() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)
    const [apptStats, setApptStats] = useState(null)
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [recentTickets, setRecentTickets] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [activityLoading, setActivityLoading] = useState(true)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [s, a, q] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/staff/stats').catch(() => ({ data: { data: null } })),
                api.get('/api/staff/appointments/stats').catch(() => ({ data: { data: null } })),
                api.get('/api/staff/queues').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
            ])
            setStats(s.data?.data || null)
            setApptStats(a.data?.data || null)

            const staffQueues = q.data?.data || []
            setQueues(staffQueues)

            if (staffQueues.length > 0) {
                const ticketsByQueue = await Promise.all(
                    staffQueues.map((qu) =>
                        api
                            .get(`/api/staff/queues/${qu.id}/tickets`)
                            .then((res) =>
                                (res.data?.data || []).map((t) => ({ ...t, queueName: qu.name }))
                            )
                            .catch(() => [])
                    )
                )
                const all = ticketsByQueue.flat()

                // Sort by most recent activity
                const sorted = all.sort((a, b) => {
                    const at = a.completedAt || a.calledAt || a.joinedAt
                        ? new Date(a.completedAt || a.calledAt || a.joinedAt).getTime() : 0
                    const bt = b.completedAt || b.calledAt || b.joinedAt
                        ? new Date(b.completedAt || b.calledAt || b.joinedAt).getTime() : 0
                    return bt - at
                })

                setRecentTickets(sorted.slice(0, 20))
            }
        } catch (err) {
            push({
                type: 'error',
                message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load activity',
            })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
            setActivityLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 10000, slowMs: 30000 })

    const derived = useMemo(() => {
        const servedToday = stats?.servedToday ?? 0
        const servedThisWeek = stats?.servedThisWeek ?? 0
        const servedAllTime = stats?.servedAllTime ?? 0
        const avgService = stats?.avgServiceMinutes ?? 0
        const apptToday = apptStats?.today ?? 0

        const weeklyPace =
            servedThisWeek > 0 ? Math.round((servedThisWeek / 7) * 10) / 10 : 0

        const completed = recentTickets.filter((t) => t.status === 'COMPLETED').length
        const noShows = recentTickets.filter((t) => t.status === 'NO_SHOW').length
        const total = recentTickets.length
        const completionRate = total > 0 ? Math.round((completed / total) * 100) : 0
        const noShowRate = total > 0 ? Math.round((noShows / total) * 100) : 0

        return {
            servedToday, servedThisWeek, servedAllTime, avgService, apptToday,
            weeklyPace, completionRate, noShowRate,
        }
    }, [stats, apptStats, recentTickets])

    const queueBreakdown = useMemo(() => {
        return queues
            .map((q) => ({
                id: q.id,
                name: q.name,
                office: q.officeName || q.department || 'General',
                waiting: q.waitingCount ?? 0,
                currentlyServing: q.currentlyServing,
                estimatedMinutes: q.estimatedMinutes ?? 0,
            }))
            .sort((a, b) => b.waiting - a.waiting)
    }, [queues])

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="h-8 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
                <SkeletonStats count={5} />
                <SkeletonStats count={3} />
                <Card padded={false}>
                    {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <SkeletonRow key={i} />)}
                </Card>
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div>
            <div className="mb-5">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    Activity
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Your complete service history, productivity, and queue activity.
                </p>
            </div>

            {/* KPI Tiles */}
            <div className="mb-7 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 lg:grid-cols-5">
                <KpiTile
                    label="Served Today"
                    value={derived.servedToday}
                    hint={derived.servedToday === 1 ? 'ticket' : 'tickets'}
                    icon={CheckCircle2}
                    accent="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400"
                    valueColor="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"
                    delay={TILE_DELAYS[0]}
                />
                <KpiTile
                    label="This Week"
                    value={derived.servedThisWeek}
                    hint={`${derived.weeklyPace}/day avg`}
                    icon={TrendingUp}
                    accent="bg-sky-50 text-sky-600 dark:bg-sky-950/40 dark:text-sky-400"
                    valueColor="text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400"
                    delay={TILE_DELAYS[1]}
                />
                <KpiTile
                    label="All Time"
                    value={derived.servedAllTime}
                    hint="Total completed"
                    icon={Award}
                    accent="bg-violet-50 text-violet-600 dark:bg-violet-950/40 dark:text-violet-400"
                    valueColor="text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400"
                    delay={TILE_DELAYS[2]}
                />
                <KpiTile
                    label="Avg Service"
                    value={`${derived.avgService}m`}
                    hint="Per ticket"
                    icon={Clock}
                    accent="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400"
                    valueColor="text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400"
                    delay={TILE_DELAYS[3]}
                />
                <KpiTile
                    label="Appointments"
                    value={derived.apptToday}
                    hint="Today"
                    icon={CalendarCheck}
                    accent="bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400"
                    valueColor="text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400"
                    delay={TILE_DELAYS[4]}
                />
            </div>

            {/* Performance cards */}
            <div className="mb-7 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/40">
                            <Zap className="h-5 w-5 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Weekly Pace
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400">
                                {derived.weeklyPace}
                                <span className="ml-1 text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">tickets/day</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Your rolling 7-day average. Aim to keep it steady across the week.
                    </p>
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40">
                            <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Completion Rate
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                                {derived.completionRate}%
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Of your recent tickets, the share you served to completion.
                    </p>
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-rose-50 dark:bg-rose-950/40">
                            <AlertCircle className="h-5 w-5 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" />
                        </div>
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                No-show Rate
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400">
                                {derived.noShowRate}%
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Students who were called but didn't show up. Lower is better.
                    </p>
                </Card>
            </div>

            {/* Two-column: Recent Activity + Queue Breakdown */}
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_400px]">
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <ActivityIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                Recent Activity
                            </h3>
                        </div>
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400">
              {recentTickets.length} {recentTickets.length === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries'}
            </span>
                    </div>

                    {activityLoading ? (
                        <div>
                            {Array.from({ length: 5 }).map((_, i) => <SkeletonRow key={i} />)}
                        </div>
                    ) : recentTickets.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <Ticket className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                No activity yet
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Tickets from your queues will appear here.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
                            {recentTickets.map((t) => {
                                const ts = t.completedAt || t.calledAt || t.joinedAt
                                const isCompleted = t.status === 'COMPLETED'
                                const isNoShow = t.status === 'NO_SHOW'
                                const isCancelled = t.status === 'CANCELLED'
                                return (
                                    <li
                                        key={t.id}
                                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-4 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                                    >
                                        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start gap-3">
                                            <div
                                                className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${
                                                    isCompleted
                                                        ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400'
                                                        : isNoShow
                                                            ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400'
                                                            : isCancelled
                                                                ? 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400'
                                                                : 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 dark:bg-brand-950/40 dark:text-brand-400'
                                                }`}
                                            >
                                                {isCompleted ? (
                                                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                ) : isNoShow ? (
                                                    <XCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                ) : (
                                                    <Ticket className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                )}
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {t.ticketNumber}
                          </span>
                                                    <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[t.status] || 'default'}>
                                                        {STATUS_LABELS[t.status] || t.status}
                                                    </Badge>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    {t.queueName}
                                                    {t.userFullName ? ` · ${t.userFullName}` : ''}
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        {ts && (
                                            <div className="shrink-0 text-right text-[10px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                                                {relativeTime(ts)}
                                            </div>
                                        )}
                                    </li>
                                )
                            })}
                        </ul>
                    )}
                </Card>

                <Card padded={false}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-5 py-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <Building2 className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                My Queues
                            </h3>
                        </div>
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400">
              {queueBreakdown.length}
            </span>
                    </div>

                    {queueBreakdown.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="px-5 py-12 text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <Building2 className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                No queues assigned
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
                            {queueBreakdown.map((q) => (
                                <li key={q.id} className="px-5 py-3.5">
                                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                            <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                {q.name}
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                {q.office}
                                            </div>
                                            {q.currentlyServing && (
                                                <div className="mt-1.5 text-xs">
                          <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            Now serving:{' '}
                          </span>
                                                    <span className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                            {q.currentlyServing}
                          </span>
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-3">
                                            <div className="text-right">
                                                <div className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                                    Waiting
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="text-base font-bold text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400">
                                                    {q.waiting}
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="text-right">
                                                <div className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                                    ~Wait
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="text-base font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                                                    {q.estimatedMinutes}m
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    )}
                </Card>
            </div>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/staff/AppointmentsList.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Calendar, CalendarCheck, Clock, User, Mail, CheckCircle2, XCircle,
    Search, Filter, Hash, AlertCircle, ChevronRight,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import CopyButton from '../../components/ui/CopyButton'
import AppointmentDetailModal from '../../components/staff/AppointmentDetailModal'
import { SkeletonRow } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    BOOKED: 'indigo',
    CONFIRMED: 'success',
    COMPLETED: 'success',
    CANCELLED: 'default',
    NO_SHOW: 'danger',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    BOOKED: 'Booked',
    CONFIRMED: 'Confirmed',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
    NO_SHOW: 'No-show',
}

const TABS = [
    { value: 'TODAY', label: 'Today' },
    { value: 'UPCOMING', label: 'Upcoming' },
    { value: 'PAST', label: 'Past' },
    { value: 'ALL', label: 'All' },
]

function formatDateTime(dt) {
    if (!dt) return '—'
    try {
        return new Date(dt).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
            weekday: 'short',
            day: '2-digit',
            month: 'short',
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
            hour12: true,
        })
    } catch {
        return '—'
    }
}

function isSameDay(a, b) {
    return (
        a.getFullYear() === b.getFullYear() &&
        a.getMonth() === b.getMonth() &&
        a.getDate() === b.getDate()
    )
}

function generateReference(appt) {
    if (!appt) return '—'
    const d = appt.bookedAt ? new Date(appt.bookedAt) : new Date()
    const yyyy = d.getFullYear()
    const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
    const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
    const idPart = String(appt.id || 0).padStart(4, '0')
