                                    className="mt-5 inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-emerald-600 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-60"
                                >
                                    <Ticket className="h-4 w-4" />
                                    {acting === 'next' ? 'Calling…' : 'Call Next Ticket'}
                                </button>
                            )}
                        </motion.div>
                    )}
                </AnimatePresence>
            </Card>

            {/* ═══ Waiting Tickets ═══ */}
            <Card padded={false}>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                    <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Waiting Tickets
                    </h3>
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400">
            {waiting.length}
          </span>
                </div>

                {ticketsLoading ? (
                    <div>
                        {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
                            <SkeletonRow key={i} />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                ) : waiting.length === 0 ? (
                    <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center">
                        <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                            <ListOrdered className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            No waiting tickets
                        </p>
                        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            New tickets will appear here automatically.
                        </p>
                    </div>
                ) : (
                    <motion.ul
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.04)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800"
                    >
                        {waiting.map((t, i) => (
                            <motion.li
                                key={t.id}
                                variants={staggerItem}
                                layout
                                className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-4 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                            >
                                <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-4">
                                    {/* Position */}
                                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-sm font-bold text-brand-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-brand-400">
                                        {t.position ?? i + 1}
                                    </div>

                                    {/* Ticket info */}
                                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="font-mono text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        {t.ticketNumber}
                      </span>
                                            <CopyButton text={t.ticketNumber} title="Copy ticket ID" />
                                            <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[t.status] || 'default'}>
                                                {STATUS_LABELS[t.status] || t.status}
                                            </Badge>
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            {t.userFullName}
                                            {t.matricNumber ? ` · ${t.matricNumber}` : ''}
                                        </div>
                                        {t.estimatedMinutesRemaining != null && (
                                            <div className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                                                <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                ~{t.estimatedMinutesRemaining} min remaining
                                            </div>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                {/* No-show button */}
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => noShow(t.id)}
                                    disabled={acting === `noshow-${t.id}`}
                                    className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-rose-700 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-100 disabled:opacity-60 dark:border-rose-900 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-300 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/60"
                                >
                                    <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                    {acting === `noshow-${t.id}` ? 'Marking…' : 'No-show'}
                                </button>
                            </motion.li>
                        ))}
                    </motion.ul>
                )}
            </Card>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/staff/SettingsPage.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Bell, Moon, Sun, Volume2, Shield, ArrowRight, Save, RefreshCw,
} from 'lucide-react'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'

const STORAGE_KEY = 'uniqueue_staff_prefs'

const DEFAULTS = {
    notifyNewTicket: true,
    notifySound: false,
    autoRefresh: true,
    compactMode: false,
}

function Toggle({ label, description, checked, onChange }) {
    return (
        <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start justify-between gap-4 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-4 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{label}</div>
                {description && (
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{description}</p>
                )}
            </div>
            <div className="relative shrink-0">
                <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={checked}
                    onChange={onChange}
                    className="peer sr-only"
                />
                <div className="h-6 w-11 rounded-full bg-slate-300 transition-colors peer-checked:bg-brand-600 dark:bg-slate-700" />
                <div className="absolute left-0.5 top-0.5 h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-white shadow-sm transition-transform peer-checked:translate-x-5" />
            </div>
        </label>
    )
}

export default function SettingsPage() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [prefs, setPrefs] = useState(DEFAULTS)

    // Load from localStorage
    useEffect(() => {
        try {
            const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
            if (raw) setPrefs({ ...DEFAULTS, ...JSON.parse(raw) })
        } catch {}
    }, [])

    const set = (k, v) => setPrefs((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: v }))

    const save = () => {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefs))
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Preferences saved' })
        } catch {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Failed to save preferences' })
        }
    }

    const reset = () => {
        setPrefs(DEFAULTS)
        try { localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY) } catch {}
        push({ type: 'info', message: 'Preferences reset' })
    }

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Settings"
                description="Personal preferences for your staff workspace."
            />

            <div className="space-y-6">
                {/* Notifications */}
                <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}>
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50 dark:bg-brand-950/40">
                                <Bell className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Notifications
                                </h2>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    How you want to be alerted when new students join your queues.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="space-y-3">
                            <Toggle
                                label="Notify me about new tickets"
                                description="Get a toast + bell indicator when someone joins a queue you serve."
                                checked={prefs.notifyNewTicket}
                                onChange={(e) => set('notifyNewTicket', e.target.checked)}
                            />
                            <Toggle
                                label="Play sound on new ticket"
                                description="Chime when a student joins. Useful if you're away from the screen."
                                checked={prefs.notifySound}
                                onChange={(e) => set('notifySound', e.target.checked)}
                            />
                            <Toggle
                                label="Auto-refresh queue list"
                                description="Keep the waiting list up to date without manual refresh."
                                checked={prefs.autoRefresh}
                                onChange={(e) => set('autoRefresh', e.target.checked)}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>

                {/* Display */}
                <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.05 }}>
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-sky-50 dark:bg-sky-950/40">
                                <Moon className="h-5 w-5 text-sky-600 dark:text-sky-400" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Display
                                </h2>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    How the workspace looks and behaves.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div className="space-y-3">
                            <Toggle
                                label="Compact mode"
                                description="Reduce spacing so more items fit on screen."
                                checked={prefs.compactMode}
                                onChange={(e) => set('compactMode', e.target.checked)}
                            />
                        </div>

                        <div className="mt-5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
                            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                <Sun className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-500" />
                                <div className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    <p className="font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">Dark mode</p>
                                    <p className="mt-0.5">Toggle using the sun/moon icon in the top-right of the navbar.</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>

                {/* Account */}
                <motion.div initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }} animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }} transition={{ delay: 0.1 }}>
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40">
                                <Shield className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Account
                                </h2>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    Manage your profile and login.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <Link
                            to="/staff/profile"
                            className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-slate-200 p-4 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50"
                        >
                            <div>
                                <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Profile & password
                                </div>
                                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    Update your name, phone, and password
                                </p>
                            </div>
                            <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400" />
                        </Link>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>

                {/* Actions */}
                <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
                    <Button variant="secondary" icon={RefreshCw} onClick={reset}>
                        Reset
                    </Button>
                    <Button icon={Save} onClick={save}>
                        Save preferences
                    </Button>
                </div>
            </div>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/staff/StaffDocuments.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    FileText, CheckCircle2, XCircle, Clock, Filter, Search, Eye,
    User, Hash, Download, AlertCircle,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import { SkeletonRow } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import DocumentReviewModal from '../../components/staff/DocumentReviewModal'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    PENDING: 'warning',
    APPROVED: 'success',
    REJECTED: 'danger',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    PENDING: 'Pending',
    APPROVED: 'Approved',
    REJECTED: 'Rejected',
}

const FILTERS = [
    { value: 'PENDING', label: 'Pending' },
    { value: 'APPROVED', label: 'Approved' },
    { value: 'REJECTED', label: 'Rejected' },
]

function formatSize(bytes) {
    if (!bytes) return '—'
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

function formatDateTime(dt) {
    if (!dt) return '—'
    try {
        return new Date(dt).toLocaleString([], {
            day: 'numeric',
            month: 'short',
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
        })
    } catch {
        return '—'
    }
}

export default function StaffDocuments() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [docs, setDocs] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [filter, setFilter] = useState('PENDING')
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
    const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/staff/documents', {
                params: { status: filter },
            })
            setDocs(res.data?.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load documents' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [filter, push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 10000, slowMs: 30000 })

    const filtered = useMemo(() => {
        if (!search.trim()) return docs
        const s = search.trim().toLowerCase()
        return docs.filter(
            (d) =>
                d.originalFilename?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                d.uploadedByName?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                d.ticketNumber?.toLowerCase().includes(s)
        )
    }, [docs, search])

    const download = async (doc) => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get(`/api/documents/${doc.id}/download`, {
                responseType: 'blob',
            })
            const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(res.data)
            const a = document.createElement('a')
            a.href = blobUrl
            a.download = doc.originalFilename || 'document'
            document.body.appendChild(a)
            a.click()
            a.remove()
            URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
        } catch {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Download failed' })
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div>
                <div className="mb-5">
                    <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Documents
                    </h2>
                </div>
                <Card padded={false}>
                    {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => <SkeletonRow key={i} />)}
                </Card>
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div>
            <div className="mb-5">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    Documents
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Review documents students have uploaded.
                </p>
            </div>

            {/* Filter chips */}
            <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {FILTERS.map((f) => {
                    const isActive = filter === f.value
                    return (
                        <button
                            key={f.value}
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => setFilter(f.value)}
                            className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                                isActive
                                    ? 'bg-slate-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-slate-900'
                                    : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:bg-slate-700'
                            }`}
                        >
                            {f.label}
                        </button>
                    )
                })}
            </div>

            {/* Search */}
            {docs.length > 0 && (
                <div className="mb-5">
                    <div className="relative">
                        <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                        <input
                            type="text"
                            value={search}
                            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                            placeholder="Search by filename, student, or ticket"
                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-2.5 pl-10 pr-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                        />
                    </div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* List */}
            {docs.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={FileText}
                    title={`No ${filter.toLowerCase()} documents`}
                    description={
                        filter === 'PENDING'
                            ? "You're all caught up — nothing to review right now."
                            : `No ${filter.toLowerCase()} documents yet.`
                    }
                />
            ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Filter}
                    title="No matches"
                    description="Try a different search term."
                    action={<Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setSearch('')}>Clear search</Button>}
                />
            ) : (
                <div className="space-y-3">
                    {filtered.map((d, idx) => {
                        const isPending = d.status === 'PENDING'
                        return (
                            <motion.div
                                key={d.id}
                                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                                transition={{ delay: idx * 0.02 }}
                            >
                                <Card>
                                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
                                        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start gap-4">
                                            <div className={`flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${
                                                d.status === 'APPROVED'
                                                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400'
                                                    : d.status === 'REJECTED'
                                                        ? 'bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-400'
                                                        : 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400'
                                            }`}>
                                                {d.status === 'APPROVED' ? (
                                                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5" />
                                                ) : d.status === 'REJECTED' ? (
                                                    <XCircle className="h-5 w-5" />
                                                ) : (
                                                    <Clock className="h-5 w-5" />
                                                )}
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                                    <h3 className="truncate text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                        {d.originalFilename}
                                                    </h3>
                                                    <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[d.status] || 'default'}>
                                                        {STATUS_LABELS[d.status] || d.status}
                                                    </Badge>
                                                </div>

                                                <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    <span>{d.type}</span>
                                                    <span>·</span>
                                                    <span>{formatSize(d.sizeBytes)}</span>
                                                </div>

                                                <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <User className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                              {d.uploadedByName || 'Unknown'}
                          </span>
                                                    {d.ticketNumber && (
                                                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono">
                              <Hash className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                                                            {d.ticketNumber}
                            </span>
                                                    )}
                                                    <span>{formatDateTime(d.createdAt)}</span>
                                                </div>

                                                {d.status === 'REJECTED' && d.reviewNotes && (
                                                    <div className="mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-3 text-xs text-rose-700 dark:border-rose-900 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-300">
                                                        <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                                                        <div>
                                                            <span className="font-semibold">Reason: </span>
                                                            {d.reviewNotes}
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1">
                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => download(d)}
                                                className="rounded-lg p-2 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                                                title="Download"
                                            >
                                                <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                                            </button>
                                            {isPending && (
                                                <Button
                                                    size="sm"
                                                    icon={Eye}
                                                    onClick={() => setSelected(d)}
                                                >
                                                    Review
                                                </Button>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </Card>
                            </motion.div>
                        )
                    })}
                </div>
            )}

            <AnimatePresence>
                {selected && (
                    <DocumentReviewModal
                        document={selected}
                        onClose={() => setSelected(null)}
                        onReviewed={load}
                    />
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/staff/StaffHome.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    ListOrdered, CalendarCheck, FileText, ArrowRight, Ticket,
    Clock, ChevronRight,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import StaffStatsCards from '../../components/staff/StaffStatsCards'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    WAITING: 'warning',
    WAITLISTED: 'info',
    CALLED: 'indigo',
    SERVING: 'indigo',
    COMPLETED: 'success',
    NO_SHOW: 'danger',
    CANCELLED: 'default',
    TRANSFERRED: 'default',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    WAITING: 'Waiting',
    WAITLISTED: 'Waitlisted',
    CALLED: 'Called',
    SERVING: 'Serving',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    NO_SHOW: 'No-show',
    CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
    TRANSFERRED: 'Transferred',
}

const QUICK_ACTIONS = [
    {
        to: '/staff/serving',
        label: 'Manage Queues',
        description: 'Call tickets and serve students',
        icon: ListOrdered,
        accent: 'bg-brand-50 text-brand-600 dark:bg-brand-950/40 dark:text-brand-400',

    {
        to: '/staff/appointments',
        label: 'View Appointments',
        description: 'See appointments booked with you',
        icon: CalendarCheck,
        accent: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400',
    },
    {
        to: '/staff/documents',
        label: 'Process Documents',
        description: 'Review pending document requests',
        icon: FileText,
        accent: 'bg-violet-50 text-violet-600 dark:bg-violet-950/40 dark:text-violet-400',
    },
]

function formatTime(dt) {
    if (!dt) return null
    try {
        return new Date(dt).toLocaleString([], {
            day: 'numeric',
            month: 'short',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
        })
    } catch {
        return null
    }
}

export default function StaffHome() {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [recentTickets, setRecentTickets] = useState([])
    const [ticketsLoading, setTicketsLoading] = useState(true)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const qRes = await api.get('/api/staff/queues')
            const staffQueues = qRes.data?.data || []
            setQueues(staffQueues)

            if (staffQueues.length === 0) {
                setRecentTickets([])
                setTicketsLoading(false)
                return
            }

            // Fetch tickets from every queue in parallel
            const ticketsByQueue = await Promise.all(
                staffQueues.map((q) =>
                    api
                        .get(`/api/staff/queues/${q.id}/tickets`)
                        .then((res) => res.data?.data || [])
                        .catch(() => [])
                )
            )
            const all = ticketsByQueue.flat()

            // Sort by joinedAt desc, take the 5 most recent
            const sorted = all
                .sort((a, b) => {
                    const at = a.joinedAt ? new Date(a.joinedAt).getTime() : 0
                    const bt = b.joinedAt ? new Date(b.joinedAt).getTime() : 0
                    return bt - at
                })
                .slice(0, 5)

            setRecentTickets(sorted)
        } catch (err) {
            push({
                type: 'error',
                message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load data',
            })
        } finally {
            setTicketsLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 5000, slowMs: 15000 })

    return (
        <>
            <StaffStatsCards />

            {/* ─── 3 Quick Actions ─── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.1 }}
                className="mb-7 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3"
            >
                {QUICK_ACTIONS.map((a, i) => {
                    const Icon = a.icon
                    return (
                        <motion.div
                            key={a.to}
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                            transition={{ delay: 0.15 + i * 0.05 }}
                        >
                            <Link to={a.to} className="group block h-full">
                                <Card className="flex h-full items-center gap-4 transition-all hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md">
                                    <div className={`flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${a.accent}`}>
                                        <Icon className="h-6 w-6" />
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                            <h3 className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                {a.label}
                                            </h3>
                                            <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-slate-400 transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-0.5 group-hover:text-brand-600" />
                                        </div>
                                        <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            {a.description}
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                </Card>
                            </Link>
                        </motion.div>
                    )
                })}
            </motion.div>

            {/* ─── Recent Queue Tickets ─── */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.25 }}
            >
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                        <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            Recent Queue Tickets
                        </h2>
                        <button
                            onClick={() => navigate('/staff/serving')}
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                        >
                            View all
                            <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        </button>
                    </div>

                    {ticketsLoading ? (
                        <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
                            {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
                                <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-4 px-6 py-4">
                                    <div className="h-4 w-32 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
                                    <div className="h-4 w-48 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-200 dark:bg-slate-800" />
                                </div>
                            ))}
                        </div>
                    ) : recentTickets.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="px-6 py-12 text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <Ticket className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                No tickets yet
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Tickets from students will show up here.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
                            {recentTickets.map((t) => (
                                <li
                                    key={t.id}
                                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 px-6 py-4 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/50"
                                >
                                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                        <div className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                            {t.queueName || t.officeName || 'Service'}
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="mt-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      <span className="font-mono">
                        Ticket: {t.ticketNumber}
                      </span>
                                            {t.userFullName && (
                                                <span>· {t.userFullName}</span>
                                            )}
                                            {t.joinedAt && (
                                                <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                    {formatTime(t.joinedAt)}
                        </span>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[t.status] || 'default'}>
                                        {STATUS_LABELS[t.status] || t.status}
                                    </Badge>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    )}
                </Card>
            </motion.div>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/student/AppointmentsTab.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Calendar, Clock, Building2, MapPin, User, Copy, Check,
    CalendarCheck, AlertCircle, Info,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    BOOKED: 'info',
    CONFIRMED: 'success',
    COMPLETED: 'success',
    CANCELLED: 'default',
    NO_SHOW: 'danger',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    BOOKED: 'Awaiting meeting',
    CONFIRMED: 'Confirmed',
    COMPLETED: 'Completed',
    CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
    NO_SHOW: 'Missed',
}

function formatDate(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '—'
    try {
        return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
            weekday: 'short',
            day: '2-digit',
            month: 'short',
            year: 'numeric',
        })
    } catch {
        return '—'
    }
}

function formatDateTime(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '—'
    try {
        return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-GB', {
            weekday: 'short',
            day: '2-digit',
            month: 'short',
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
            hour12: true,
        })
    } catch {
        return '—'
    }
}

function formatTimeOnly(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '—'
    try {
        return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', {
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
            hour12: true,
        })
    } catch {
        return '—'
    }
}

function dateKey(iso) {
    if (!iso) return ''
    return String(iso).slice(0, 10)
}

function generateReference(appt) {
    if (!appt) return '—'
    const d = appt.bookedAt ? new Date(appt.bookedAt) : new Date()
    const yyyy = d.getFullYear()
    const mm = String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')
    const dd = String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
    const idPart = String(appt.id || 0).padStart(4, '0')
    return `APP${yyyy}${mm}${dd}${idPart}`
}

function CopyButton({ text }) {
    const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)
    const copy = async (e) => {
        e.stopPropagation()
        try {
            await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
            setCopied(true)
            setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500)
        } catch {}
    }
    return (
        <button
            type="button"
            onClick={copy}
            title="Copy reference"
            className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700 dark:hover:bg-slate-800 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
        >
            {copied ? (
                <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" />
            ) : (
                <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            )}
        </button>
    )
}

export default function AppointmentsTab() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [slots, setSlots] = useState([])
    const [mine, setMine] = useState([])
    const [offices, setOffices] = useState([])
    const [services, setServices] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [booking, setBooking] = useState(false)
    const [cancelTarget, setCancelTarget] = useState(null)
    const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState(false)

    // Form
    const [selectedOfficeId, setSelectedOfficeId] = useState('')
    const [selectedServiceId, setSelectedServiceId] = useState('')
    const [selectedDate, setSelectedDate] = useState('')
    const [selectedSlotId, setSelectedSlotId] = useState('')
    const [notes, setNotes] = useState('')

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [s, m, o, q] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/user/appointments/available').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
                api.get('/api/user/appointments').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
                api.get('/api/offices').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
                api.get('/api/queues').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
            ])
            setSlots(s.data?.data || [])
            setMine(m.data?.data || [])
            setOffices(o.data?.data || [])
            setServices(q.data?.data || [])
        } catch {
            // silent
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 10000, slowMs: 30000 })

    // ─── Office options: real offices + any queue-derived fallback ───
    const officeOptions = useMemo(() => {
        const map = new Map()
        offices.forEach((o) => {
            if (o.isActive === false) return
            map.set(String(o.id), { id: String(o.id), name: o.name })
        })
        services.forEach((q) => {
            if (!q.officeId) return
            const key = String(q.officeId)
            if (!map.has(key)) {
                map.set(key, { id: key, name: q.officeName || 'General' })
            }
        })
        return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    }, [offices, services])

    // ─── Services in selected office ───
    const serviceOptions = useMemo(() => {
        if (!selectedOfficeId) return []
        return services
            .filter((q) => String(q.officeId) === String(selectedOfficeId) && q.isActive !== false)
            .map((q) => ({ id: String(q.id), name: q.name }))
            .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    }, [services, selectedOfficeId])

    const selectedService = useMemo(
        () => services.find((s) => String(s.id) === String(selectedServiceId)) || null,
        [services, selectedServiceId]
    )

    // ─── Slots matching the current selection ───
    const matchingSlots = useMemo(() => {
        if (!selectedService) return []
        const svcName = (selectedService.name || '').toLowerCase()
        const officeName = (selectedService.officeName || '').toLowerCase()
        return slots.filter((sl) => {
            const t = (sl.title || '').toLowerCase()
            const o = (sl.officeName || sl.department || sl.faculty || '').toLowerCase()
            return t === svcName && (!officeName || o === officeName || !o)
        })
    }, [slots, selectedService])

    const dateOptions = useMemo(() => {
        const set = new Set()
        matchingSlots.forEach((s) => {
            const k = dateKey(s.startTime)
            if (k) set.add(k)
        })
        return Array.from(set).sort()
    }, [matchingSlots])

    const timeSlots = useMemo(() => {
        if (!selectedDate) return []
        return matchingSlots
            .filter((s) => dateKey(s.startTime) === selectedDate)
            .sort((a, b) => (a.startTime || '').localeCompare(b.startTime || ''))
    }, [matchingSlots, selectedDate])

    const selectedSlot = useMemo(
        () => timeSlots.find((s) => String(s.id) === String(selectedSlotId)) || null,
        [timeSlots, selectedSlotId]
    )

    // Reset downstream on upstream changes
    useEffect(() => {
        setSelectedServiceId('')
        setSelectedDate('')
        setSelectedSlotId('')
    }, [selectedOfficeId])

    useEffect(() => {
        setSelectedDate('')
        setSelectedSlotId('')
    }, [selectedServiceId])

    useEffect(() => {
        setSelectedSlotId('')
    }, [selectedDate])

    // ─── Upcoming appointment guard ───
    const upcomingAppointments = useMemo(
        () =>
            mine.filter(
                (a) =>
                    (a.status === 'BOOKED' || a.status === 'CONFIRMED') &&
                    a.startTime &&
                    new Date(a.startTime).getTime() > Date.now()
            ),
        [mine]
    )
    const hasUpcoming = upcomingAppointments.length > 0

    // ─── Actions ───
    const book = async () => {
        if (hasUpcoming) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'You already have an upcoming appointment' })
            return
        }
        if (!selectedSlot) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Pick a time slot first' })
            return
        }
        setBooking(true)
        try {
            await api.post('/api/user/appointments/book', {
                slotId: selectedSlot.id,
                notes: notes.trim() || null,
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: `Booked: ${selectedSlot.title}` })
            setSelectedOfficeId('')
            setSelectedServiceId('')
            setSelectedDate('')
            setSelectedSlotId('')
            setNotes('')
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({
                type: 'error',
                message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to book',
            })
        } finally {
            setBooking(false)
        }
    }

    const cancel = async () => {
        if (!cancelTarget) return
        setCancelling(true)
        try {
            await api.post(`/api/user/appointments/${cancelTarget.id}/cancel`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Appointment cancelled' })
            setCancelTarget(null)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({
                type: 'error',
                message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to cancel',
            })
        } finally {
            setCancelling(false)
        }
    }

    const sortedMine = useMemo(() => {
        const now = Date.now()
        return [...mine].sort((a, b) => {
            const at = a.startTime ? new Date(a.startTime).getTime() : 0
            const bt = b.startTime ? new Date(b.startTime).getTime() : 0
            const aFuture = at > now
            const bFuture = bt > now
            if (aFuture && !bFuture) return -1
            if (!aFuture && bFuture) return 1
            return aFuture ? at - bt : bt - at
        })
    }, [mine])

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[360px_1fr]">
                <div className="h-96 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800" />
                <div className="h-96 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800" />
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div>
            <div className="mb-5">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    Appointments
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Book 1-on-1 slots with staff and track your bookings.
                </p>
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 items-start gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[360px_1fr]">
                {/* ═══ LEFT: Book Appointment ═══ */}
                <div className="lg:sticky lg:top-[150px]">
                    <Card className="h-fit">
                        <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            Book Appointment
                        </h3>

                        {officeOptions.length === 0 ? (
                            <EmptyState
                                icon={Building2}
                                title="No offices available"
                                description="Your university hasn't set up any offices yet."
                            />
                        ) : (
                            <div className="space-y-3">
                                {hasUpcoming && (
                                    <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-3 text-xs text-amber-800 dark:border-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-300">
                                        <Info className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                                        <span>
                      You have an upcoming appointment. Complete or cancel it
                      before booking another.
                    </span>
                                    </div>
                                )}

                                {/* Office */}
                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                        Office
                                    </label>
                                    <select
                                        value={selectedOfficeId}
                                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedOfficeId(e.target.value)}
                                        disabled={hasUpcoming}
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                    >
                                        <option value="">Select Office</option>
                                        {officeOptions.map((o) => (
                                            <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>
                                                {o.name}
                                            </option>
                                        ))}
                                    </select>
                                </div>

                                {/* Service */}
                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                        Service
                                    </label>
                                    <select
                                        value={selectedServiceId}
                                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedServiceId(e.target.value)}
                                        disabled={!selectedOfficeId || hasUpcoming}
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                    >
                                        <option value="">
                                            {!selectedOfficeId
                                                ? 'Pick an office first'
                                                : serviceOptions.length === 0
                                                    ? 'No services in this office'
                                                    : 'Select Service'}
                                        </option>
                                        {serviceOptions.map((s) => (
                                            <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                                                {s.name}
                                            </option>
                                        ))}
                                    </select>
                                </div>

                                {/* No slots message */}
                                {selectedService && matchingSlots.length === 0 && (
                                    <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 p-3 text-xs text-sky-800 dark:border-sky-900 dark:bg-sky-950/40 dark:text-sky-300">
                                        <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                                        <span>
                      No slots published yet for <strong>{selectedService.name}</strong>.
                      Check back soon — staff publish new slots regularly.
                    </span>
                                    </div>
                                )}

                                {/* Date */}
                                {selectedService && matchingSlots.length > 0 && (
                                    <div>
                                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                            Date
                                        </label>
                                        <select
                                            value={selectedDate}
                                            onChange={(e) => setSelectedDate(e.target.value)}
                                            disabled={hasUpcoming}
                                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                        >
                                            <option value="">Select Date</option>
                                            {dateOptions.map((d) => (
                                                <option key={d} value={d}>
                                                    {formatDate(d)}
                                                </option>
                                            ))}
                                        </select>
                                    </div>
                                )}

                                {/* Time */}
                                {selectedDate && (
                                    <div>
                                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                            Time
                                        </label>
                                        <select
                                            value={selectedSlotId}
                                            onChange={(e) => setSelectedSlotId(e.target.value)}
                                            disabled={hasUpcoming}
                                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 disabled:opacity-50 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                        >
                                            <option value="">Select Time</option>
                                            {timeSlots.map((s) => (
                                                <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                                                    {formatTimeOnly(s.startTime)} — {s.remaining} left
                                                </option>
                                            ))}
                                        </select>
                                    </div>
                                )}

                                {/* Preview */}
                                {selectedSlot && (
                                    <motion.div
                                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                                        className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3 text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800/50"
                                    >
                                        <div className="font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                            {selectedSlot.title}
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="mt-1.5 space-y-1 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                {formatTimeOnly(selectedSlot.startTime)} –{' '}
                                                {formatTimeOnly(selectedSlot.endTime)}
                                            </div>
                                            {selectedSlot.staffName && (
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                    <User className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                    {selectedSlot.staffName}
                                                    {selectedSlot.staffRole && ` · ${selectedSlot.staffRole}`}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                            {selectedSlot.location && (
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                    <MapPin className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                    {selectedSlot.roomDetail
                                                        ? `${selectedSlot.location} · ${selectedSlot.roomDetail}`
                                                        : selectedSlot.location}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    </motion.div>
                                )}

                                {/* Notes */}
                                {selectedSlot && (
                                    <div>
                                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                            Note for the host{' '}
                                            <span className="font-normal text-slate-400">(optional)</span>
                                        </label>
                                        <textarea
                                            rows={2}
                                            value={notes}
                                            onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
                                            placeholder="What would you like to discuss?"
                                            className="w-full resize-none rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                )}

                                <Button
                                    onClick={book}
                                    loading={booking}
                                    disabled={booking || hasUpcoming || !selectedSlot}
                                    fullWidth
                                    className="!mt-4"
                                >
                                    {hasUpcoming
                                        ? 'You have an appointment already'
                                        : booking
                                            ? 'Booking…'
                                            : 'Book Appointment'}
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </Card>
                </div>

                {/* ═══ RIGHT: My Appointments ═══ */}
                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                            <CalendarCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400" />
                            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                My Appointments
                            </h3>
                        </div>
                        <span className="rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2.5 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-600 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400">
              {sortedMine.length}
            </span>
                    </div>

                    {sortedMine.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="py-12 text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <Calendar className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                No appointments yet
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Book a slot from the left to get started.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <ul className="max-h-[600px] space-y-3 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                            {sortedMine.map((a) => {
                                const reference = generateReference(a)
                                const isUpcoming =
                                    a.status === 'BOOKED' || a.status === 'CONFIRMED'
                                const officeName = a.officeName || a.slotTitle || 'Appointment'

                                return (
                                    <motion.li
                                        key={a.id}
                                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                                        className={`rounded-lg border p-4 transition-colors ${
                                            isUpcoming
                                                ? 'border-brand-200 bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-50/40 to-white dark:border-brand-900 dark:from-brand-950/20 dark:to-slate-900'
                                                : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800'
                                        }`}
                                    >
                                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                                <h4 className="truncate text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                    {officeName}
                                                </h4>

                                                {a.slotTitle && a.slotTitle !== officeName && (
                                                    <p className="mt-0.5 truncate text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                        {a.slotTitle}
                                                    </p>
                                                )}

                                                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />
                                                    <span>{formatDateTime(a.startTime)}</span>
                                                </div>

                                                {a.staffName && (
                                                    <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                        <User className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                                                        {a.staffName}
                                                    </div>
                                                )}

                                                <div className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs">
                          <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            Reference:
                          </span>
                                                    <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {reference}
                          </span>
                                                    <CopyButton text={reference} />
                                                </div>
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-col items-end gap-2">
                                                <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[a.status] || 'default'}>
                                                    {STATUS_LABELS[a.status] || a.status}
                                                </Badge>

                                                {isUpcoming && (
                                                    <button
                                                        onClick={() => setCancelTarget(a)}
                                                        className="rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-xs text-rose-600 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40"
                                                    >
                                                        Cancel
                                                    </button>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </motion.li>
                                )
                            })}
                        </ul>
                    )}
                </Card>
            </div>

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!cancelTarget}
                onClose={() => !cancelling && setCancelTarget(null)}
                onConfirm={cancel}
                title="Cancel this appointment?"
                description={`${cancelTarget?.officeName || cancelTarget?.slotTitle || 'This appointment'} · ${formatDateTime(
                    cancelTarget?.startTime
                )}. The slot will be freed for other students.`}
                confirmLabel="Cancel appointment"
                cancelLabel="Keep it"
                variant="danger"
                loading={cancelling}
            />
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/StudentDashboard.jsx =====
import { Routes, Route, Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import {
    ListOrdered,
    History,
    Calendar,
    Route as RouteIcon,
    FileText,
    Building2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import DashboardLayout from '../components/layout/DashboardLayout'
import StudentHeader from '../components/student/StudentHeader'
import StudentHome from './student/StudentHome'
import QueuesTab from './student/QueuesTab'
import OfficesTab from './student/OfficesTab'
import HistoryTab from './student/HistoryTab'
import AppointmentsTab from './student/AppointmentsTab'
import WorkflowsTab from './student/WorkflowsTab'
import DocumentsTab from './student/DocumentsTab'
import { useUniversityStore } from '../store/universityStore'

const ROUTE_SUBTITLES = {
    '/student': "Skip the line — join a queue from anywhere and get notified when it's your turn.",
    '/student/queues': 'Join a queue, track your position, and manage your tickets.',
    '/student/offices': 'Browse every office and the services it offers.',
    '/student/history': 'Your past queue tickets and how long you waited.',
    '/student/appointments': 'Book and manage your appointments.',
    '/student/workflows': 'Track your multi-step clearance processes.',
    '/student/documents': 'Your uploaded documents.',
}

export default function StudentDashboard() {
    const { university } = useUniversityStore()
    const location = useLocation()

    const tabs = [
        { to: '/student/queues', label: 'Browse Queues', icon: ListOrdered },
        { to: '/student/offices', label: 'Offices', icon: Building2 },
        { to: '/student/history', label: 'History', icon: History },
    ]

    if (!university || university.featureAppointments !== false) {
        tabs.push({ to: '/student/appointments', label: 'Appointments', icon: Calendar })
    }
    if (!university || university.featureWorkflows !== false) {
        tabs.push({ to: '/student/workflows', label: 'Workflows', icon: RouteIcon })
    }
    if (!university || university.featureDocuments !== false) {
        tabs.push({ to: '/student/documents', label: 'Documents', icon: FileText })
    }

    const subtitle =
        ROUTE_SUBTITLES[location.pathname] || 'Everything you need for today.'

    return (
        <DashboardLayout tabs={tabs}>
            <StudentHeader subtitle={subtitle} />
            <Routes>
                <Route index element={<StudentHome />} />
                <Route path="queues" element={<QueuesTab />} />
                <Route path="offices" element={<OfficesTab />} />
                <Route path="history" element={<HistoryTab />} />
                <Route path="appointments" element={<AppointmentsTab />} />
                <Route path="workflows" element={<WorkflowsTab />} />
                <Route path="documents" element={<DocumentsTab />} />
                <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/student" replace />} />
            </Routes>
        </DashboardLayout>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/student/DocumentsTab.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Upload, FileText, Trash2, Download, AlertCircle,
    CheckCircle2, Clock, XCircle, Filter,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import { SkeletonRow } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'

const DOC_TYPES = [
    { value: 'PAYMENT_PROOF', label: 'Payment proof' },
    { value: 'TRANSCRIPT', label: 'Transcript' },
    { value: 'ID_CARD', label: 'ID card' },
    { value: 'ADMISSION_LETTER', label: 'Admission letter' },
    { value: 'MEDICAL_REPORT', label: 'Medical report' },
    { value: 'CERTIFICATE', label: 'Certificate' },
    { value: 'OTHER', label: 'Other' },
]

const STATUS_VARIANTS = {
    PENDING: 'warning',
    APPROVED: 'success',
    REJECTED: 'danger',
}

const STATUS_LABELS = {
    PENDING: 'Pending review',
    APPROVED: 'Approved',
    REJECTED: 'Rejected',
}

const STATUS_ICONS = {
    PENDING: Clock,
    APPROVED: CheckCircle2,
    REJECTED: XCircle,
}

const FILTERS = [
    { value: 'ALL', label: 'All' },
    { value: 'PENDING', label: 'Pending' },
    { value: 'APPROVED', label: 'Approved' },
    { value: 'REJECTED', label: 'Rejected' },
]

function formatSize(bytes) {
    if (!bytes) return '—'
    if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`
    return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

function formatDateTime(dt) {
    if (!dt) return '—'
    try {
        return new Date(dt).toLocaleString([], {
            day: 'numeric',
            month: 'short',
            year: 'numeric',
            hour: '2-digit',
            minute: '2-digit',
        })
    } catch {
        return '—'
    }
}

export default function DocumentsTab() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [docs, setDocs] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
    const [uploadOpen, setUploadOpen] = useState(false)
    const [filter, setFilter] = useState('ALL')
    const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState(null)
    const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false)

    const [type, setType] = useState('OTHER')
    const [file, setFile] = useState(null)
    const fileInputRef = useRef(null)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/user/documents')
            setDocs(res.data?.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load documents' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 10000, slowMs: 30000 })

    const upload = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!file) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Select a file first' })
            return
        }
        setUploading(true)
        try {
            const form = new FormData()
            form.append('file', file)
            form.append('type', type)
            await api.post('/api/user/documents', form, {
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' },
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Document uploaded' })
            setFile(null)
            setType('OTHER')
            if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = ''
            setUploadOpen(false)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Upload failed' })
        } finally {
            setUploading(false)
        }
    }

    const destroy = async () => {
        if (!deleteTarget) return
        setDeleting(true)
        try {
            await api.delete(`/api/user/documents/${deleteTarget.id}`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Document deleted' })
            setDeleteTarget(null)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to delete' })
        } finally {
            setDeleting(false)
        }
    }

    const download = async (doc) => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get(`/api/documents/${doc.id}/download`, {
                responseType: 'blob',
            })
