                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                    >
                                        {officeGroups.map((g) => (
                                            <option key={g.key} value={String(g.officeId ?? g.key)}>
                                                {g.label} ({g.queues.length})
                                            </option>
                                        ))}
                                    </select>
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <label className="mb-1.5 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                                        Service
                                    </label>
                                    <select
                                        value={selectedServiceId}
                                        onChange={(e) => setSelectedServiceId(e.target.value)}
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100"
                                    >
                                        <option value="">Select Service</option>
                                        {servicesInOffice.map((q) => {
                                            const isLocked = lockedQueueIds.has(q.id)
                                            const matricLocked = isFresher && q.matricRequired
                                            const suffix = isLocked ? ' (locked)' : matricLocked ? ' (matric required)' : ''
                                            return (
                                                <option key={q.id} value={q.id} disabled={isLocked || matricLocked}>
                                                    {q.name}{suffix}
                                                </option>
                                            )
                                        })}
                                    </select>
                                </div>

                                {selectedService && (
                                    <motion.div
                                        initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                                        className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3 text-xs dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800/50"
                                    >
                                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1">
                                            {selectedService.location && (
                                                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                          <MapPin className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                    {selectedService.location}
                        </span>
                                            )}
                                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400">
                        <Users className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                {selectedService.waitingCount ?? 0} waiting
                      </span>
                                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                        <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                        ~{selectedService.estimatedMinutes ?? 0} min
                      </span>
                                        </div>
                                        {selectedService.currentlyServing && (
                                            <div className="mt-2 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                Now serving:{' '}
                                                <span className="font-mono font-bold text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400">
                          {selectedService.currentlyServing}
                        </span>
                                            </div>
                                        )}
                                    </motion.div>
                                )}

                                <Button
                                    onClick={joinQueue}
                                    disabled={!selectedService || !!activeTicket || joining}
                                    loading={joining}
                                    fullWidth
                                    className="!mt-4"
                                >
                                    {activeTicket ? 'Queue turn is near' : joining ? 'Joining…' : 'Join Queue'}
                                </Button>

                                {activeTicket && (
                                    <p className="text-center text-xs text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                                        Complete your current queue turn before joining another queue.
                                    </p>
                                )}
                            </div>
                        )}
                    </Card>
                </div>

                <Card>
                    <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            My Queue Tickets
                        </h3>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                            <div className="relative">
                                <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={ticketSearch}
                                    onChange={(e) => setTicketSearch(e.target.value)}
                                    placeholder="Search ticket ID"
                                    className="w-40 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-1.5 pl-8 pr-2 text-xs text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                                />
                            </div>
                            <select
                                value={statusFilter}
                                onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
                                className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-300"
                            >
                                {STATUS_FILTERS.map((s) => (
                                    <option key={s.value} value={s.value}>
                                        {s.label}
                                    </option>
                                ))}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {ticketList.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="py-12 text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <Ticket className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                {ticketSearch.trim()
                                    ? 'No tickets match your search'
                                    : statusFilter === 'ALL'
                                        ? 'No queue tickets yet'
                                        : `No ${STATUS_LABELS[statusFilter]?.toLowerCase() || ''} tickets`}
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                {ticketSearch.trim()
                                    ? 'Check the ticket ID and try again.'
                                    : statusFilter === 'ALL'
                                        ? 'Join a service from the left to get started.'
                                        : 'Try a different filter.'}
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <div className="max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                            <ul className="space-y-3">
                                {ticketList.slice(0, 20).map((t) => {
                                    const isActive = t._isActive
                                    const isActionable = t.status === 'WAITING' || t.status === 'CALLED'
                                    return (
                                        <motion.li
                                            key={t.id}
                                            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
                                            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                                            className={`rounded-lg border p-4 transition-colors ${
                                                isActive
                                                    ? 'border-brand-200 bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-50/60 to-white dark:border-brand-900 dark:from-brand-950/30 dark:to-slate-900'
                                                    : 'border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-800'
                                            }`}
                                        >
                                            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                                    <h4 className="truncate text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                        {t.queueName || t.officeName || 'Service'}
                                                    </h4>
                                                </div>
                                                <Badge variant={STATUS_VARIANTS[t.status] || 'default'}>
                                                    {STATUS_LABELS[t.status] || t.status}
                                                </Badge>
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
                                                <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Ticket:</span>
                                                <span className="font-mono font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                          {t.ticketNumber}
                        </span>
                                                <CopyButton text={t.ticketNumber} />
                                            </div>

                                            {t.position != null && (
                                                <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    <span className="text-slate-500">Position:</span>{' '}
                                                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {t.position}
                          </span>
                                                </div>
                                            )}

                                            {t.estimatedMinutesRemaining != null && (
                                                <div className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    <span className="text-slate-500">Est. Wait:</span>{' '}
                                                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                            {t.estimatedMinutesRemaining} min
                          </span>
                                                </div>
                                            )}

                                            {isActive && isActionable && (
                                                <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-3 text-xs dark:border-slate-700">
                                                    <button
                                                        onClick={() => rescheduleTurn(t)}
                                                        disabled={acting === `reschedule-${t.id}`}
                                                        className="font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 disabled:opacity-50 dark:text-brand-400"
                                                    >
                                                        {acting === `reschedule-${t.id}` ? 'Rescheduling…' : 'Reschedule turn'}
                                                    </button>
                                                    <button
                                                        onClick={() => skipTurn(t)}
                                                        disabled={acting === `skip-${t.id}`}
                                                        className="font-medium text-rose-600 transition-colors hover:text-rose-700 disabled:opacity-50 dark:text-rose-400"
                                                    >
                                                        {acting === `skip-${t.id}` ? 'Skipping…' : 'Skip'}
                                                    </button>
                                                    <button
                                                        onClick={() => setConfirmLeave(true)}
                                                        className="font-medium text-slate-500 transition-colors hover:text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400 dark:hover:text-slate-300"
                                                    >
                                                        Leave queue
                                                    </button>
                                                </div>
                                            )}

                                            {isActive && !isActionable && (
                                                <div className="mt-3 border-t border-slate-200 pt-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-400">
                                                    {t.status === 'SERVING'
                                                        ? 'You are being served.'
                                                        : 'No actions available for this ticket.'}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </motion.li>
                                    )
                                })}
                            </ul>
                        </div>
                    )}
                </Card>
            </div>

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={confirmLeave}
                onClose={() => !leaving && setConfirmLeave(false)}
                onConfirm={leaveQueue}
                title="Leave the queue?"
                description={`You'll lose your current position for ticket ${activeTicket?.ticketNumber}. You can rejoin later, but you'll go to the end of the line.`}
                confirmLabel="Leave queue"
                cancelLabel="Keep my spot"
                variant="danger"
                loading={leaving}
            />
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/student/StudentHome.jsx =====
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Calendar, Ticket, FileText, CheckCircle2, Building2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import { useAdaptivePoll } from '../../hooks/useAdaptivePoll'
import { groupQueuesByOffice } from '../../lib/groupQueues'

function StatTile({ label, value, icon: Icon, accent, valueColor, to, ctaLabel, delay }) {
    return (
        <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.4, delay }}
        >
            <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                            {label}
                        </div>
                        <div className={`mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight ${valueColor}`}>
                            {value}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    {Icon && (
                        <div className={`flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${accent}`}>
                            <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                        </div>
                    )}
                </div>
                {to && (
                    <Link
                        to={to}
                        className="mt-auto pt-3 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                    >
                        {ctaLabel || 'View all'} →
                    </Link>
                )}
            </Card>
        </motion.div>
    )
}

function LiveQueueCard({ office }) {
    const hasQueue = office.queue != null
    const waitingCount = office.queue?.waitingCount ?? 0
    const currentlyServing = office.queue?.currentlyServing || 'None'
    const avgWait = office.queue?.estimatedMinutes ?? 0

    return (
        <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
            <div className="mb-3 flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
                <h3 className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                    {office.name}
                </h3>
                {hasQueue && (
                    <span className="inline-flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400">
            <span className="relative flex h-1.5 w-1.5">
              <span className="absolute inline-flex h-full w-full animate-ping rounded-full bg-emerald-400 opacity-75" />
              <span className="relative inline-flex h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
            </span>
            Live
          </span>
                )}
            </div>

            <div className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                Waiting:{' '}
                <span className="font-bold text-brand-600 dark:text-brand-400">
          {waitingCount}
        </span>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Now Serving
                    </div>
                    <div
                        className={`mt-1 truncate text-sm font-bold ${
                            currentlyServing && currentlyServing !== 'None'
                                ? 'font-mono text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400'
                                : 'text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500'
                        }`}
                    >
                        {currentlyServing}
                    </div>
                </div>
                <div>
                    <div className="text-[10px] font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Avg Wait
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                        {avgWait} min
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-3 dark:border-slate-800">
                <Link
                    to="/student/queues"
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                >
                    Join this queue →
                </Link>
            </div>
        </Card>
    )
}

export default function StudentHome() {
    const [queues, setQueues] = useState([])
    const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)
    const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([])
    const [documents, setDocuments] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [q, s, a, d] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/queues').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
                api.get('/api/user/stats').catch(() => ({ data: { data: null } })),
                api.get('/api/user/appointments').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
                api.get('/api/user/documents').catch(() => ({ data: { data: [] } })),
            ])
            setQueues(q.data?.data || [])
            setStats(s.data?.data || null)
            setAppointments(a.data?.data || [])
            setDocuments(d.data?.data || [])
        } catch {
            // silent
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [])

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
    useAdaptivePoll(load, { fastMs: 5000, slowMs: 15000 })

    const liveQueues = useMemo(() => {
        const groups = groupQueuesByOffice(queues.filter((q) => q.isActive !== false))
        return groups
            .map((g) => {
                const sorted = [...g.queues].sort(
                    (a, b) => (b.waitingCount ?? 0) - (a.waitingCount ?? 0)
                )
                return {
                    key: g.key,
                    name: g.label,
                    queue: sorted[0] || null,
                }
            })
            .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name))
    }, [queues])

    const myAppointments = appointments.filter(
        (a) => a.status === 'BOOKED' || a.status === 'CONFIRMED'
    ).length
    const myQueueTickets = stats?.hasActiveTicket ? 1 : stats?.todayJoined ?? 0
    const myDocuments = documents.length
    const completedTasks = stats?.totalCompleted ?? 0

    return (
        <>
            {/* Stat tiles */}
            <div className="mb-7 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
                <StatTile
                    label="My Appointments"
                    value={myAppointments}
                    icon={Calendar}
                    accent="bg-violet-50 text-violet-600 dark:bg-violet-950/40 dark:text-violet-400"
                    valueColor="text-violet-600 dark:text-violet-400"
                    to="/student/appointments"
                    ctaLabel="View all"
                    delay={0}
                />
                <StatTile
                    label="My Queue Tickets"
                    value={myQueueTickets}
                    icon={Ticket}
                    accent="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-400"
                    valueColor="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400"
                    to="/student/queues"
                    ctaLabel="View all"
                    delay={0.05}
                />
                <StatTile
                    label="My Documents"
                    value={myDocuments}
                    icon={FileText}
                    accent="bg-purple-50 text-purple-600 dark:bg-purple-950/40 dark:text-purple-400"
                    valueColor="text-purple-600 dark:text-purple-400"
                    to="/student/documents"
                    ctaLabel="View all"
                    delay={0.1}
                />
                <StatTile
                    label="Completed Tasks"
                    value={completedTasks}
                    icon={CheckCircle2}
                    accent="bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-400"
                    valueColor="text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400"
                    delay={0.15}
                />
            </div>

            {/* Live Queues */}
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ delay: 0.2 }}
            >
                <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Live Queues
                    </h2>
                    <Link
                        to="/student/queues"
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                    >
                        View my queues →
                    </Link>
                </div>

                {loading ? (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                        {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => (
                            <div
                                key={i}
                                className="h-44 animate-pulse rounded-xl bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800"
                            />
                        ))}
                    </div>
                ) : liveQueues.length === 0 ? (
                    <Card>
                        <div className="py-12 text-center">
                            <div className="mx-auto mb-3 flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <Building2 className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-400" />
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                No open queues
                            </p>
                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                Check back shortly — your university hasn't opened any queues yet.
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                ) : (
                    <motion.div
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        variants={{ initial: {}, animate: { transition: { staggerChildren: 0.04 } } }}
                        className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3"
                    >
                        {liveQueues.map((o) => (
                            <motion.div
                                key={o.key}
                                variants={{ initial: { opacity: 0, y: 6 }, animate: { opacity: 1, y: 0 } }}
                            >
                                <LiveQueueCard office={o} />
                            </motion.div>
                        ))}
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </motion.div>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/student/WorkflowsTab.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Route as RouteIcon,
    CheckCircle2,
    Lock,
    ArrowRight,
    Play,
    X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import { SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { formatDateTime } from '../../lib/format'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

const statusVariant = {
    IN_PROGRESS: 'warning',
    COMPLETED: 'success',
    ABANDONED: 'default',
}

export default function WorkflowsTab() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [mine, setMine] = useState([])
    const [available, setAvailable] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [starting, setStarting] = useState(null)
    const [abandonTarget, setAbandonTarget] = useState(null)
    const [abandoning, setAbandoning] = useState(false)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [m, a] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/user/workflows'),
                api.get('/api/user/workflows/available'),
            ])
            setMine(m.data.data || [])
            setAvailable(a.data.data || [])
        } catch {
            // silent
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const start = async (workflowId) => {
        setStarting(workflowId)
        try {
            await api.post(`/api/user/workflows/${workflowId}/start`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Workflow started' })
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to start' })
        } finally {
            setStarting(null)
        }
    }

    const abandon = async () => {
        if (!abandonTarget) return
        setAbandoning(true)
        try {
            await api.post(`/api/user/workflows/${abandonTarget.id}/abandon`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Workflow abandoned' })
            setAbandonTarget(null)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to abandon' })
        } finally {
            setAbandoning(false)
        }
    }

    const startedIds = new Set(mine.map((m) => m.workflowId))
    const notStarted = available.filter((w) => !startedIds.has(w.id))

    const inProgress = mine.filter((m) => m.status === 'IN_PROGRESS')
    const completed = mine.filter((m) => m.status === 'COMPLETED')
    const abandoned = mine.filter((m) => m.status === 'ABANDONED')

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <div className="space-y-10">
            <div>
                <h2 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                    Clearance Workflows
                </h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    Multi-step clearance chains. Complete each step in order — you can only join
                    the current step's queue.
                </p>
            </div>

            {inProgress.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                    <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        In progress
                    </h3>
                    <motion.div
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="space-y-4"
                    >
                        {inProgress.map((w) => (
                            <motion.div key={w.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                                <WorkflowProgressCard
                                    workflow={w}
                                    onAbandon={() => setAbandonTarget(w)}
                                />
                            </motion.div>
                        ))}
                    </motion.div>
                </section>
            )}

            {notStarted.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                    <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Available workflows
                    </h3>
                    <motion.div
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2"
                    >
                        {notStarted.map((w) => (
                            <motion.div key={w.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                                <Card className="flex h-full flex-col">
                                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                                        <div className="min-w-0">
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                <RouteIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                                <h4 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                    {w.name}
                                                </h4>
                                            </div>
                                            {w.session && (
                                                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                                    Session {w.session}
                                                </p>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                        <Badge variant="default">{w.stepCount} steps</Badge>
                                    </div>

                                    {w.description && (
                                        <p className="mt-3 line-clamp-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {w.description}
                                        </p>
                                    )}

                                    <ol className="mt-4 space-y-1.5 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                                        {w.steps.map((s, idx) => (
                                            <li
                                                key={s.order}
                                                className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600"
                                            >
                        <span className="flex h-5 w-5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-100 text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600">
                          {idx + 1}
                        </span>
                                                <span className="truncate">{s.label || s.queueName}</span>
                                            </li>
                                        ))}
                                    </ol>

                                    <div className="mt-auto pt-5">
                                        <Button
                                            fullWidth
                                            icon={Play}
                                            onClick={() => start(w.id)}
                                            loading={starting === w.id}
                                            disabled={starting === w.id}
                                        >
                                            Start workflow
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>
                                </Card>
                            </motion.div>
                        ))}
                    </motion.div>
                </section>
            )}

            {completed.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                    <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Completed
                    </h3>
                    <Card padded={false}>
                        <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                            {completed.map((w) => (
                                <li
                                    key={w.id}
                                    className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 px-6 py-3"
                                >
                                    <div>
                    <span className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                      {w.workflowName}
                    </span>
                                        <span className="ml-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                      · {formatDateTime(w.completedAt)}
                    </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <Badge variant="success">
                                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                                        Completed
                                    </Badge>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </Card>
                </section>
            )}

            {abandoned.length > 0 && (
                <section>
                    <h3 className="mb-3 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                        Abandoned
                    </h3>
                    <Card padded={false}>
                        <ul className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                            {abandoned.map((w) => (
                                <li
                                    key={w.id}
                                    className="flex items-center justify-between px-6 py-3 text-sm text-slate-600"
                                >
                                    <span>{w.workflowName}</span>
                                    <Badge variant="default">Abandoned</Badge>
                                </li>
                            ))}
                        </ul>
                    </Card>
                </section>
            )}

            {mine.length === 0 && notStarted.length === 0 && (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={RouteIcon}
                    title="No workflows available"
                    description="Your university hasn't published any clearance workflows yet."
                />
            )}

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!abandonTarget}
                onClose={() => !abandoning && setAbandonTarget(null)}
                onConfirm={abandon}
                title={`Abandon "${abandonTarget?.workflowName}"?`}
                description="You'll lose your progress on this workflow. You can restart it later, but you'll begin from step 1."
                confirmLabel="Abandon"
                cancelLabel="Keep going"
                variant="danger"
                loading={abandoning}
            />
        </div>
    )
}

function WorkflowProgressCard({ workflow, onAbandon }) {
    const {
        name,
        session,
        steps,
        currentStepOrder,
        completedSteps,
        totalSteps,
        currentQueueName,
    } = workflow

    return (
        <Card>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <RouteIcon className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                        <h4 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">{name}</h4>
                    </div>
                    {session && <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">Session {session}</p>}
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Badge variant="warning">In progress</Badge>
                    <button
                        onClick={onAbandon}
                        className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 hover:text-rose-600"
                        aria-label="Abandon workflow"
                    >
                        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div className="mt-4">
                <div className="mb-1.5 flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
          <span>
            Step {currentStepOrder} of {totalSteps}
          </span>
                    <span>
            {completedSteps}/{totalSteps} complete
          </span>
                </div>
                <div className="h-1.5 w-full overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-slate-100">
                    <motion.div
                        initial={{ width: 0 }}
                        animate={{ width: `${(completedSteps / totalSteps) * 100}%` }}
                        transition={{ duration: 0.6, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
                        className="h-full rounded-full bg-brand-600"
                    />
                </div>
            </div>

            {currentQueueName && (
                <div className="mt-4 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-amber-900">
                    <ArrowRight className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>
            <strong>Next step:</strong> join the <strong>{currentQueueName}</strong> queue
            to continue.
          </span>
                </div>
            )}

            <ol className="mt-5 space-y-2">
                {steps.map((s) => (
                    <li key={s.order} className="flex items-start gap-3">
                        <div
                            className={`flex h-6 w-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-semibold ${
                                s.state === 'COMPLETED'
                                    ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700'
                                    : s.state === 'CURRENT'
                                        ? 'bg-brand-600 text-white'
                                        : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-500'
                            }`}
                        >
                            {s.state === 'COMPLETED' ? (
                                <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            ) : s.state === 'CURRENT' ? (
                                s.order
                            ) : (
                                <Lock className="h-3 w-3" />
                            )}
                        </div>
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                            <div
                                className={`text-sm ${
                                    s.state === 'LOCKED' ? 'text-slate-400' : 'font-medium text-slate-900'
                                }`}
                            >
                                {s.queueName}
                                {s.label && (
                                    <span className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">
                    · {s.label}
                  </span>
                                )}
                            </div>
                            {s.state === 'CURRENT' && (
                                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600">
                                    ← You are here
                                </p>
                            )}
                        </div>
                        {s.state === 'CURRENT' && (
                            <span className="rounded-full bg-brand-50 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase text-brand-700">
                Current
              </span>
                        )}
                    </li>
                ))}
            </ol>
        </Card>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/AdmittedStudentsButton.jsx =====
import { useState } from 'react'
import { AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import { Upload } from 'lucide-react'
import AdmittedStudentsModal from './AdmittedStudentsModal'

export default function AdmittedStudentsButton({ university }) {
    const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
    return (
        <>
            <button
                onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-violet-600 transition-colors hover:text-violet-700"
            >
                <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                Admission list
            </button>
            <AnimatePresence>
                {open && <AdmittedStudentsModal university={university} onClose={() => setOpen(false)} />}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/AdmittedStudentsModal.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    X, Upload, Trash2, FileText, AlertTriangle, CheckCircle2,
    Loader2, Search,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'

export default function AdmittedStudentsModal({ university, onClose }) {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const fileRef = useRef(null)

    const [list, setList] = useState([])
    const [stats, setStats] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
    const [clearing, setClearing] = useState(false)
    const [confirmClear, setConfirmClear] = useState(false)
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
    const [replaceExisting, setReplaceExisting] = useState(false)

    const load = async () => {
        setLoading(true)
        try {
            const [l, s] = await Promise.all([
                api.get(`/api/super-admin/universities/${university.id}/admitted-students`, {
                    params: { size: 100 },
                }),
                api.get(`/api/super-admin/universities/${university.id}/admitted-students/stats`),
            ])
            setList(l.data?.data?.content || [])
            setStats(s.data?.data || null)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load list' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }

    useEffect(() => { load() }, [university.id])

    const onFile = async (e) => {
        const file = e.target.files?.[0]
        if (!file) return

        if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.csv')) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Please select a .csv file' })
            return
        }

        setUploading(true)
        try {
            const form = new FormData()
            form.append('file', file)
            const res = await api.post(
                `/api/super-admin/universities/${university.id}/admitted-students/upload?replace=${replaceExisting}`,
                form,
                { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data' } }
            )
            push({
                type: 'success',
                message: res.data?.message || 'Uploaded',
            })
            if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = ''
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({
                type: 'error',
                message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Upload failed',
            })
        } finally {
            setUploading(false)
        }
    }

    const clearAll = async () => {
        setClearing(true)
        try {
            await api.delete(`/api/super-admin/universities/${university.id}/admitted-students`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'List cleared' })
            setConfirmClear(false)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to clear' })
        } finally {
            setClearing(false)
        }
    }

    const filtered = search.trim()
        ? list.filter((r) => {
            const s = search.toLowerCase()
            return (
                r.fullName?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                r.jambRegNumber?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                r.admissionRef?.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
                r.applicationNumber?.toLowerCase().includes(s)
            )
        })
        : list

    return (
        <>
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                onClick={onClose}
                className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm"
            />
            <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center overflow-y-auto p-4">
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 12 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                    exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 12 }}
                    onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                    className="relative my-8 w-full max-w-3xl rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                >
                    <button
                        onClick={onClose}
                        className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                    >
                        <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </button>

                    <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Admitted students list — {university.name}
                    </h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Upload a CSV so freshers can be verified against the official admission list.
                    </p>

                    {/* Stats */}
                    {stats && (
                        <div className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                            <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-3 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Total</div>
                                <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    {stats.total}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className="rounded-lg border border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50 p-3 dark:border-emerald-900 dark:bg-emerald-950/40">
                                <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-600">Claimed</div>
                                <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-emerald-700 dark:text-emerald-400">
                                    {stats.claimed}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <div className="rounded-lg border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 p-3 dark:border-sky-900 dark:bg-sky-950/40">
                                <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-sky-600">Available</div>
                                <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold text-sky-700 dark:text-sky-400">
                                    {stats.unclaimed}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Upload area */}
                    <div className="mt-5 rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 p-5 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
                            <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-white shadow-sm dark:bg-slate-700">
                                <FileText className="h-6 w-6 text-slate-500" />
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex-1">
                                <div className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Upload admission list CSV
                                </div>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    Required column: <code className="rounded bg-slate-200 px-1 dark:bg-slate-700">full_name</code>.
                                    Optional: jamb_reg_number, admission_ref, application_number, department, faculty, admission_year.
                                </p>

                                <label className="mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                                    <input
                                        type="checkbox"
                                        checked={replaceExisting}
                                        onChange={(e) => setReplaceExisting(e.target.checked)}
                                        className="h-3.5 w-3.5 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500"
                                    />
                                    Replace existing list (deletes all rows first)
                                </label>

                                <div className="mt-3">
                                    <input
                                        ref={fileRef}
                                        type="file"
                                        accept=".csv,text/csv"
                                        onChange={onFile}
                                        disabled={uploading}
                                        className="hidden"
                                        id="admitted-csv-input"
                                    />
                                    <label
                                        htmlFor="admitted-csv-input"
                                        className={`inline-flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:bg-slate-800 ${
                                            uploading ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-60' : ''
                                        }`}
                                    >
                                        {uploading ? (
                                            <>
                                                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                                                Uploading…
                                            </>
                                        ) : (
                                            <>
                                                <Upload className="h-4 w-4" />
                                                Choose CSV file
                                            </>
                                        )}
                                    </label>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            {stats?.total > 0 && (
                                <button
                                    type="button"
                                    onClick={() => setConfirmClear(true)}
                                    className="rounded-lg p-2 text-rose-600 transition-colors hover:bg-rose-50 dark:hover:bg-rose-950/40"
                                    title="Clear entire list"
                                >
                                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </button>
                            )}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* List */}
                    {loading ? (
                        <div className="mt-5 py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">Loading…</div>
                    ) : list.length === 0 ? (
                        <div className="mt-5 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-8 text-center dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800">
                            <div className="text-sm text-slate-500">No admitted students uploaded yet</div>
                        </div>
                    ) : (
                        <>
                            <div className="mt-5">
                                <Input
                                    placeholder="Search by name, JAMB, ref, or application number…"
                                    value={search}
                                    onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                                    icon={Search}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div className="mt-4 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto rounded-lg border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
                                <table className="w-full">
                                    <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-slate-50 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                    <tr>
                                        <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Name</th>
                                        <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">JAMB</th>
                                        <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ref</th>
                                        <th className="px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Status</th>
                                    </tr>
                                    </thead>
                                    <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
                                    {filtered.map((r) => (
                                        <tr key={r.id} className="text-sm">
                                            <td className="px-3 py-2 text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">{r.fullName}</td>
                                            <td className="px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">{r.jambRegNumber || '—'}</td>
                                            <td className="px-3 py-2 font-mono text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">{r.admissionRef || r.applicationNumber || '—'}</td>
                                            <td className="px-3 py-2">
                                                {r.claimed ? (
                                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-emerald-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-emerald-700">
                              <CheckCircle2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                              Claimed
                            </span>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium text-slate-600">
                              Available
                            </span>
                                                )}
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    ))}
                                    {filtered.length === 0 && (
                                        <tr>
                                            <td colSpan={4} className="px-3 py-6 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                                                No matches
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                    )}
                                    </tbody>
                                </table>
                            </div>
                        </>
                    )}

                    <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end">
                        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={onClose}>
                            Done
                        </Button>
                    </div>

                    {/* Clear confirm */}
                    <AnimatePresence>
                        {confirmClear && (
                            <>
                                <motion.div
                                    initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                                    animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                                    exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                                    onClick={() => setConfirmClear(false)}
                                    className="absolute inset-0 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/60 backdrop-blur-sm"
                                />
                                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center rounded-xl p-4">
                                    <motion.div
                                        initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                                        animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                                        exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                                        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                                        className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-xl bg-white p-5 shadow-xl dark:bg-slate-900"
                                    >
                                        <div className="mb-3 flex items-start gap-3">
                                            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40">
                                                <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 text-rose-600" />
                                            </div>
                                            <div>
                                                <h4 className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                                    Clear entire list?
                                                </h4>
                                                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                                    This permanently deletes all {stats?.total} rows. Freshers won't be
                                                    able to register until you upload a new list.
                                                </p>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end gap-2">
                                            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setConfirmClear(false)} disabled={clearing}>
                                                Cancel
                                            </Button>
                                            <Button
                                                onClick={clearAll}
                                                loading={clearing}
                                                disabled={clearing}
                                                className="bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700"
                                            >
                                                Clear all
                                            </Button>
                                        </div>
                                    </motion.div>
                                </div>
                            </>
                        )}
                    </AnimatePresence>
                </motion.div>
            </div>
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/AuditLogs.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    History,
    Filter,
    X,
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    User as UserIcon,
    Building2,
    Activity,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Select from '../../components/ui/Select'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import ExportButton from '../../components/ui/ExportButton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { formatDateTime, formatTimeAgo } from '../../lib/format'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

const ACTION_GROUPS = [
    {
        label: 'Authentication',
        actions: ['USER_REGISTERED', 'USER_LOGIN', 'USER_LOGIN_FAILED'],
    },
    {
        label: 'Tickets',
        actions: [
            'TICKET_JOINED',
            'TICKET_WAITLISTED',
            'TICKET_WAITLIST_PROMOTED',
            'TICKET_CALLED',
            'TICKET_SERVED',
            'TICKET_COMPLETED',
            'TICKET_NO_SHOW',
            'TICKET_TRANSFERRED',
            'TICKET_CANCELLED',
            'TICKET_PRIORITY_CHANGED',
        ],
    },
    {
        label: 'Queues',
        actions: ['QUEUE_CREATED', 'QUEUE_UPDATED', 'QUEUE_DELETED'],
    },
    {
        label: 'Staff',
        actions: ['STAFF_CREATED', 'STAFF_ASSIGNED', 'STAFF_UNASSIGNED'],
    },
    {
        label: 'Universities',
        actions: [
            'UNIVERSITY_CREATED',
            'UNIVERSITY_UPDATED',
            'UNIVERSITY_DEACTIVATED',
            'UNIVERSITY_REACTIVATED',
            'ADMIN_CREATED',
        ],
    },
    { label: 'Feedback', actions: ['FEEDBACK_SUBMITTED'] },
    { label: 'Documents', actions: ['DOCUMENT_UPLOADED', 'DOCUMENT_DELETED'] },
    {
        label: 'Appointments',
        actions: [
            'APPOINTMENT_SLOT_CREATED',
            'APPOINTMENT_SLOT_UPDATED',
            'APPOINTMENT_SLOT_DELETED',
            'APPOINTMENT_BOOKED',
            'APPOINTMENT_CANCELLED',
            'APPOINTMENT_COMPLETED',
            'APPOINTMENT_NO_SHOW',
        ],
    },
    {
        label: 'Workflows',
        actions: [
            'WORKFLOW_CREATED',
            'WORKFLOW_UPDATED',
            'WORKFLOW_DELETED',
            'WORKFLOW_STARTED',
            'WORKFLOW_STEP_COMPLETED',
            'WORKFLOW_COMPLETED',
            'WORKFLOW_ABANDONED',
        ],
    },
]

function actionVariant(action) {
    if (!action) return 'default'
    if (
        action.includes('FAILED') ||
        action.includes('DEACTIVATED') ||
        action.includes('DELETED')
    )
        return 'danger'
    if (
        action.includes('COMPLETED') ||
        action.includes('CREATED') ||
        action.includes('LOGIN')
    )
        return 'success'
    if (action.startsWith('TICKET') || action.startsWith('WORKFLOW')) return 'indigo'
    if (action.startsWith('UNIVERSITY') || action.startsWith('ADMIN')) return 'warning'
    return 'default'
}

export default function AuditLogs() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [logs, setLogs] = useState([])
    const [universities, setUniversities] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false)

    const [page, setPage] = useState(0)
    const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0)
    const [totalElements, setTotalElements] = useState(0)
    const PAGE_SIZE = 30

    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({
        universityId: '',
        action: '',
        from: '',
        to: '',
    })

    const loadUniversities = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/super-admin/universities')
            setUniversities(res.data.data || [])
        } catch {
            // silent
        }
    }, [])

    const loadLogs = useCallback(
        async (pageToLoad = 0) => {
            setLoading(true)
            try {
                const params = new URLSearchParams()
                params.append('page', String(pageToLoad))
                params.append('size', String(PAGE_SIZE))
                if (filters.universityId) params.append('universityId', filters.universityId)
                if (filters.action) params.append('action', filters.action)
                if (filters.from) params.append('from', new Date(filters.from).toISOString())
                if (filters.to) params.append('to', new Date(filters.to).toISOString())

                const res = await api.get(`/api/super-admin/audit-logs?${params.toString()}`)
                const data = res.data.data
                setLogs(data.content || [])
                setTotalPages(data.totalPages || 0)
                setTotalElements(data.totalElements || 0)
                setPage(pageToLoad)
            } catch (err) {
                push({
                    type: 'error',
                    message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load audit logs',
                })
            } finally {
                setLoading(false)
            }
        },
        [filters, push]
    )

    useEffect(() => {
        loadUniversities()
    }, [loadUniversities])

    useEffect(() => {
        loadLogs(0)
    }, [loadLogs])

    const clearFilters = () => {
        setFilters({ universityId: '', action: '', from: '', to: '' })
    }

    const setFilter = (field) => (e) => setFilters({ ...filters, [field]: e.target.value })

    const universityOptions = universities.map((u) => ({
        value: u.id,
        label: `${u.code} · ${u.name}`,
    }))

    const activeFilterCount = Object.values(filters).filter(Boolean).length
    const universityById = new Map(universities.map((u) => [u.id, u]))

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Audit Logs"
                description="Every meaningful action recorded across the platform."
                action={
                    <Button
                        variant={showFilters ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                        icon={Filter}
                        onClick={() => setShowFilters((v) => !v)}
                    >
                        Filters
                        {activeFilterCount > 0 && (
                            <span className="ml-1.5 rounded-full bg-brand-600 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white">
                {activeFilterCount}
              </span>
                        )}
                    </Button>
                }
            />

            {showFilters && (
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                    className="mb-6 overflow-hidden"
                >
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Filter logs</h3>
                            {activeFilterCount > 0 && (
                                <button
                                    onClick={clearFilters}
                                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700"
                                >
                                    <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                    Clear all
                                </button>
                            )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
                            <Select
                                label="University"
                                value={filters.universityId}
                                onChange={setFilter('universityId')}
                                options={universityOptions}
                                placeholder="All universities"
                            />

                            <div className="mb-4">
                                <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                    Action
                                </label>
                                <select
                                    value={filters.action}
                                    onChange={setFilter('action')}
                                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus-visible:border-brand-500 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-500/20"
                                >
                                    <option value="">All actions</option>
                                    {ACTION_GROUPS.map((g) => (
                                        <optgroup key={g.label} label={g.label}>
                                            {g.actions.map((a) => (
                                                <option key={a} value={a}>
                                                    {a.replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                                                </option>
                                            ))}
                                        </optgroup>
                                    ))}
                                </select>
                            </div>

                            <Input
                                label="From"
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={filters.from}
                                onChange={setFilter('from')}
                            />
                            <Input
                                label="To"
                                type="datetime-local"
                                value={filters.to}
                                onChange={setFilter('to')}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>
            )}

            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-500">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Activity className="h-4 w-4" />
                        <span>
              {loading ? (
                  'Loading…'
              ) : (
                  <>
                      <strong className="text-slate-900">{totalElements}</strong>{' '}
                      {totalElements === 1 ? 'entry' : 'entries'}
                      {activeFilterCount > 0 && ' (filtered)'}
                  </>
              )}
            </span>
                    </div>
                    <ExportButton
                        rows={logs}
                        columns={[
                            { key: 'id', label: 'ID' },
                            { key: 'action', label: 'Action' },
                            { key: 'entityType', label: 'Entity Type' },
                            { key: 'entityId', label: 'Entity ID' },
                            { key: 'userId', label: 'User ID' },
                            { key: 'universityId', label: 'University ID' },
                            { key: 'ipAddress', label: 'IP Address' },
                            { key: 'details', label: 'Details' },
                            { key: 'createdAt', label: 'Timestamp' },
                        ]}
                        filename="uniqueue-audit-logs"
                        label="Export page"
                    />
                </div>
                {totalPages > 1 && (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>
              Page {page + 1} of {totalPages}
            </span>
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>

            {loading ? (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
                        <div
                            key={i}
                            className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4"
                        >
                            <div className="h-10 w-10 animate-pulse rounded-lg bg-slate-100" />
                            <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                                <div className="h-3 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-100" />
                                <div className="h-3 w-1/2 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-100" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </Card>
            ) : logs.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={History}
                    title="No audit logs found"
                    description={
                        activeFilterCount > 0
                            ? 'Try adjusting or clearing your filters.'
                            : 'Once users perform actions, they will appear here.'
                    }
                    action={
                        activeFilterCount > 0 ? (
                            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={clearFilters}>
                                Clear filters
                            </Button>
                        ) : undefined
                    }
                />
            ) : (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <motion.ul
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.02)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                    >
                        {logs.map((log) => {
                            const uni = log.universityId ? universityById.get(log.universityId) : null
                            return (
                                <motion.li
                                    key={log.id}
                                    variants={staggerItem}
                                    className="flex flex-col gap-2 px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:px-6"
                                >
                                    <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start gap-3">
                                        <div className="flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-100">
                                            <Activity className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-500" />
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
