                                                <Badge variant={actionVariant(log.action)}>
                                                    {String(log.action).replace(/_/g, ' ')}
                                                </Badge>
                                                {log.entityType && (
                                                    <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                            {log.entityType}
                                                        {log.entityId ? ` #${log.entityId}` : ''}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                                {log.userId && (
                                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <UserIcon className="h-3 w-3" />
                            User #{log.userId}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                                {uni && (
                                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Building2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                        {uni.code}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                                {log.ipAddress && (
                                                    <span className="font-mono">{log.ipAddress}</span>
                                                )}
                                            </div>

                                            {log.details && (
                                                <div className="mt-1.5 truncate rounded bg-slate-50 px-2 py-1 font-mono text-xs text-slate-600">
                                                    {log.details}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="ml-12 shrink-0 text-xs text-slate-500 sm:ml-0 sm:text-right">
                                        <div className="font-medium text-slate-700">
                                            {formatTimeAgo(log.createdAt)}
                                        </div>
                                        <div>{formatDateTime(log.createdAt)}</div>
                                    </div>
                                </motion.li>
                            )
                        })}
                    </motion.ul>
                </Card>
            )}

            {totalPages > 1 && (
                <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        icon={ChevronLeft}
                        onClick={() => loadLogs(page - 1)}
                        disabled={page === 0 || loading}
                    >
                        Previous
                    </Button>
                    <span className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            Page {page + 1} of {totalPages}
          </span>
                    <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => loadLogs(page + 1)}
                        disabled={page >= totalPages - 1 || loading}
                    >
                        Next
                        <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Button>
                </div>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/Broadcast.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Send,
    Megaphone,
    Users,
    Building2,
    AlertCircle,
    CheckCircle2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Select from '../../components/ui/Select'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
    { value: '', label: 'All roles' },
    { value: 'ADMIN', label: 'Admins only' },
    { value: 'STAFF', label: 'Staff only' },
    { value: 'USER', label: 'Students only' },
]

export default function Broadcast() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [universities, setUniversities] = useState([])
    const [recipientCount, setRecipientCount] = useState(null)
    const [countLoading, setCountLoading] = useState(false)
    const [confirmOpen, setConfirmOpen] = useState(false)
    const [sending, setSending] = useState(false)

    const [form, setForm] = useState({
        title: '',
        message: '',
        universityId: '',
        role: '',
    })

    const loadUniversities = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/super-admin/universities')
            setUniversities(res.data.data || [])
        } catch {
            // silent
        }
    }, [])

    useEffect(() => {
        loadUniversities()
    }, [loadUniversities])

    // Preview recipient count when filters change
    useEffect(() => {
        const fetchCount = async () => {
            setCountLoading(true)
            try {
                const params = new URLSearchParams()
                params.append('page', '0')
                params.append('size', '1')
                if (form.universityId) params.append('universityId', form.universityId)
                if (form.role) params.append('role', form.role)
                params.append('isActive', 'true')

                const res = await api.get(`/api/super-admin/users?${params.toString()}`)
                setRecipientCount(res.data.data.totalElements)
            } catch {
                setRecipientCount(null)
            } finally {
                setCountLoading(false)
            }
        }
        const t = setTimeout(fetchCount, 300)
        return () => clearTimeout(t)
    }, [form.universityId, form.role])

    const setField = (f) => (e) => setForm({ ...form, [f]: e.target.value })

    const handleSendClick = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!form.title.trim() || !form.message.trim()) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Title and message are required' })
            return
        }
        setConfirmOpen(true)
    }

    const confirmSend = async () => {
        setSending(true)
        try {
            const payload = {
                title: form.title.trim(),
                message: form.message.trim(),
                universityId: form.universityId ? Number(form.universityId) : null,
                role: form.role || null,
            }
            const res = await api.post('/api/super-admin/broadcast', payload)
            push({ type: 'success', message: res.data.message })
            setConfirmOpen(false)
            setForm({ title: '', message: '', universityId: '', role: '' })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to send' })
        } finally {
            setSending(false)
        }
    }

    const universityOptions = universities.map((u) => ({
        value: u.id,
        label: `${u.code} · ${u.name}`,
    }))

    const audienceLabel = [
        form.universityId
            ? universities.find((u) => String(u.id) === String(form.universityId))?.name
            : 'All universities',
        form.role ? `(${form.role.toLowerCase()}s only)` : '(all roles)',
    ].join(' ')

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Broadcast"
                description="Send announcements to users across the platform. They'll appear in their notification bell."
            />

            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-3">
                {/* Compose form */}
                <div className="lg:col-span-2">
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                            <div className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand-50">
                                <Megaphone className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                            </div>
                            <div>
                                <h2 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Compose message</h2>
                                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                                    Only users with active accounts will receive it.
                                </p>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <form onSubmit={handleSendClick} className="space-y-4">
                            <div>
                                <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                    Title <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                                </label>
                                <input
                                    type="text"
                                    value={form.title}
                                    onChange={setField('title')}
                                    placeholder="e.g. Scheduled maintenance this weekend"
                                    maxLength={255}
                                    className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus-visible:border-brand-500 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-500/20"
                                    required
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div>
                                <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                    Message <span className="text-rose-500">*</span>
                                </label>
                                <textarea
                                    rows={6}
                                    value={form.message}
                                    onChange={setField('message')}
                                    placeholder="Write your announcement here. Keep it short and clear."
                                    maxLength={2000}
                                    className="w-full resize-none rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 focus-visible:border-brand-500 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-500/20"
                                    required
                                />
                                <div className="mt-1 flex justify-end">
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">
                    {form.message.length} / 2000
                  </span>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
                                <Select
                                    label="Target university"
                                    value={form.universityId}
                                    onChange={setField('universityId')}
                                    options={universityOptions}
                                    placeholder="All universities"
                                />
                                <Select
                                    label="Target role"
                                    value={form.role}
                                    onChange={setField('role')}
                                    options={ROLE_OPTIONS}
                                />
                            </div>

                            <div className="flex justify-end pt-2">
                                <Button
                                    type="submit"
                                    icon={Send}
                                    disabled={sending}
                                >
                                    Send broadcast
                                </Button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </Card>
                </div>

                {/* Preview panel */}
                <div className="space-y-4">
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Audience</h3>
                        </div>

                        <div className="space-y-3">
                            <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2.5">
                                <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Recipients
                                </div>
                                <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                                    {countLoading ? (
                                        <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">Calculating…</span>
                                    ) : (
                                        <>
                      <span className="text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {recipientCount ?? '—'}
                      </span>
                                            <span className="text-sm text-slate-500">users</span>
                                        </>
                                    )}
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2.5">
                                <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Scope
                                </div>
                                <div className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                    {audienceLabel}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </Card>

                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-600" />
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Good to know</h3>
                        </div>
                        <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs text-slate-600">
                            <li className="flex gap-2">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
                                <span>Broadcasts appear in the recipient's notification bell instantly.</span>
                            </li>
                            <li className="flex gap-2">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
                                <span>Recipients can dismiss them like any other notification.</span>
                            </li>
                            <li className="flex gap-2">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
                                <span>Disabled accounts are skipped automatically.</span>
                            </li>
                            <li className="flex gap-2">
                                <CheckCircle2 className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0 text-emerald-500" />
                                <span>The action is recorded in Audit Logs.</span>
                            </li>
                        </ul>
                    </Card>
                </div>
            </div>

            <ConfirmDialog
                open={confirmOpen}
                onClose={() => !sending && setConfirmOpen(false)}
                onConfirm={confirmSend}
                title="Send this broadcast?"
                description={`"${form.title}" will be sent to ${recipientCount ?? '?'} users. This cannot be undone.`}
                confirmLabel="Send broadcast"
                variant="info"
                loading={sending}
            />
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/SuperAdminDashboard.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { Routes, Route, Navigate, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Building2,
    Users,
    BarChart3,
    Plus,
    Power,
    PowerOff,
    History,
    UserPlus,
    X,
    Layers,
    Megaphone,
    Eye,
    DollarSign,
    Pencil,
    Trash2,
    AlertTriangle,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api/axios'
import Card from '../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../components/ui/Input'
import Badge from '../components/ui/Badge'
import StatCard from '../components/ui/StatCard'
import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import ImageUrlInput from '../components/ui/ImageUrlInput'
import DashboardLayout from '../components/layout/DashboardLayout'
import { SkeletonStats } from '../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'
import { useAuthStore } from '../store/authStore'
import { useUniversityStore } from '../store/universityStore'
import { startImpersonation } from '../components/layout/ImpersonationBanner'
import { stagger, staggerItem, expand } from '../lib/motion'
import AuditLogs from './super-admin/AuditLogs'
import SystemAnalytics from './super-admin/SystemAnalytics'
import UsersPage from './super-admin/Users'
import TiersAndFeatures from './super-admin/TiersAndFeatures'
import Broadcast from './super-admin/Broadcast'
import Plans from './super-admin/Plans'
import AdmittedStudentsButton from './super-admin/AdmittedStudentsButton'

const emptyForm = {
    name: '',
    code: '',
    emailDomain: '',
    adminFullName: '',
    adminEmail: '',
    adminPassword: '',
    matricPattern: '',
    matricExample: '',
    matricDescription: '',
    logoUrl: null,
    campusImageUrl: null,
    tagline: '',
}

const emptyAdminForm = {
    fullName: '',
    email: '',
    password: '',
    department: '',
    phone: '',
}

const tabs = [
    { to: '/super-admin', label: 'Universities', icon: Building2, end: true },
    { to: '/super-admin/plans', label: 'Plans', icon: DollarSign },
    { to: '/super-admin/tiers', label: 'Tiers & Features', icon: Layers },
    { to: '/super-admin/broadcast', label: 'Broadcast', icon: Megaphone },
    { to: '/super-admin/analytics', label: 'Analytics', icon: BarChart3 },
    { to: '/super-admin/users', label: 'All Users', icon: Users },
    { to: '/super-admin/audit-logs', label: 'Audit Logs', icon: History },
]

function UniversitiesTab() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const { user, token=REDACTED
    const { refresh: refreshUniversity } = useUniversityStore()
    const navigate = useNavigate()

    const [universities, setUniversities] = useState([])
    const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

    // Create
    const [showCreate, setShowCreate] = useState(false)
    const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
    const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)

    // Edit
    const [editTarget, setEditTarget] = useState(null)
    const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false)
    const [editForm, setEditForm] = useState(emptyForm)

    // Delete
    const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState(null)
    const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false)
    const [deleteConfirmCode, setDeleteConfirmCode] = useState('')

    // Other
    const [toggling, setToggling] = useState(null)
    const [confirmDeactivate, setConfirmDeactivate] = useState(null)
    const [addAdminTarget, setAddAdminTarget] = useState(null)
    const [adminForm, setAdminForm] = useState(emptyAdminForm)
    const [addingAdmin, setAddingAdmin] = useState(false)
    const [impersonating, setImpersonating] = useState(null)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const [u, a] = await Promise.all([
                api.get('/api/super-admin/universities'),
                api.get('/api/super-admin/analytics').catch(() => ({ data: { data: null } })),
            ])
            setUniversities(u.data.data || [])
            setAnalytics(a.data.data || null)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    // ═══════ CREATE ═══════
    const createUniversity = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setCreating(true)
        try {
            await api.post('/api/super-admin/universities', form)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'University registered' })
            setForm(emptyForm)
            setShowCreate(false)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to register university' })
        } finally {
            setCreating(false)
        }
    }

    // ═══════ EDIT ═══════
    const openEdit = (u) => {
        setEditTarget(u)
        setEditForm({
            name: u.name || '',
            code: u.code || '',
            emailDomain: u.emailDomain || '',
            adminFullName: '',
            adminEmail: '',
            adminPassword: '',
            matricPattern: u.matricPattern || '',
            matricExample: u.matricExample || '',
            matricDescription: u.matricDescription || '',
            logoUrl: u.logoUrl || null,
            campusImageUrl: u.campusImageUrl || null,
            tagline: u.tagline || '',
        })
    }

    const closeEdit = () => {
        setEditTarget(null)
        setEditForm(emptyForm)
    }

    const saveEdit = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!editTarget) return
        setEditing(true)
        try {
            await api.put(`/api/super-admin/universities/${editTarget.id}`, {
                name: editForm.name,
                emailDomain: editForm.emailDomain,
                matricPattern: editForm.matricPattern,
                matricExample: editForm.matricExample,
                matricDescription: editForm.matricDescription,
                logoUrl: editForm.logoUrl,
                campusImageUrl: editForm.campusImageUrl,
                tagline: editForm.tagline,
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'University updated' })
            closeEdit()
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to update' })
        } finally {
            setEditing(false)
        }
    }

    // ═══════ DELETE ═══════
    const performDelete = async () => {
        if (!deleteTarget) return
        if (deleteConfirmCode.trim().toUpperCase() !== deleteTarget.code.toUpperCase()) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: 'Code does not match' })
            return
        }
        setDeleting(true)
        try {
            await api.delete(`/api/super-admin/universities/${deleteTarget.id}`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: `${deleteTarget.name} deleted` })
            setDeleteTarget(null)
            setDeleteConfirmCode('')
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to delete' })
        } finally {
            setDeleting(false)
        }
    }

    // ═══════ TOGGLE ═══════
    const handleToggle = (u) => {
        if (u.isActive) setConfirmDeactivate(u)
        else performToggle(u)
    }

    const performToggle = async (u) => {
        setToggling(u.id)
        try {
            if (u.isActive) {
                await api.post(`/api/super-admin/universities/${u.id}/deactivate`)
                push({ type: 'success', message: `${u.name} deactivated` })
            } else {
                await api.post(`/api/super-admin/universities/${u.id}/reactivate`)
                push({ type: 'success', message: `${u.name} reactivated` })
            }
            setConfirmDeactivate(null)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed' })
        } finally {
            setToggling(null)
        }
    }

    const submitNewAdmin = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        if (!addAdminTarget) return
        setAddingAdmin(true)
        try {
            await api.post(
                `/api/super-admin/universities/${addAdminTarget.id}/admins`,
                adminForm
            )
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Admin added' })
            setAddAdminTarget(null)
            setAdminForm(emptyAdminForm)
            await load()
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to add admin' })
        } finally {
            setAddingAdmin(false)
        }
    }

    const handleImpersonate = async (university) => {
        setImpersonating(university.id)
        try {
            const usersRes = await api.get(
                `/api/super-admin/users?universityId=${university.id}&role=ADMIN&size=1&isActive=true`
            )
            const admins = usersRes.data.data?.content || []
            if (admins.length === 0) {
                push({ type: 'error', message: 'No active admin found for this university' })
                return
            }
            const admin = admins[0]
            const res = await api.post(`/api/super-admin/impersonate/${admin.id}`)
            const d = res.data.data

            startImpersonation(token, user, {
                userId: d.userId,
                email: d.email,
                fullName: d.fullName,
                role: d.role,
                universityId: d.universityId,
            })

            setAuth(d.accessToken, {
                userId: d.userId,
                email: d.email,
                fullName: d.fullName,
                role: d.role,
                universityId: d.universityId,
            })
            refreshUniversity()

            push({ type: 'success', message: `Now viewing as ${d.fullName}` })

            const dashboards = {
                SUPER_ADMIN: '/super-admin',
                ADMIN: '/admin',
                STAFF: '/staff',
                USER: '/student',
            }
            navigate(dashboards[d.role] || '/')
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Impersonation failed' })
        } finally {
            setImpersonating(null)
        }
    }

    if (loading) return <SkeletonStats count={4} />

    return (
        <>
            <div className="mb-6 flex justify-end">
                <Button
                    icon={Plus}
                    onClick={() => setShowCreate((v) => !v)}
                    variant={showCreate ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                >
                    {showCreate ? 'Cancel' : 'Register university'}
                </Button>
            </div>

            {analytics && (
                <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
                    <StatCard label="Universities" value={analytics.totalUniversities} icon={Building2} delay={0} />
                    <StatCard label="Total users" value={analytics.totalUsers} icon={Users} delay={0.05} />
                    <StatCard label="Active queues" value={analytics.activeQueues} icon={BarChart3} delay={0.1} />
                    <StatCard
                        label="Tickets today"
                        value={analytics.ticketsToday}
                        icon={BarChart3}
                        delay={0.15}
                        hint={`${analytics.ticketsAllTime ?? 0} all time`}
                    />
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Register form */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {showCreate && (
                    <motion.div
                        variants={expand}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        exit="exit"
                        className="mb-8 overflow-hidden"
                    >
                        <Card>
                            <h3 className="mb-4 text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                Register a new university
                            </h3>
                            <form onSubmit={createUniversity} className="space-y-6">
                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                    <Input label="University name" value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} placeholder="University of Lagos" required />
                                    <Input label="Code" value={form.code} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, code: e.target.value })} placeholder="UNILAG" required />
                                    <Input label="Email domain" value={form.emailDomain} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, emailDomain: e.target.value })} placeholder="@unilag.edu.ng" />
                                    <Input label="Tagline (optional)" value={form.tagline} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, tagline: e.target.value })} placeholder="Skip the line. Not the service." />
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <h4 className="mb-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Matric number format</h4>
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
                                        <Input label="Pattern (regex)" value={form.matricPattern} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, matricPattern: e.target.value })} placeholder="^\\d{2}/\\d{5}/\\d{3}$" />
                                        <Input label="Example" value={form.matricExample} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, matricExample: e.target.value })} placeholder="21/12345/001" />
                                        <Input label="Description" value={form.matricDescription} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, matricDescription: e.target.value })} placeholder="YY/XXXXX/XXX" />
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <h4 className="mb-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Branding</h4>
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                        <ImageUrlInput
                                            label="University logo URL"
                                            value={form.logoUrl}
                                            onChange={(url) => setForm({ ...form, logoUrl: url })}
                                            placeholder="https://unilag.edu.ng/logo.png"
                                            hint="Right-click the school's logo on their website → Copy image address."
                                            previewHeight="h-32"
                                        />
                                        <ImageUrlInput
                                            label="Campus photo URL"
                                            value={form.campusImageUrl}
                                            onChange={(url) => setForm({ ...form, campusImageUrl: url })}
                                            placeholder="https://unilag.edu.ng/campus.jpg"
                                            hint="Paste any public image URL."
                                            previewHeight="h-32"
                                        />
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div>
                                    <h4 className="mb-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">First administrator</h4>
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                        <Input label="Full name" value={form.adminFullName} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, adminFullName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Jane Doe" required />
                                        <Input label="Email" type="email" value={form.adminEmail} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, adminEmail: e.target.value })} placeholder="admin@unilag.edu.ng" required />
                                        <Input label="Password=REDACTED
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                                    <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => { setForm(emptyForm); setShowCreate(false) }} disabled={creating}>
                                        Cancel
                                    </Button>
                                    <Button type="submit" loading={creating} disabled={creating}>
                                        Register university
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </form>
                        </Card>
                    </motion.div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {/* Table */}
            <Card padded={false}>
                <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Registered universities</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table className="w-full">
                        <thead className="bg-slate-50">
                        <tr>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Code</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Name</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Plan</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Status</th>
                            <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Actions</th>
                        </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <motion.tbody
                            variants={stagger(0, 0.03)}
                            initial="initial"
                            animate="animate"
                            className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                        >
                            {universities.map((u) => (
                                <motion.tr key={u.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-3 font-mono text-sm text-slate-700">{u.code}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                            {u.logoUrl ? (
                                                <img
                                                    src={u.logoUrl}
                                                    alt={u.name}
                                                    className="h-8 w-8 shrink-0 rounded-md object-contain ring-1 ring-slate-200"
                                                    onError={(e) => { e.target.style.display = 'none' }}
                                                />
                                            ) : (
                                                <div className="flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-500">
                                                    {(u.code || '?').slice(0, 2)}
                                                </div>
                                            )}
                                            <span>{u.name}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm text-slate-600">{u.plan}</td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-3">
                                        <Badge variant={u.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                                            {u.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                                        </Badge>
                                    </td>
                                    <td className="px-6 py-3">
                                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => openEdit(u)}
                                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 transition-colors hover:text-slate-900"
                                            >
                                                <Pencil className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                Edit
                                            </button>

                                            <AdmittedStudentsButton university={u} />

                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => handleImpersonate(u)}
                                                disabled={impersonating === u.id}
                                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-sky-600 transition-colors hover:text-sky-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                                            >
                                                <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                {impersonating === u.id ? 'Loading…' : 'Login as admin'}
                                            </button>

                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => {
                                                    setAddAdminTarget(u)
                                                    setAdminForm(emptyAdminForm)
                                                }}
                                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700"
                                            >
                                                <UserPlus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                Add admin
                                            </button>

                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => handleToggle(u)}
                                                disabled={toggling === u.id}
                                                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 ${
                                                    u.isActive
                                                        ? 'text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700'
                                                        : 'text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700'
                                                }`}
                                            >
                                                {u.isActive ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        <PowerOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                        Deactivate
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <>
                                                        <Power className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                        Reactivate
                                                    </>
                                                )}
                                            </button>

                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => {
                                                    setDeleteTarget(u)
                                                    setDeleteConfirmCode('')
                                                }}
                                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-rose-600 transition-colors hover:text-rose-700"
                                            >
                                                <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                Delete
                                            </button>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </motion.tr>
                            ))}
                        </motion.tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {/* Deactivate confirm */}
            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!confirmDeactivate}
                onClose={() => !toggling && setConfirmDeactivate(null)}
                onConfirm={() => performToggle(confirmDeactivate)}
                title={`Deactivate ${confirmDeactivate?.name}?`}
                description="All users will be blocked from logging in. Data is preserved and you can reactivate at any time."
                confirmLabel="Deactivate"
                variant="danger"
                loading={toggling === confirmDeactivate?.id}
            />

            {/* EDIT MODAL */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {editTarget && (
                    <>
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            onClick={() => !editing && closeEdit()}
                            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm"
                        />
                        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-start justify-center overflow-y-auto p-4">
                            <motion.div
                                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 12 }}
                                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                                exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.96, y: 12 }}
                                transition={{ duration: 0.2 }}
                                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                                className="relative my-8 w-full max-w-2xl rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900"
                            >
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => !editing && closeEdit()}
                                    className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                                    aria-label="Close"
                                >
                                    <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </button>

                                <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                    Edit {editTarget.name}
                                </h3>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                    Update the university's details and branding.
                                </p>

                                <form onSubmit={saveEdit} className="mt-6 space-y-6">
                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                        <Input label="University name" value={editForm.name} onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, name: e.target.value })} required />
                                        <div>
                                            <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">Code</label>
                                            <input
                                                type="text"
                                                value={editForm.code}
                                                readOnly
                                                className="w-full cursor-not-allowed rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2 font-mono text-sm text-slate-500 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-400"
                                            />
                                            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Code is immutable.</p>
                                        </div>
                                        <Input label="Email domain" value={editForm.emailDomain} onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, emailDomain: e.target.value })} placeholder="@unilag.edu.ng" />
                                        <Input label="Tagline" value={editForm.tagline} onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, tagline: e.target.value })} placeholder="Skip the line. Not the service." />
                                    </div>

                                    <div>
                                        <h4 className="mb-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Matric number format</h4>
                                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
                                            <Input label="Pattern (regex)" value={editForm.matricPattern} onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, matricPattern: e.target.value })} />
                                            <Input label="Example" value={editForm.matricExample} onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, matricExample: e.target.value })} />
                                            <Input label="Description" value={editForm.matricDescription} onChange={(e) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, matricDescription: e.target.value })} />
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div>
                                        <h4 className="mb-3 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">Branding</h4>
                                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                                            <ImageUrlInput
                                                label="University logo URL"
                                                value={editForm.logoUrl}
                                                onChange={(url) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, logoUrl: url })}
                                                placeholder="https://unilag.edu.ng/logo.png"
                                                hint="Right-click the school's logo on their website → Copy image address."
                                                previewHeight="h-32"
                                            />
                                            <ImageUrlInput
                                                label="Campus photo URL"
                                                value={editForm.campusImageUrl}
                                                onChange={(url) => setEditForm({ ...editForm, campusImageUrl: url })}
                                                placeholder="https://unilag.edu.ng/campus.jpg"
                                                hint="Paste any public image URL."
                                                previewHeight="h-32"
                                            />
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 dark:border-slate-800">
                                        <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={closeEdit} disabled={editing}>
                                            Cancel
                                        </Button>
                                        <Button type="submit" loading={editing} disabled={editing}>
                                            Save changes
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>
                                </form>
                            </motion.div>
                        </div>
                    </>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {/* DELETE MODAL */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {deleteTarget && (
                    <>
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            onClick={() => !deleting && setDeleteTarget(null)}
                            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/50 backdrop-blur-sm"
                        />
                        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                            <motion.div
                                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 8 }}
                                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                                exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 8 }}
                                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                                className="relative w-full max-w-md rounded-xl border border-rose-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl dark:border-rose-900 dark:bg-slate-900"
                            >
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => !deleting && setDeleteTarget(null)}
                                    className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
                                    aria-label="Close"
                                >
                                    <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </button>

                                <div className="mb-5 flex items-start gap-4">
                                    <div className="flex h-12 w-12 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-rose-100 dark:bg-rose-950/40">
                                        <AlertTriangle className="h-6 w-6 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400" />
                                    </div>
                                    <div>
                                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                                            Delete {deleteTarget.name}?
                                        </h3>
                                        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                                            This permanently removes the university and <strong>all</strong> its data — users, queues, tickets, feedback, and notifications. This cannot be undone.
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800">
                                    <label className="mb-2 block text-xs font-medium text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                                        Type <span className="rounded bg-slate-200 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 dark:bg-slate-700 dark:text-slate-100">{deleteTarget.code}</span> to confirm
                                    </label>
                                    <input
                                        type="text"
                                        value={deleteConfirmCode}
                                        onChange={(e) => setDeleteConfirmCode(e.target.value)}
                                        placeholder={deleteTarget.code}
                                        autoFocus
                                        className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 font-mono text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:border-rose-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-rose-500/20 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100"
                                    />
                                </div>

                                <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end gap-2">
                                    <Button
                                        type="button"
                                        variant="secondary"
                                        onClick={() => setDeleteTarget(null)}
                                        disabled={deleting}
                                    >
                                        Cancel
                                    </Button>
                                    <Button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={performDelete}
                                        loading={deleting}
                                        disabled={deleting || deleteConfirmCode.trim().toUpperCase() !== deleteTarget.code.toUpperCase()}
                                        className="bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 focus:ring-rose-500"
                                    >
                                        Delete permanently
                                    </Button>
                                </div>
                            </motion.div>
                        </div>
                    </>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {/* ADD ADMIN MODAL */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {addAdminTarget && (
                    <>
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            onClick={() => !addingAdmin && setAddAdminTarget(null)}
                            className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-sm"
                        />
                        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                            <motion.div
                                initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 8 }}
                                animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                                exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 8 }}
                                onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                                className="relative w-full max-w-md max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
                            >
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => !addingAdmin && setAddAdminTarget(null)}
                                    className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-400 transition-colors hover:bg-slate-100"
                                    aria-label="Close"
                                >
                                    <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                                </button>

                                <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                    Add admin to {addAdminTarget.name}
                                </h3>
                                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                                    They'll get full admin access for this university.
                                </p>

                                <form onSubmit={submitNewAdmin} className="mt-5 space-y-1">
                                    <Input label="Full name" value={adminForm.fullName} onChange={(e) => setAdminForm({ ...adminForm, fullName: e.target.value })} placeholder="Jane Doe" required />
                                    <Input label="Email" type="email" value={adminForm.email} onChange={(e) => setAdminForm({ ...adminForm, email: e.target.value })} placeholder="admin2@unilag.edu.ng" required />
                                    <Input label="Password=REDACTED
                                    <Input label="Department (optional)" value={adminForm.department} onChange={(e) => setAdminForm({ ...adminForm, department: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Registry" />

                                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
                                        <Button type="button" variant="secondary" onClick={() => setAddAdminTarget(null)} disabled={addingAdmin}>
                                            Cancel
                                        </Button>
                                        <Button type="submit" loading={addingAdmin} disabled={addingAdmin}>
                                            Add admin
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>
                                </form>
                            </motion.div>
                        </div>
                    </>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>
        </>
    )
}

===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/Plans.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import { Save, DollarSign, Layers, Users, Ticket, Info } from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
    FREE: 'slate',
    BASIC: 'sky',
    PREMIUM: 'violet',
    ENTERPRISE: 'amber',
}

export default function Plans() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [plans, setPlans] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState({})

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/super-admin/plans')
            setPlans(res.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load plans' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const updateLocal = (id, field, value) => {
        setPlans((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === id ? { ...p, [field]: value } : p)))
    }

    const save = async (plan) => {
        setSaving((s) => ({ ...s, [plan.id]: true }))
        try {
            const res = await api.put(`/api/super-admin/plans/${plan.id}`, {
                label: plan.label,
                priceUsd: Number(plan.priceUsd),
                maxQueues: Number(plan.maxQueues),
                maxStaff: Number(plan.maxStaff),
                maxTicketsPerDay: Number(plan.maxTicketsPerDay),
                isActive: plan.isActive,
            })
            setPlans((prev) => prev.map((p) => (p.id === plan.id ? res.data.data : p)))
            push({ type: 'success', message: `${plan.label} plan updated` })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to save plan' })
            await load()
        } finally {
            setSaving((s) => ({ ...s, [plan.id]: false }))
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Plans & Pricing"
                description="Set prices and limits for each subscription tier. Changes apply immediately to new assignments."
            />

            <div className="mb-6 flex items-start gap-3 rounded-xl border border-sky-200 bg-sky-50 p-4 text-sm text-sky-900">
                <Info className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-sky-600" />
                <div>
                    <p className="font-medium">How pricing works</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-sky-800">
                        Prices are for display only — billing integration comes later. When you change
                        a plan's limits, universities currently on that plan keep their existing
                        limits until you re-save their plan on the Tiers tab. Use the limits below as
                        the source of truth for new plan assignments.
                    </p>
                </div>
            </div>

            {plans.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={DollarSign}
                    title="No plans configured"
                    description="Restart the backend to seed the default plans."
                />
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2"
                >
                    {plans.map((plan) => {
                        const color = DEFAULT_COLORS[plan.code] || 'slate'
                        const planSaving = saving[plan.id]

                        return (
                            <motion.div key={plan.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                                <Card>
                                    <div className="mb-4 flex items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                                        <div>
                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                    {plan.label}
                                                </h3>
                                                <Badge variant={color}>{plan.code}</Badge>
                                                {!plan.isActive && <Badge variant="default">Inactive</Badge>}
                                            </div>
                                            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                                Currently at <strong>${plan.priceUsd}</strong>/month
                                            </p>
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="text-right">
                                            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">
                                                ${plan.priceUsd}
                                            </div>
                                            <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                                per month
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    {/* Price */}
                                    <div className="mb-4">
                                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
                                            Monthly price (USD)
                                        </label>
                                        <div className="relative">
                      <span className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 left-3 flex items-center text-sm text-slate-400">
                        $
                      </span>
                                            <input
                                                type="number"
                                                min="0"
                                                step="1"
                                                value={plan.priceUsd}
                                                onChange={(e) => updateLocal(plan.id, 'priceUsd', e.target.value)}
                                                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white py-2 pl-7 pr-3 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20"
                                            />
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    {/* Limits */}
                                    <div className="space-y-3">
                                        <div>
                                            <label className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                                                <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                                Max queues
                                            </label>
                                            <input
                                                type="number"
                                                min="1"
                                                value={plan.maxQueues}
                                                onChange={(e) => updateLocal(plan.id, 'maxQueues', e.target.value)}
                                                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20"
                                            />
                                        </div>

                                        <div>
                                            <label className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                                                <Users className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                                Max staff members
                                            </label>
                                            <input
                                                type="number"
                                                min="1"
                                                value={plan.maxStaff}
                                                onChange={(e) => updateLocal(plan.id, 'maxStaff', e.target.value)}
                                                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20"
                                            />
                                        </div>

                                        <div>
                                            <label className="mb-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                                                <Ticket className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                                                Max tickets per day
                                            </label>
                                            <input
                                                type="number"
                                                min="1"
                                                value={plan.maxTicketsPerDay}
                                                onChange={(e) => updateLocal(plan.id, 'maxTicketsPerDay', e.target.value)}
                                                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20"
                                            />
                                        </div>

                                        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1 text-sm text-slate-700">
                                            <input
                                                type="checkbox"
                                                checked={plan.isActive}
                                                onChange={(e) => updateLocal(plan.id, 'isActive', e.target.checked)}
                                                className="h-4 w-4 rounded border-slate-300 text-brand-600 focus:ring-brand-500"
                                            />
                                            Available for new assignments
                                        </label>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="mt-5 flex justify-end border-t border-slate-100 pt-4">
                                        <Button
                                            size="sm"
                                            icon={Save}
                                            onClick={() => save(plan)}
                                            loading={planSaving}
                                            disabled={planSaving}
                                        >
                                            {planSaving ? 'Saving…' : 'Save plan'}
                                        </Button>
                                    </div>
                                </Card>
                            </motion.div>
                        )
                    })}
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/SystemAnalytics.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Users,
    Ticket,
    CheckCircle2,
    Building2,
    TrendingUp,
    TrendingDown,
    Clock,
    Trophy,
    ArrowUpRight,
    ArrowDownRight,
} from 'lucide-react'
import {
    ResponsiveContainer,
    AreaChart,
    Area,
    LineChart,
    Line,
    XAxis,
    YAxis,
    CartesianGrid,
    Tooltip,
    Legend,
} from 'recharts'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import ExportButton from '../../components/ui/ExportButton'
import { SkeletonStats, SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

function percentChange(current, previous) {
    if (previous === 0) {
        return current > 0 ? 100 : 0
    }
    return Math.round(((current - previous) / previous) * 1000) / 10
}

function KpiCard({ label, value, delta, icon: Icon, accent }) {
    const isPositive = delta >= 0
    return (
        <motion.div
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
            className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5"
        >
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div className="min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium text-slate-600">
                        <span>{label}</span>
                        <svg
                            className="h-3 w-3 text-slate-400"
                            viewBox="0 0 12 12"
                            fill="currentColor"
                        >
                            <path d="M3 5l3 3 3-3z" />
                        </svg>
                    </div>
                    <div className="mt-2 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-slate-900">
                        {value}
                    </div>
                    <div
                        className={`mt-1.5 inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium ${
                            isPositive ? 'text-emerald-600' : 'text-rose-600'
                        }`}
                    >
                        {isPositive ? (
                            <ArrowUpRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                        ) : (
                            <ArrowDownRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                        )}
                        {isPositive ? '+' : ''}
                        {delta}%
                        <span className="text-slate-400">vs last week</span>
                    </div>
                </div>
                {Icon && (
                    <div className={`rounded-lg p-2 ${accent}`}>
                        <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                    </div>
                )}
            </div>
        </motion.div>
    )
}

function ChartTooltip({ active, payload, label }) {
    if (!active || !payload?.length) return null
    return (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white p-3 text-xs shadow-lg">
            <div className="mb-1.5 font-medium text-slate-900">
                {new Date(label).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                    weekday: 'short',
                    day: '2-digit',
                    month: 'short',
                })}
            </div>
            {payload.map((entry) => (
                <div key={entry.name} className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <span
              className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full"
              style={{ backgroundColor: entry.color }}
          />
                    <span className="text-slate-500">{entry.name}:</span>
                    <span className="font-medium text-slate-900">{entry.value}</span>
                </div>
            ))}
        </div>
    )
}

export default function SystemAnalytics() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [analytics, setAnalytics] = useState(null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/super-admin/analytics')
            setAnalytics(res.data.data)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-6">
                <SkeletonStats count={4} />
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    if (!analytics) {
        return (
            <EmptyState
                icon={TrendingUp}
                title="Analytics unavailable"
                description="Couldn't load system analytics. Try again later."
            />
        )
    }

    const ticketsDelta = percentChange(analytics.ticketsThisWeek, analytics.ticketsLastWeek)
    const completedDelta = percentChange(analytics.completedThisWeek, analytics.completedLastWeek)
    const newUsersDelta = percentChange(analytics.newUsersThisWeek, analytics.newUsersLastWeek)

    const trendData = (analytics.dailyTrend || []).map((d) => ({
        date: d.date,
        Joined: d.joined,
        Completed: d.completed,
        'New users': d.newUsers,
    }))

    const sortedUniversities = [...(analytics.universities || [])].sort(
        (a, b) => (b.ticketsAllTime || 0) - (a.ticketsAllTime || 0)
    )
    const topUniversities = sortedUniversities.slice(0, 5)

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="System Analytics"
                description="Platform-wide metrics across every university."
                action={
                    <ExportButton
                        rows={analytics.universities || []}
                        columns={[
                            { key: 'code', label: 'Code' },
                            { key: 'name', label: 'Name' },
                            { key: 'users', label: 'Users' },
                            { key: 'staff', label: 'Staff' },
                            { key: 'queues', label: 'Queues' },
                            { key: 'ticketsToday', label: 'Tickets Today' },
                            { key: 'ticketsAllTime', label: 'Tickets All-Time' },
                        ]}
                        filename="uniqueue-system-analytics"
                        label="Export universities"
                    />
                }
            />

            {/* ─── KPI ROW ─── */}
            <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
                <KpiCard
                    label="Tickets (7d)"
                    value={analytics.ticketsThisWeek?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={ticketsDelta}
                    icon={Ticket}
                    accent="bg-brand-50 text-brand-600"
                />
                <KpiCard
                    label="Completed (7d)"
                    value={analytics.completedThisWeek?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={completedDelta}
                    icon={CheckCircle2}
                    accent="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600"
                />
                <KpiCard
                    label="New users (7d)"
                    value={analytics.newUsersThisWeek?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={newUsersDelta}
                    icon={Users}
                    accent="bg-sky-50 text-sky-600"
                />
                <KpiCard
                    label="Active universities"
                    value={analytics.activeUniversities?.toLocaleString() || '0'}
                    delta={0}
                    icon={Building2}
                    accent="bg-violet-50 text-violet-600"
                />
            </div>

            {/* ─── BIG TREND CHART ─── */}
            <Card className="mb-6">
