                <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                        <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            Platform activity
                        </h3>
                        <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                            Tickets joined, completed, and new users — last 14 days
                        </p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-brand-600" />
              Joined
            </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500" />
              Completed
            </span>
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
              <span className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-sky-500" />
              New users
            </span>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div className="h-72 w-full">
                    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                        <AreaChart
                            data={trendData}
                            margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
                        >
                            <defs>
                                <linearGradient id="colorJoined" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                                    <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#4f46e5" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                                    <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#4f46e5" stopOpacity={0} />
                                </linearGradient>
                                <linearGradient id="colorCompleted" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                                    <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#10b981" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                                    <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#10b981" stopOpacity={0} />
                                </linearGradient>
                                <linearGradient id="colorNewUsers" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                                    <stop offset="5%" stopColor="#0ea5e9" stopOpacity={0.25} />
                                    <stop offset="95%" stopColor="#0ea5e9" stopOpacity={0} />
                                </linearGradient>
                            </defs>
                            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" vertical={false} />
                            <XAxis
                                dataKey="date"
                                tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }}
                                tickLine={false}
                                axisLine={{ stroke: '#e2e8f0' }}
                                tickFormatter={(d) =>
                                    new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                                        day: '2-digit',
                                        month: 'short',
                                    })
                                }
                            />
                            <YAxis
                                tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }}
                                tickLine={false}
                                axisLine={false}
                                allowDecimals={false}
                            />
                            <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />
                            <Area
                                type="monotone"
                                dataKey="Joined"
                                stroke="#4f46e5"
                                strokeWidth={2}
                                fill="url(#colorJoined)"
                            />
                            <Area
                                type="monotone"
                                dataKey="Completed"
                                stroke="#10b981"
                                strokeWidth={2}
                                fill="url(#colorCompleted)"
                            />
                            <Area
                                type="monotone"
                                dataKey="New users"
                                stroke="#0ea5e9"
                                strokeWidth={2}
                                fill="url(#colorNewUsers)"
                            />
                        </AreaChart>
                    </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {/* ─── SECONDARY METRICS ─── */}
            <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                Avg wait time
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {analytics.avgWaitMinutes ?? 0}
                                <span className="ml-1 text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">min</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="rounded-lg bg-brand-50 p-2">
                            <Clock className="h-5 w-5 text-brand-600" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                Avg service time
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {analytics.avgServiceMinutes ?? 0}
                                <span className="ml-1 text-sm font-normal text-slate-500">min</span>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 p-2">
                            <TrendingUp className="h-5 w-5 text-emerald-600" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>

                <Card>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                        <div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                Currently waiting
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 text-2xl font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                {analytics.waitingNow ?? 0}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div className="rounded-lg bg-amber-50 p-2">
                            <Users className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-600" />
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            </div>

            {/* ─── USER GROWTH CHART ─── */}
            <Card className="mb-6">
                <div className="mb-4">
                    <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">New user signups</h3>
                    <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">Last 14 days</p>
                </div>
                <div className="h-56 w-full">
                    <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                        <LineChart
                            data={trendData}
                            margin={{ top: 10, right: 10, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}
                        >
                            <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e2e8f0" vertical={false} />
                            <XAxis
                                dataKey="date"
                                tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }}
                                tickLine={false}
                                axisLine={{ stroke: '#e2e8f0' }}
                                tickFormatter={(d) =>
                                    new Date(d).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
                                        day: '2-digit',
                                        month: 'short',
                                    })
                                }
                            />
                            <YAxis
                                tick={{ fontSize: 11, fill: '#64748b' }}
                                tickLine={false}
                                axisLine={false}
                                allowDecimals={false}
                            />
                            <Tooltip content={<ChartTooltip />} />
                            <Line
                                type="monotone"
                                dataKey="New users"
                                stroke="#0ea5e9"
                                strokeWidth={2.5}
                                dot={{ r: 3, fill: '#0ea5e9', strokeWidth: 0 }}
                                activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                            />
                        </LineChart>
                    </ResponsiveContainer>
                </div>
            </Card>

            {/* ─── LEADERBOARD ─── */}
            {topUniversities.length > 0 && (
                <Card className="mb-6">
                    <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Trophy className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500" />
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            Most active universities
                        </h3>
                    </div>

                    <motion.ul
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="space-y-2"
                    >
                        {topUniversities.map((u, idx) => (
                            <motion.li
                                key={u.universityId}
                                variants={staggerItem}
                                className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-4 py-3"
                            >
                                <div
                                    className={`flex h-8 w-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
                                        idx === 0
                                            ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700'
                                            : idx === 1
                                                ? 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'
                                                : idx === 2
                                                    ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700'
                                                    : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'
                                    }`}
                                >
                                    {idx + 1}
                                </div>
                                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      {u.name}
                    </span>
                                        <Badge variant="default">{u.code}</Badge>
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="mt-0.5 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                        <span>{u.users} users</span>
                                        <span>{u.queues} queues</span>
                                        <span>{u.ticketsAllTime} tickets all-time</span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div className="shrink-0 text-right">
                                    <div className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                        {u.ticketsToday}
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                        today
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </motion.li>
                        ))}
                    </motion.ul>
                </Card>
            )}

            {/* ─── FULL BREAKDOWN TABLE ─── */}
            {analytics.universities && analytics.universities.length > 0 && (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <div className="border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4">
                        <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            All universities — detailed breakdown
                        </h3>
                    </div>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                        <table className="w-full">
                            <thead className="bg-slate-50">
                            <tr>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Code
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Name
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Users
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Staff
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Queues
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Today
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    All-time
                                </th>
                                <th className="px-6 py-3 text-left text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">
                                    Status
                                </th>
                            </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <motion.tbody
                                variants={stagger(0, 0.03)}
                                initial="initial"
                                animate="animate"
                                className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                            >
                                {analytics.universities.map((u) => (
                                    <motion.tr key={u.universityId} variants={staggerItem}>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 font-mono text-sm text-slate-700">
                                            {u.code}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900">{u.name}</td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {u.users}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {u.staff}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {u.queues}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm font-medium text-slate-900">
                                            {u.ticketsToday}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3 text-right text-sm text-slate-600">
                                            {u.ticketsAllTime}
                                        </td>
                                        <td className="px-6 py-3">
                                            <Badge variant={u.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                                                {u.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                                            </Badge>
                                        </td>
                                    </motion.tr>
                                ))}
                            </motion.tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </Card>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/TiersAndFeatures.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Save,
    Sparkles,
    CheckCircle2,
    XCircle,
    Building2,
    DollarSign,
    Layers,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import { SkeletonCard } from '../../components/ui/Skeleton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

export const PLANS = {
    FREE: { label: 'Free', color: 'slate', maxQueues: 5, maxStaff: 10, maxTicketsPerDay: 200, price: 0 },
    BASIC: { label: 'Basic', color: 'sky', maxQueues: 15, maxStaff: 30, maxTicketsPerDay: 1000, price: 49 },
    PREMIUM: { label: 'Premium', color: 'violet', maxQueues: 50, maxStaff: 100, maxTicketsPerDay: 5000, price: 199 },
    ENTERPRISE: { label: 'Enterprise', color: 'amber', maxQueues: 500, maxStaff: 1000, maxTicketsPerDay: 50000, price: 799 },
}

const FEATURES = [
    { key: 'featureWaitlist', label: 'Waitlist', description: 'Queue overflow handling' },
    { key: 'featureAppointments', label: 'Appointments', description: '1-on-1 and group bookings' },
    { key: 'featureWorkflows', label: 'Workflows', description: 'Multi-step clearances' },
    { key: 'featureDocuments', label: 'Documents', description: 'File uploads (proofs, transcripts)' },
]

export default function TiersAndFeatures() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [universities, setUniversities] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [saving, setSaving] = useState({})

    const load = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/super-admin/universities')
            setUniversities(res.data.data || [])
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load' })
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }, [push])

    useEffect(() => {
        load()
    }, [load])

    const updateLocal = (id, field, value) => {
        setUniversities((prev) =>
            prev.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, [field]: value } : u))
        )
    }

    const savePlan = async (uni) => {
        setSaving((s) => ({ ...s, [uni.id]: { ...s[uni.id], plan: true } }))
        try {
            const res = await api.put(`/api/super-admin/universities/${uni.id}/plan`, {
                plan: uni.plan,
            })
            setUniversities((prev) =>
                prev.map((u) => (u.id === uni.id ? { ...u, ...res.data.data } : u))
            )
            push({ type: 'success', message: `${uni.name} → ${uni.plan}` })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to update plan' })
            await load()
        } finally {
            setSaving((s) => ({ ...s, [uni.id]: { ...s[uni.id], plan: false } }))
        }
    }

    const saveFeatures = async (uni) => {
        setSaving((s) => ({ ...s, [uni.id]: { ...s[uni.id], features: true } }))
        try {
            await api.put(`/api/super-admin/universities/${uni.id}/features`, {
                featureWaitlist: uni.featureWaitlist,
                featureAppointments: uni.featureAppointments,
                featureWorkflows: uni.featureWorkflows,
                featureDocuments: uni.featureDocuments,
            })
            push({ type: 'success', message: `${uni.name} features updated` })
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to update features' })
            await load()
        } finally {
            setSaving((s) => ({ ...s, [uni.id]: { ...s[uni.id], features: false } }))
        }
    }

    if (loading) {
        return (
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
                <SkeletonCard />
            </div>
        )
    }

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="Tiers & Features"
                description="Control billing plans and enable/disable platform features per university."
            />

            {/* Plan reference */}
            <Card className="mb-6">
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <DollarSign className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Available plans</h3>
                </div>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-4">
                    {Object.entries(PLANS).map(([key, plan]) => (
                        <div
                            key={key}
                            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50/50 p-3"
                        >
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">{plan.label}</span>
                                <Badge variant={plan.color}>{plan.price === 0 ? 'Free' : `$${plan.price}/mo`}</Badge>
                            </div>
                            <div className="mt-2 space-y-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                <div>{plan.maxQueues} queues</div>
                                <div>{plan.maxStaff} staff</div>
                                <div>{plan.maxTicketsPerDay.toLocaleString()} tickets/day</div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </div>
            </Card>

            {/* Universities */}
            {universities.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={Building2}
                    title="No universities yet"
                    description="Register a university first to manage tiers and features."
                />
            ) : (
                <motion.div
                    variants={stagger(0, 0.05)}
                    initial="initial"
                    animate="animate"
                    className="space-y-4"
                >
                    {universities.map((uni) => {
                        const planMeta = PLANS[uni.plan] || PLANS.FREE
                        const planSaving = saving[uni.id]?.plan
                        const featuresSaving = saving[uni.id]?.features

                        return (
                            <motion.div key={uni.id} variants={staggerItem}>
                                <Card>
                                    {/* Header row */}
                                    <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-4">
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                            <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-slate-900 text-sm font-bold text-white">
                                                {uni.code?.slice(0, 2)}
                                            </div>
                                            <div>
                                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                          <span className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            {uni.name}
                          </span>
                                                    <Badge variant={planMeta.color}>{planMeta.label}</Badge>
                                                    <Badge variant={uni.isActive ? 'success' : 'default'}>
                                                        {uni.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                                                    </Badge>
                                                </div>
                                                <div className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                                                    Code: {uni.code} · {uni.queueCount || 0} queues · {uni.staffCount || 0} staff
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
                                        {/* Plan section */}
                                        <div>
                                            <h4 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                <Layers className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                                Billing plan
                                            </h4>

                                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                <select
                                                    value={uni.plan || 'FREE'}
                                                    onChange={(e) => updateLocal(uni.id, 'plan', e.target.value)}
                                                    className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus-visible:border-brand-500 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-brand-500/20"
                                                >
                                                    {Object.entries(PLANS).map(([key, p]) => (
                                                        <option key={key} value={key}>
                                                            {p.label} {p.price > 0 && `— $${p.price}/mo`}
                                                        </option>
                                                    ))}
                                                </select>
                                                <Button
                                                    size="sm"
                                                    icon={Save}
                                                    onClick={() => savePlan(uni)}
                                                    loading={planSaving}
                                                    disabled={planSaving}
                                                >
                                                    Save
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-50 p-3 text-xs">
                                                <div>
                                                    <div className="text-slate-500">Max queues</div>
                                                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                        {uni.maxQueues}
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div>
                                                    <div className="text-slate-500">Max staff</div>
                                                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                        {uni.maxStaff}
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                                <div>
                                                    <div className="text-slate-500">Max tickets/day</div>
                                                    <div className="mt-0.5 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                        {(uni.maxTicketsPerDay || 0).toLocaleString()}
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        {/* Features section */}
                                        <div>
                                            <h4 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                                                <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-brand-600" />
                                                Feature access
                                            </h4>

                                            <div className="space-y-2">
                                                {FEATURES.map((f) => {
                                                    const enabled = uni[f.key] !== false
                                                    return (
                                                        <button
                                                            key={f.key}
                                                            type="button"
                                                            onClick={() => updateLocal(uni.id, f.key, !enabled)}
                                                            className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-left transition-colors ${
                                                                enabled
                                                                    ? 'border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50'
                                                                    : 'border-slate-200 bg-slate-50'
                                                            }`}
                                                        >
                                                            <div className="min-w-0">
                                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                                    {enabled ? (
                                                                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-emerald-600" />
                                                                    ) : (
                                                                        <XCircle className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-slate-400" />
                                                                    )}
                                                                    <span
                                                                        className={`text-sm font-medium ${
                                                                            enabled ? 'text-emerald-900' : 'text-slate-600'
                                                                        }`}
                                                                    >
                                    {f.label}
                                  </span>
                                                                </div>
                                                                <div
                                                                    className={`ml-6 text-xs ${
                                                                        enabled ? 'text-emerald-700' : 'text-slate-500'
                                                                    }`}
                                                                >
                                                                    {f.description}
                                                                </div>
                                                            </div>

                                                            {/* Toggle switch */}
                                                            <div
                                                                className={`relative h-6 w-11 shrink-0 rounded-full transition-colors ${
                                                                    enabled ? 'bg-emerald-600' : 'bg-slate-300'
                                                                }`}
                                                            >
                                                                <div
                                                                    className="absolute top-0.5 h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-transform"
                                                                    style={{
                                                                        transform: enabled ? 'translateX(20px)' : 'translateX(0)',
                                                                        left: '2px',
                                                                    }}
                                                                />
                                                            </div>
                                                        </button>
                                                    )
                                                })}
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="mt-3 flex justify-end">
                                                <Button
                                                    size="sm"
                                                    variant="secondary"
                                                    icon={Save}
                                                    onClick={() => saveFeatures(uni)}
                                                    loading={featuresSaving}
                                                    disabled={featuresSaving}
                                                >
                                                    Save features
                                                </Button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </Card>
                            </motion.div>
                        )
                    })}
                </motion.div>
            )}
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/super-admin/Users.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { motion } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Search,
    Filter,
    X,
    ChevronLeft,
    ChevronRight,
    User as UserIcon,
    Building2,
    Power,
    PowerOff,
    Mail,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../../api/axios'
import Card from '../../components/ui/Card'
import Button from '../../components/ui/Button'
import Input from '../../components/ui/Input'
import Select from '../../components/ui/Select'
import Badge from '../../components/ui/Badge'
import EmptyState from '../../components/ui/EmptyState'
import ConfirmDialog from '../../components/ui/ConfirmDialog'
import PageHeader from '../../components/layout/PageHeader'
import ExportButton from '../../components/ui/ExportButton'
import { useToastStore } from '../../store/toastStore'
import { formatDate } from '../../lib/format'
import { stagger, staggerItem } from '../../lib/motion'

const ROLE_OPTIONS = [
    { value: '', label: 'All roles' },
    { value: 'SUPER_ADMIN', label: 'Super Admin' },
    { value: 'ADMIN', label: 'Administrator' },
    { value: 'STAFF', label: 'Staff' },
    { value: 'USER', label: 'Student' },
]

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
    { value: '', label: 'All statuses' },
    { value: 'true', label: 'Active only' },
    { value: 'false', label: 'Disabled only' },
]

const roleVariant = {
    SUPER_ADMIN: 'indigo',
    ADMIN: 'warning',
    STAFF: 'info',
    USER: 'success',
}

export default function Users() {
    const { push } = useToastStore()
    const [users, setUsers] = useState([])
    const [universities, setUniversities] = useState([])
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
    const [showFilters, setShowFilters] = useState(false)

    const [page, setPage] = useState(0)
    const [totalPages, setTotalPages] = useState(0)
    const [totalElements, setTotalElements] = useState(0)
    const PAGE_SIZE = 25

    const [filters, setFilters] = useState({
        universityId: '',
        role: '',
        isActive: '',
        search: '',
    })

    const [toggleTarget, setToggleTarget] = useState(null)
    const [toggling, setToggling] = useState(false)

    const loadUniversities = useCallback(async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/super-admin/universities')
            setUniversities(res.data.data || [])
        } catch {
            // silent
        }
    }, [])

    const loadUsers = useCallback(
        async (pageToLoad = 0) => {
            setLoading(true)
            try {
                const params = new URLSearchParams()
                params.append('page', String(pageToLoad))
                params.append('size', String(PAGE_SIZE))
                if (filters.universityId) params.append('universityId', filters.universityId)
                if (filters.role) params.append('role', filters.role)
                if (filters.isActive) params.append('isActive', filters.isActive)
                if (filters.search) params.append('search', filters.search)

                const res = await api.get(`/api/super-admin/users?${params.toString()}`)
                const data = res.data.data
                setUsers(data.content || [])
                setTotalPages(data.totalPages || 0)
                setTotalElements(data.totalElements || 0)
                setPage(pageToLoad)
            } catch (err) {
                push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to load users' })
            } finally {
                setLoading(false)
            }
        },
        [filters, push]
    )

    useEffect(() => {
        loadUniversities()
    }, [loadUniversities])

    useEffect(() => {
        loadUsers(0)
    }, [loadUsers])

    const clearFilters = () => {
        setFilters({ universityId: '', role: '', isActive: '', search: '' })
    }

    const setFilter = (field) => (e) => setFilters({ ...filters, [field]: e.target.value })

    const handleToggle = async () => {
        if (!toggleTarget) return
        setToggling(true)
        try {
            const res = await api.post(`/api/super-admin/users/${toggleTarget.id}/toggle-active`)
            push({ type: 'success', message: res.data.message })
            setToggleTarget(null)
            await loadUsers(page)
        } catch (err) {
            push({ type: 'error', message: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed' })
        } finally {
            setToggling(false)
        }
    }

    const activeFilterCount = Object.values(filters).filter(Boolean).length
    const universityOptions = universities.map((u) => ({
        value: u.id,
        label: `${u.code} · ${u.name}`,
    }))
    const universityById = new Map(universities.map((u) => [u.id, u]))

    return (
        <>
            <PageHeader
                title="All Users"
                description="Every user across every university on the platform."
                action={
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <ExportButton
                            rows={users}
                            columns={[
                                { key: 'id', label: 'ID' },
                                { key: 'fullName', label: 'Name' },
                                { key: 'email', label: 'Email' },
                                { key: 'role', label: 'Role' },
                                { key: 'universityCode', label: 'University' },
                                { key: 'matricNumber', label: 'Matric' },
                                { key: 'department', label: 'Department' },
                                { key: 'isActive', label: 'Active' },
                                { key: 'createdAt', label: 'Joined' },
                            ]}
                            filename="uniqueue-users"
                            label="Export page"
                        />
                        <Button
                            variant={showFilters ? 'secondary' : 'primary'}
                            icon={Filter}
                            onClick={() => setShowFilters((v) => !v)}
                        >
                            Filters
                            {activeFilterCount > 0 && (
                                <span className="ml-1.5 rounded-full bg-brand-600 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-white">
                  {activeFilterCount}
                </span>
                            )}
                        </Button>
                    </div>
                }
            />

            {/* Search bar (always visible) */}
            <div className="mb-4">
                <Input
                    placeholder="Search by name, email, or matric number…"
                    value={filters.search}
                    onChange={setFilter('search')}
                    icon={Search}
                />
            </div>

            {/* Filters panel */}
            {showFilters && (
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, height: 0 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, height: 'auto' }}
                    className="mb-6 overflow-hidden"
                >
                    <Card>
                        <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
                            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Filters</h3>
                            {activeFilterCount > 0 && (
                                <button
                                    onClick={clearFilters}
                                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-rose-600 hover:text-rose-700"
                                >
                                    <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                    Clear all
                                </button>
                            )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
                            <Select
                                label="University"
                                value={filters.universityId}
                                onChange={setFilter('universityId')}
                                options={universityOptions}
                                placeholder="All universities"
                            />
                            <Select
                                label="Role"
                                value={filters.role}
                                onChange={setFilter('role')}
                                options={ROLE_OPTIONS}
                            />
                            <Select
                                label="Status"
                                value={filters.isActive}
                                onChange={setFilter('isActive')}
                                options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
                            />
                        </div>
                    </Card>
                </motion.div>
            )}

            {/* Summary */}
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-500">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <UserIcon className="h-4 w-4" />
                    <span>
            {loading ? (
                'Loading…'
            ) : (
                <>
                    <strong className="text-slate-900">{totalElements}</strong>{' '}
                    {totalElements === 1 ? 'user' : 'users'}
                    {activeFilterCount > 0 && ' (filtered)'}
                </>
            )}
          </span>
                </div>
                {totalPages > 1 && (
                    <span>
            Page {page + 1} of {totalPages}
          </span>
                )}
            </div>

            {/* Users list */}
            {loading ? (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    {Array.from({ length: 8 }).map((_, i) => (
                        <div
                            key={i}
                            className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 px-6 py-4"
                        >
                            <div className="h-10 w-10 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-slate-100" />
                            <div className="flex-1 space-y-2">
                                <div className="h-3 w-1/4 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-100" />
                                <div className="h-3 w-1/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-slate-100" />
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    ))}
                </Card>
            ) : users.length === 0 ? (
                <EmptyState
                    icon={UserIcon}
                    title="No users found"
                    description={
                        activeFilterCount > 0
                            ? 'Try adjusting or clearing your filters.'
                            : 'No users have registered yet.'
                    }
                    action={
                        activeFilterCount > 0 ? (
                            <Button variant="secondary" onClick={clearFilters}>
                                Clear filters
                            </Button>
                        ) : undefined
                    }
                />
            ) : (
                <Card padded={false}>
                    <motion.ul
                        variants={stagger(0, 0.02)}
                        initial="initial"
                        animate="animate"
                        className="divide-y divide-slate-100"
                    >
                        {users.map((u) => {
                            const uni = u.universityId ? universityById.get(u.universityId) : null
                            return (
                                <motion.li
                                    key={u.id}
                                    variants={staggerItem}
                                    className="flex flex-col gap-3 px-4 py-3 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:px-6"
                                >
                                    <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-start gap-3">
                                        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-slate-900 text-xs font-semibold text-white">
                                            {u.fullName?.split(' ').map((p) => p[0]).slice(0, 2).join('')}
                                        </div>
                                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">
                          {u.fullName}
                        </span>
                                                <Badge variant={roleVariant[u.role] || 'default'}>
                                                    {String(u.role).replace('_', ' ')}
                                                </Badge>
                                                {!u.isActive && (
                                                    <Badge variant="danger">Disabled</Badge>
                                                )}
                                            </div>

                                            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                          <Mail className="h-3 w-3" />
                            {u.email}
                        </span>
                                                {uni && (
                                                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Building2 className="h-3 w-3" />
                                                        {uni.code}
                          </span>
                                                )}
                                                {u.matricNumber && (
                                                    <span className="font-mono">{u.matricNumber}</span>
                                                )}
                                                {u.createdAt && (
                                                    <span>Joined {formatDate(u.createdAt)}</span>
                                                )}
                                            </div>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>

                                    <div className="shrink-0">
                                        {u.role !== 'SUPER_ADMIN' && (
                                            <button
                                                onClick={() => setToggleTarget(u)}
                                                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium transition-colors ${
                                                    u.isActive
                                                        ? 'text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50'
                                                        : 'text-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-50'
                                                }`}
                                            >
                                                {u.isActive ? (
                                                    <>
                                                        <PowerOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                        Disable
                                                    </>
                                                ) : (
                                                    <>
                                                        <Power className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                                        Enable
                                                    </>
                                                )}
                                            </button>
                                        )}
                                    </div>
                                </motion.li>
                            )
                        })}
                    </motion.ul>
                </Card>
            )}

            {/* Pagination */}
            {totalPages > 1 && (
                <div className="mt-6 flex items-center justify-between">
                    <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        icon={ChevronLeft}
                        onClick={() => loadUsers(page - 1)}
                        disabled={page === 0 || loading}
                    >
                        Previous
                    </Button>
                    <span className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            Page {page + 1} of {totalPages}
          </span>
                    <Button
                        variant="secondary"
                        size="sm"
                        onClick={() => loadUsers(page + 1)}
                        disabled={page >= totalPages - 1 || loading}
                    >
                        Next
                        <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Button>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Toggle confirmation */}
            <ConfirmDialog
                open={!!toggleTarget}
                onClose={() => !toggling && setToggleTarget(null)}
                onConfirm={handleToggle}
                title={
                    toggleTarget?.isActive
                        ? `Disable ${toggleTarget?.fullName}?`
                        : `Enable ${toggleTarget?.fullName}?`
                }
                description={
                    toggleTarget?.isActive
                        ? "They won't be able to log in or use the platform until re-enabled."
                        : 'They will regain full access to their account.'
                }
                confirmLabel={toggleTarget?.isActive ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}
                variant={toggleTarget?.isActive ? 'danger' : 'info'}
                loading={toggling}
            />
        </>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/pages/VerifyEmail.jsx =====
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import { motion, AnimatePresence } from 'framer-motion'
import {
    Mail,
    ShieldCheck,
    Loader2,
    CheckCircle2,
    AlertCircle,
    ArrowRight,
    RefreshCw,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api/axios'
import Button from '../components/ui/Button'
import { useToastStore } from '../store/toastStore'

export default function VerifyEmail() {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    const location = useLocation()
    const { push } = useToastStore()

    const passedEmail = location.state?.email || ''
    const passedCode = location.state?.devCode || ''

    const [email, setEmail] = useState(passedEmail)
    const [code, setCode] = useState('')
    const [devCode, setDevCode] = useState(passedCode || null)
    const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
    const [resending, setResending] = useState(false)
    const [error, setError] = useState(null)
    const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false)
    const [resendIn, setResendIn] = useState(0)

    const codeInputRef = useRef(null)

    // Resend cooldown timer
    useEffect(() => {
        if (resendIn <= 0) return
        const t = setTimeout(() => setResendIn((s) => s - 1), 1000)
        return () => clearTimeout(t)
    }, [resendIn])

    // If no email passed, send the user back to login
    useEffect(() => {
        if (!passedEmail && !location.state) {
            // Don't force redirect — user might type it manually.
            // But show a hint below.
        }
    }, [passedEmail, location.state])

    const verify = async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault()
        setError(null)

        if (!email.trim()) {
            setError('Enter the email you registered with')
            return
        }
        if (!code.trim() || code.length < 6) {
            setError('Enter the 6-digit code')
            return
        }

        setLoading(true)
        try {
            const res = await api.post('/api/auth/verify-email', {
                email: email.trim(),
                code: code.trim(),
            })
            const data = res.data?.data

            // Save the auth token from the response
            if (data?.accessToken) {
                localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_token', data.accessToken)
                const user = {
                    userId: data.userId,
                    email: data.email,
                    fullName: data.fullName,
                    role: data.role,
                    universityId: data.universityId,
                }
                localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_user', JSON.stringify(user))
            }

            setSuccess(true)
            push({ type: 'success', message: 'Email verified! Redirecting…' })

            // Auto-redirect to dashboard
            setTimeout(() => {
                const dashboards = {
                    SUPER_ADMIN: '/super-admin',
                    ADMIN: '/admin',
                    STAFF: '/staff',
                    USER: '/student',
                    FRESHER: '/student',
                }
                navigate(dashboards[data?.role] || '/login', { replace: true })
            }, 1200)
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Verification failed')
        } finally {
            setLoading(false)
        }
    }

    const resend = async () => {
        if (!email.trim()) {
            setError('Enter your email to resend the code')
            return
        }
        setResending(true)
        setError(null)
        try {
            const res = await api.post('/api/auth/resend-verification', {
                email: email.trim(),
            })
            const data = res.data?.data
            // In dev mode, backend returns the fresh code
            if (data?.devVerificationCode) {
                setDevCode(data.devVerificationCode)
            }
            setResendIn(30)
            push({
                type: 'success',
                message: 'New verification code generated',
            })
            codeInputRef.current?.focus()
        } catch (err) {
            setError(err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to resend code')
        } finally {
            setResending(false)
        }
    }

    // ── SUCCESS STATE ──
    if (success) {
        return (
            <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 px-4 dark:bg-slate-950">
                <motion.div
                    initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}
                    animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}
                    transition={{ type: 'spring', stiffness: 200, damping: 20 }}
                    className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 text-center shadow-lg dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
                >
                    <motion.div
                        initial={{ scale: 0.5, opacity: 0 }}
                        animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
                        transition={{ delay: 0.1, type: 'spring', stiffness: 200, damping: 15 }}
                        className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-100 dark:bg-emerald-950/40"
                    >
                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-8 w-8 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400" />
                    </motion.div>

                    <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Email verified!
                    </h1>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Redirecting you to your dashboard…
                    </p>

                    <div className="mt-5 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
                        <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 animate-spin" />
                        Just a moment
                    </div>
                </motion.div>
            </div>
        )
    }

    // ── FORM STATE ──
    return (
        <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-slate-50 px-4 py-12 dark:bg-slate-950">
            <motion.div
                initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 8 }}
                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                transition={{ duration: 0.4 }}
                className="w-full max-w-md"
            >
                {/* Header icon */}
                <div className="mb-6 flex justify-center">
                    <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-brand-600 shadow-lg shadow-brand-500/30">
                        <ShieldCheck className="h-7 w-7 text-white" />
                    </div>
                </div>

                {/* Title */}
                <div className="mb-8 text-center">
                    <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 dark:text-slate-100">
                        Verify your email
                    </h1>
                    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        Enter the 6-digit code we sent to your email to activate your account.
                    </p>
                </div>

                {/* Dev code banner */}
                <AnimatePresence>
                    {devCode && (
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -4 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
                            className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 dark:border-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/40"
                        >
                            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                                <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400" />
                                <div className="flex-1">
                                    <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300">
                                        Dev mode — code
                                    </div>
                                    <div className="mt-1 font-mono text-2xl font-bold tracking-[0.3em] text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-100">
                                        {devCode}
                                    </div>
                                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400">
                                        In production, this code arrives by email.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </motion.div>
                    )}
                </AnimatePresence>

                <form onSubmit={verify} className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
                    {/* Email */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                            Email address
                        </label>
                        <div className="relative">
                            <Mail className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3 top-1/2 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
                            <input
                                type="email"
                                value={email}
                                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                                placeholder="Enter your email"
                                required
                                disabled={loading}
                                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-2.5 pl-9 pr-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 transition-colors focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 disabled:opacity-60 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-500"
                            />
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Code */}
                    <div>
                        <label className="mb-1.5 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                            Verification code
                        </label>
                        <input
                            ref={codeInputRef}
                            type="text"
                            inputMode="numeric"
                            value={code}
                            onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6))}
                            placeholder="Enter 6-digit code"
                            required
                            disabled={loading}
                            autoFocus={!devCode}
                            className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white py-3 text-center font-mono text-2xl tracking-[0.5em] text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-300 transition-colors focus:border-brand-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-500/20 disabled:opacity-60 dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 dark:placeholder-slate-600"
                        />
                    </div>

                    {/* Error */}
                    {error && (
                        <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-rose-200 bg-rose-50 p-3 text-xs text-rose-700 dark:border-rose-900 dark:bg-rose-950/40 dark:text-rose-300">
                            <AlertCircle className="mt-0.5 h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
                            <span>{error}</span>
                        </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Verify button */}
                    <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={loading || code.length < 6}
                        className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-brand-600 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-white transition-colors hover:bg-brand-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
                    >
                        {loading ? (
                            <>
                                <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
                                Verifying…
                            </>
                        ) : (
                            <>
                                Verify email
                                <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
                            </>
                        )}
                    </button>

                    {/* Resend row */}
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-2 text-xs">
            <span className="text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Didn't receive the code?
            </span>
                        <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={resend}
                            disabled={resending || resendIn > 0 || loading}
                            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-medium text-brand-600 transition-colors hover:text-brand-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:text-slate-400"
                        >
                            <RefreshCw className={`h-3 w-3 ${resending ? 'animate-spin' : ''}`} />
                            {resendIn > 0
                                ? `Resend in ${resendIn}s`
                                : resending
                                    ? 'Sending…'
                                    : 'Resend code'}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>

                {/* Footer */}
                <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Already verified?{' '}
                    <Link
                        to="/login"
                        className="font-medium text-brand-600 hover:text-brand-700 dark:text-brand-400"
                    >
                        Sign in
                    </Link>
                </p>
            </motion.div>
        </div>
    )
}
===== ./frontend/src/store/authStore.js =====
import { create } from 'zustand'
import api from '../api/axios'

const savedUser = (() => {
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem('uniqueue_user')
        return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null
    } catch {
        return null
    }
})()

export const useAuthStore = create((set, get) => ({
    user: savedUser,
    token: localStorage.getItem('uniqueue_token'),
    loading: false,
    error: null,

    isAuthenticated: () => !!get().token && !!get().user,

    login: async (email, password=REDACTED
        set({ loading: true, error: null })
        try {
            const res = await api.post('/api/auth/login', { email, password })
            const d = res.data.data
            const user = {
                userId: d.userId,
                email: d.email,
                fullName: d.fullName,
                role: d.role,
                universityId: d.universityId,
            }
            localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_token', d.accessToken)
            localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_user', JSON.stringify(user))
            set({ token: d.accessToken, user, loading: false })
            return { success: true, role: d.role }
        } catch (err) {
            const message = err.response?.data?.message || 'Login failed'
            set({ loading: false, error: message })
            return { success: false, error: message }
        }
    },

    register: async (payload) => {
        set({ loading: true, error: null })
        try {
            const res = await api.post('/api/auth/register', payload)
            const d = res.data.data
            const user = {
                userId: d.userId,
                email: d.email,
                fullName: d.fullName,
                role: d.role,
                universityId: d.universityId,
            }
            localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_token', d.accessToken)
            localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_user', JSON.stringify(user))
            set({ token: d.accessToken, user, loading: false })
            return {
                success: true,
                role: d.role,
                devVerificationCode: d.devVerificationCode,
                isVerified: d.isVerified,
            }
        } catch (err) {
            const message = err.response?.data?.message || 'Registration failed'
            set({ loading: false, error: message })
            return { success: false, error: message }
        }
    },

    setAuth: (token=REDACTED
        localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_token', token)
        localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_user', JSON.stringify(user))
        set({ token, user })
    },

    /**
     * Update the current user object (partial merge) without touching the token.
     * Writes through to localStorage so the change survives reload.
     */
    setUser: (patch) => {
        const current = get().user || {}
        const next = { ...current, ...(patch || {}) }
        localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_user', JSON.stringify(next))
        set({ user: next })
    },

    /**
     * Refetch the user's profile from the backend and sync the store.
     * Use after profile updates so the Navbar / other pages reflect changes.
     */
    refreshProfile: async () => {
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/profile')
            const p = res.data?.data
            if (!p) return { success: false }

            const current = get().user || {}
            const next = {
                ...current,
                userId: p.id ?? current.userId,
                email: p.email ?? current.email,
                fullName: p.fullName ?? current.fullName,
                role: p.role ?? current.role,
                universityId: p.universityId ?? current.universityId,
            }

            localStorage.setItem('uniqueue_user', JSON.stringify(next))
            set({ user: next })
            return { success: true, profile: p }
        } catch (err) {
            return {
                success: false,
                error: err.response?.data?.message || 'Failed to refresh profile',
            }
        }
    },

    logout: () => {
        localStorage.removeItem('uniqueue_token')
        localStorage.removeItem('uniqueue_user')
        localStorage.removeItem('uniqueue_impersonation')
        set({ user: null, token: null })
    },

    clearError: () => set({ error: null }),
}))
===== ./frontend/src/store/universityStore.js =====
import { create } from 'zustand'
import api from '../api/axios'

export const useUniversityStore = create((set, get) => ({
    university: null, // { id, name, code, logoUrl, primaryColor }
    loading: false,
    loaded: false,

    load: async () => {
        if (get().loading) return
        set({ loading: true })
        try {
            const res = await api.get('/api/profile')
            const p = res.data.data
            set({
                university: p.universityId
                    ? {
                        id: p.universityId,
                        name: p.universityName,
                        code: p.universityCode,
                        logoUrl: p.universityLogoUrl,
                        primaryColor: p.universityPrimaryColor,
                    }
                    : null,
                loading: false,
                loaded: true,
            })
        } catch {
            set({ loading: false, loaded: true })
        }
    },

    refresh: () => set({ loaded: false }),
    reset: () => set({ university: null, loaded: false }),
}))
